Conclusion: 该研究说明，将大语言模型的结构生成能力与仿真优化的参数校准能力结合，可形成一种可迭代、可反馈、可复用的区块链数字孪生体自动构建范式。其主要贡献在于把传统高度依赖人工经验的建模流程转化为受约束的双层搜索与优化过程，并使构建结果能够支持后续决策任务。

Abstract: Building a blockchain digital twin largely requires translating domain knowledge and specific system descriptions into a simulator architecture, calibrating its parameters against behavioral evidence, and validating the constructed twin. These steps are commonly performed through application-specific modeling efforts that can be difficult to reuse across systems and downstream decision problems. We consider automating this process through Spec2Twin-Chain, a framework that formulates blockchain digital-twin construction as a bi-level optimization problem. At the upper level, a large language model proposes and revises structurally admissible architectures using system specifications, behavioral evidence, and feedback from evaluated designs. At the lower level, a simulation-based optimizer calibrates the architecture-conditioned parameters under explicit objectives and guardrail constraints. The two levels iterate. The evaluated candidates at lower levels are retained in a global archive and used to guide subsequent proposals at upper levels. We conduct controlled experiments involving twin calibration, feedback-driven recovery, stress analysis, downstream policy optimization, and policy updating. The results demonstrate that the framework can construct behaviorally accurate twins, improve initial designs through iterative feedback, and reuse calibrated twins to support downstream decisions.

</details>


### [127] [Mitigating Over-Optimization in PRM-Guided Search in Mathematical Reasoning by Optimizing the Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30051)
*Taejong Joo,Diego Klabjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“极大极小（maximin）PRM 引导搜索”，一种无需训练或在线适配的鲁棒过程监督方法，用于缓解过程奖励模型（PRM）噪声导致的搜索误导，并在多数实验设置中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM 能为多步推理提供逐步奖励，从而将测试时计算资源集中于更有前景的部分解。然而，若 PRM 的逐步评分存在误差，常规搜索会过度追逐偶然获得高分的错误前缀，并过早剪除实际可行的推理路径。随着推理深度增加，这种由极值效应放大的伪高分问题会更加严重。

Method: 作者从理论上指出，直接依据 PRM 分数进行搜索容易受到验证器噪声影响：在更多候选前缀中，不可行前缀获得异常高分的概率会上升。为此，论文将 PRM 引导搜索表述为针对合理奖励扰动集合的鲁棒优化问题，并采用极大极小准则选择在最不利奖励扰动下仍具竞争力的路径。该方法不需要重新训练 PRM，也不需要在线更新模型参数，其核心是降低搜索策略对异常高分和奖励噪声的敏感性，同时保留更多潜在有效的替代推理轨迹。

Result: 在无需微调或在线适配的条件下，极大极小搜索相较标准 PRM 引导搜索平均提升17%至35%。该方法在16个实验设置中的14个设置里优于结果级监督和步骤级监督基线，表明其在存在过程评分噪声时具有更稳定的搜索收益。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是识别并分析了 PRM 引导搜索中的极值噪声放大机制，并通过训练无关的极大极小鲁棒搜索缓解奖励过优化。实验结果显示，该方法能更可靠地利用不完美的过程奖励，减少错误高分路径主导搜索的问题，并提升多步推理搜索的整体表现。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) provide dense step-level guidance for search-based reasoning, enabling inference-time compute to be allocated toward promising partial solutions. However, recent evidence suggests that PRM-guided search can over-optimize imperfect process rewards, pruning viable trajectories while expanding spurious ones. In this work, we theoretically show that directly leveraging PRM score is vulnerable to verifier noise through an extreme-value effect: non-viable prefixes become more likely to receive spuriously high scores as reasoning depth increase. Therefore, we formulate the PRM-guided search as a robust optimization problem over plausible reward perturbations, termed maximin PRM-guided search, leading to a training-free robust process supervision method that preserves promising alternatives when step-level scores are noisy. Maximin PRM-guided search mitigates this failure mode by reducing sensitivity to over-optimized PRM outliers. Without fine-tuning or online adaptation, maximin search consistently improves the PRM-guided search by 17-35\% on average, outperforming outcome- and step-level baselines in 14 out of 16 settings. Our source code is available at https://github.com/tjoo512/maximin-search.

</details>


### [128] [Game-Agnostic Value Functions through Automatic JSON Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30056)
*Dien Nguyen,Diego Perez-Liebana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出JSON-Bag VF：一种基于JSON轨迹词元表示、可跨游戏复用的价值函数训练方法，并将其用于一步前瞻决策。实验表明，该方法在多数测试游戏中优于基线，而特征选择是提升性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏智能体的价值函数通常依赖特定游戏的手工特征，泛化性有限。该研究旨在利用JSON形式的游戏状态或轨迹描述，构建无需针对单一游戏专门设计表示的通用价值函数方法。

Method: 方法首先以JSON-Bag将游戏轨迹的JSON描述词元化，并据此构建原型特征；随后训练价值函数。论文进一步引入两类特征优化：基于随机森林的重要特征筛选，以及按游戏阶段选择不同特征。训练得到的价值函数与一步前瞻搜索结合，形成JSON-Bag OSLA智能体。作者在六种桌面游戏上，对不同原型词元化策略和特征选择组合进行比较。

Result: JSON-Bag OSLA在大多数测试游戏中超过了基线一步前瞻智能体。特征选择能够显著改善JSON-Bag VF的效果；相较于原型词元化方式，特征选择对最终性能的影响更大。

Conclusion: JSON-Bag VF证明了利用JSON词元表示训练通用游戏价值函数的可行性。对于该框架，优先采用有效的、阶段感知的特征选择策略，比单纯调整原型词元化方案更可能带来显著性能收益。

Abstract: JSON Bag-of-Tokens (JSON-Bag) is a recently proposed method to generically represent game trajectories by tokenizing their JSON descriptions. We introduce JSON-Bag VF, a game-agnostic approach to training value functions for game-playing agents using JSON-Bag prototypes. We show that this approach can be enhanced with Random Forest-based feature selection and a method to select game-stage-specific features. We evaluate JSON-Bag VF with One-step-look-ahead (JSON-Bag OSLA) on six tabletop games over different combinations of prototype-tokenization and feature selections. JSON-Bag OSLA outperforms baseline OSLA agents in most games. Our analysis also shows that feature selection significantly improves JSON-Bag VF and that feature selection is the most important factor in JSON-Bag VF performance, over prototype-tokenization.

</details>


### [129] [VERA: Authority-Preserving Edge Revocation for Federated AI-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30091)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VERA，一种面向多代理运行时的可验证边撤销机制，用于精确识别撤销某条授权边后真正失去权限的代理，避免树式级联的过度撤销和部署者范围级联的撤销不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代代理系统形成包含规划器、工具代理、远程服务和共享专家的动态委派图，而传统令牌或子树撤销机制无法正确处理共享代理及跨域授权，导致授权状态不一致。

Method: VERA将撤销规则定义为可由验证器检查的合约，并由运行时适配器生成带签名的证据。在析取式授权下，边e的撤销目标定义为T_intent(e,G)=reach(G)\reach(G\{e})，即所有授权根路径均依赖e的代理。该机制同时验证签名者身份、授权证据完整性及撤销目标证明，并在LangGraph、A2A、AutoGen和CrewAI工件上进行测试。

Result: LangGraph实验重复20次，生成500条框架轨迹和2000条有效签名委派决策；25个测试单元中有13个包含运行时多父共享关系，8个包含跨部署者关系。VERA通过500/500个目标证明，保留全部320个具有替代父节点的共享代理，而树式级联会错误撤销这些代理；同时能够拒绝未授权签名者和遗漏攻击。跨框架验证覆盖9条轨迹，其中5条可执行的签名委派事件通过了模式与签名检查。

Conclusion: VERA为动态代理委派图提供了比传统树式或部署者范围撤销更精确、可验证且可移植的权限撤销方案。实验表明，该机制能够正确处理共享代理、跨域后代和对抗性输入，但摘要中部分实验描述存在明显文本损坏，具体基线规模和个别结果仍需查阅正文确认。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks compose planners, tool agents, remote services, and shared specialists into runtime delegation graphs, but their revocation APIs still resemble token or subtree invalidation. When one delegation is withdrawn, the runtime must know which agents lose authority while independently authorized agents keep working. We study this authority consistency problem and introduce VERA (Verifiable Edge Revocation for Agents), a verifier-checkable revocation contract and API emitted by agent-runtime adapters as signed evidence. Under disjunctive authority, revoking edge e invalidates exactly T_intent(e,G) = reach(G) \ reach(G \ {e}), the agents whose every authorizing root path used e. Used as a contract, this target exposes two runtime failures: tree cascades over-revoke shared agents, while deployer-scoped cascades under-revoke cross-domain descendants. In a LangGraph framework-replt cells repeated 20 times yield 500compiled-framework traces and 2,000 valid signed delegation decisions; 13/25 cells contain runtime multi-parsharing and 8/25 contain cross-deployer shies 500/500 target proofs, preserves all320 alternate-parent shared-agent cases that tree cascade revokes, and rejects unauthorized signers and omission attacks. Baseline replay over 1,9that holder/node and tree-style targetscannot express this behavior. We further validate schema portability on A2A, AutoGen, and CrewAI artifacts: nine traces, including five executable Cregned delegation events that pass schema and signature checks.

</details>


### [130] [A.X K2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30181)
*Cheolseung Baek,Dhammiko Arya,Eunki Kim,Gun Song,Gyoungeun Han,Hyunho Yang,Hyunjun Eun,Jin Kim,Junyoung Park,Juyun Wee,Minki Hong,Minkyung Park,Minsang Kim,Minsoo Kang,SaeRom Kim,Sangjin Kim,Sangyeol Lee,Seojin Lee,Seokhwan Jo,Seokyoung Hong,Seongho Choi,Seonghye Cho,Seongmin Ok,Sereimony Sek,Seungmo Cho,Seungsik Kim,Singon Kim,Sohee Park,Sooyeon Park,Subin Yi,Sungbin Yoon,Sungeun Lee,Sung Jun Cheon,Sungwan Kim,Sunwoo Lee,Tae Yoon Kim,Wonbeom Jang,Yohan Ra,Yong-jin Han,Youngjin Kim,Youngrang Kim,Yujin Kang,Yujin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We introduce A.X K2, a 688B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language model trained from scratch as a high-performance foundation for \emph{agentic} applications. Trained on approximately 8.5T tokens---fewer than its predecessor, A.X K1---on a smaller but higher-quality mixture with substantially expanded agentic and software-engineering data, it nonetheless improves over A.X K1 across the board, by over 30 percentage points on some benchmarks, reflecting large gains in token efficiency. To support long contexts efficiently, we introduce Sparse Gated Attention (SGA), which combines sparse attention with gated attention, and adopt Gated Norm (GN) to stabilize large-scale training. SGA is trained natively at 128K through a \emph{sparse} indexer warmup that optimizes the indexer against its own sparse top-$k$ selection rather than the dense attention distribution, making adaptation markedly cheaper: each query reads only 2,048 positions, yet long-context quality is unchanged and A.X K2 scores 94.6 on RULER out to 256K. The outlier suppression of GN in turn keeps 4-bit NVFP4 serving within one point of FP8 accuracy. A simple yet effective Think-Fusion recipe further lets users switch between thinking and non-thinking modes within a single unified model. Extensive evaluations show that A.X K2 performs competitively against strong open-weight baselines, matching or exceeding them on math and Korean-language benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [FaVOR: LLM-Based Agentic Framework for Factor Mining via Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30192)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Minseok Kim,Seunghyeon Jung,Sujin Pyo,Huisu Jang,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FaVOR提出一种以经济假设证据为核心的智能体因子挖掘框架，通过分解、验证和整合三个阶段，确保因子数学形式与经济含义一致，从而提升可解释性、跨市场状态稳健性和经济有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体因子挖掘通常直接优化收益，缺乏对因子是否仍表达原始经济假设的检验，容易产生仅依赖偶然相关性的伪信号，并在市场状态变化时失效。

Method: FaVOR采用“可观测推理”机制构建三阶段一致性闭环：首先将宽泛的经济假设分解为相互独立的可观测条件；随后验证每个因子是否真实反映其对应条件；最后将通过验证的因子整合为结构保持可解释性的复合因子。

Result: 在2025年中国CSI 500和美国标普500数据上的实验表明，FaVOR优于现有基线方法，并在不同市场状态下保持有效。其生成的因子具有内在可解释性、较强的状态转换稳健性和更高的经济一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，围绕经济假设而非单纯收益目标进行因子发现，有助于减少伪相关、维持因子经济含义，并提高策略在不同市场状态下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Traditional finance relies on experts to hand-craft factors through a principled process grounded in economic rationale. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have automated this process, scaling factor mining far beyond manual effort. However, these automated approaches optimize directly for returns and rarely check whether a generated factor still expresses the economic hypothesis that motivated it. We identify this inconsistency between mathematical form and economic meaning as a structural failure mode of return-oriented automation. The resulting factors blur the line between real signals and spurious correlations and break down across regime shifts. We propose FaVOR (Factor Validation through Observable Reasoning), an agentic framework that restructures factor mining around hypothesis-level evidence rather than return outcomes. In place of the standard hypothesis-to-formula leap, FaVOR enforces a three-stage consistency loop tying mathematical form to economic rationale throughout. (1) Decomposition splits a broad economic hypothesis into independent observable conditions. (2) Validation checks whether each factor reflects its intended condition. (3) Integration merges them into a composite whose structure remains interpretable. On the CSI 500 and S&P 500 in 2025, FaVOR outperforms existing baselines while remaining effective across regimes. FaVOR shows that hypothesis-grounded factor discovery produces signals that are interpretable by construction, regime-robust, and economically faithful. The code is available at https://github.com/damilab/FaVOR.

</details>


### [132] [SPARK: Skeleton-Guided Reasoning Synthesis from Large-Scale Scientific Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30214)
*Yu Li,Wei Li,Xin Gao,Mengyuan Sun,Xiaoyang Wang,Qizhi Pei,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SPARK框架，从科研论文摘要中提取“主张—证据—推导”的推理骨架，并据此构建Spark-234K科学推理数据集。该数据集覆盖机制推理、假设证伪、定量推导和边界校准四类任务，在更少训练样本下取得优于既有科学推理数据集的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型的科学推理能力受限于高质量训练数据不足。现有数据集多偏向事实记忆或模板化计算题，较少训练对作用机制、证据支撑、推导链条及假设有效性的理解与评估。

Method: SPARK基于覆盖10个学科的大规模论文语料Sci-Base，不直接把论文转换为问答对，而是先蒸馏每篇论文的核心主张、支持证据及推导关系，形成紧凑且自包含的推理骨架；随后从四个视角生成任务：机制推理、假设证伪、定量推导和边界校准；最后通过一致性验证过滤缺乏依据或存在矛盾的样本。

Result: 框架构建了Spark-234K数据集。摘要声称，该数据集相较现有资源具有更高难度与更多样的推理形式；实验中，使用Spark-234K训练的模型在科学推理表现上持续优于使用既有数据集的模型，并表现出更高的数据效率。

Conclusion: SPARK将科研论文中的论证结构而非孤立事实作为数据合成基础，能够生成更贴近真实科研思维的训练样本。其核心贡献是以论文证据链驱动多类型科学推理任务构建，从而提升模型的科学推理能力和样本利用效率。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning remains challenging for open-source models, largely due to the lack of high-quality scientific reasoning data. Existing datasets are often dominated by factual recall or formulaic problem solving, with limited emphasis on mechanism understanding, evidence-grounded reasoning, and hypothesis evaluation. To address this, we introduce SPARK (Scientific Paper Abstracted Reasoning sKeleton), a paper-oriented synthesis framework built on Sci-Base, a large-scale corpus of research papers spanning 10 scientific disciplines. Instead of directly converting papers into question-answer pairs, SPARK treats the claim-evidence-derivation structure of a paper as the fundamental unit of reasoning synthesis. Specifically, SPARK (1) distills each paper into a compact reasoning skeleton capturing its central claims and supporting evidence, enabling self-contained question generation, and (2) synthesizes reasoning tasks from four scientific perspectives: mechanistic reasoning, hypothesis falsification, quantitative derivation, and boundary calibration. A final consistency verification stage further removes unsupported or contradictory outputs. Using this framework, we construct Spark-234K, a scientific reasoning dataset with substantially higher difficulty and diversity than existing resources. Experiments show that Spark-234K consistently outperforms existing scientific reasoning datasets while achieving stronger performance with significantly fewer training samples.

</details>


### [133] [LaMoC: Loss-Aware Modular Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30226)
*Mohanad Odema,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaMoC是一种面向大语言模型联合模块压缩的损失感知方法。它通过将激活统计与经验Fisher统计结合，并依据梯度—重构误差对齐关系调整融合比例，在减少参数的同时更好地保持语言建模与下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合模块压缩方法主要依赖激活统计量，难以显式反映不同模块压缩误差对最终损失的影响。论文旨在引入模块级损失敏感度信息，使压缩准则不仅考虑局部重构质量，还能更准确地服务于下游性能保持。

Method: 方法以经验Fisher信息作为模块级的损失感知代理，并与压缩所需的激活统计进行融合。作者将联合模块压缩表述为双层优化：内层最小化各模块的重构误差，外层调节激活信息与梯度信息的融合率，使局部重构误差与下游损失变化更一致。随后采用基于实证评估和统计验证的流程求解该压缩问题。

Result: 在覆盖4个模型家族、共8个模型的实验中，相对于先进的模块化压缩方法，LaMoC在4B至8B模型上实现了平均2.5%的困惑度降低，以及1%的相对任务准确率提升。结果表明，融合经验Fisher统计能够改善压缩后模型的性能保留。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将经验Fisher引入联合模块压缩，以补足仅依赖激活统计时缺失的损失敏感度刻画。实验显示，该损失感知机制可在相同压缩场景下获得更优的语言建模质量和下游任务表现，说明模块级梯度信息对压缩决策具有实际价值。

Abstract: Modular compression has enabled considerable parameter reduction in LLMs while preserving strong language understanding and downstream task accuracy. However, existing joint modular compression methods primarily rely on activation statistics, leaving loss-sensitivity information and its module-level characterization underexplored. We investigate addressing this gap with LaMoC, a loss-aware modular compression methodology that blends activation and Empirical Fisher statistics through gradient-error alignment. LaMoC improves joint compression by selecting compression statistics that better align local module reconstruction error with the downstream loss. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) We characterize the Empirical Fisher as a module-level loss-aware proxy that can be blended with the activation statistics required for compression. (2) We reformulate joint modular compression as a two-tiered optimization problem that minimizes module reconstruction error while tuning the activation and gradient information blending rate. (3) We implement an empirically driven methodology with statistical validation to solve the resulting compression problem. We evaluate LaMoC across four model families spanning eight models. On the 4-8B models, LaMoC achieves an average 2.5% reduction in perplexity and a 1% relative improvement in task accuracy over state-of-the-art modular compression methods.

</details>


### [134] [Rethinking the Test-Time Prompt Tuning Objective from the Perspective of Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30230)
*Jungwon Choi,Hyeonseo Jang,Kibok Lee,Eunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向测试时提示调优的校准感知目标函数，通过利用增强视图构造包含样本不确定性的软目标分布，在提升分类准确率的同时缓解传统熵最小化造成的过度自信与校准退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时提示调优通常对多增强视图采用熵最小化，使预测趋于尖锐。该机制未区分可靠样本与模糊样本的内在不确定性，容易对不确定样本产生过高置信度，因而损害概率校准质量。

Method: 方法以原始视图预测与由增强视图预测聚合得到的目标分布之间的交叉熵替代传统熵最小化。目标分布的熵被纳入对抗式优化，以保留增强视图间分歧所反映的样本级不确定性；同时，对每个增强视图按其置信度实施温度缩放：高置信预测被锐化，低置信预测被平滑，从而形成更可靠的监督目标。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的实验表明，该方法取得了领先的分类准确率，并显著改善模型校准表现。摘要未给出具体数据集、评价指标或数值增益，因此无法进一步判断其跨任务稳定性及计算开销。

Conclusion: 该工作将测试时提示调优从单纯压低预测熵转向不确定性约束下的目标分布对齐。其核心贡献是让模型仅在增强视图提供可靠一致证据时提高置信度，并在样本存在歧义或视图冲突时保留不确定性，兼顾性能与校准。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) has emerged as a powerful paradigm, refining prompts for each test sample via entropy minimization (EM) over multiple augmented views. However, we identify a limitation in the standard EM-based adaptation: it inherently drives the model toward overconfident predictions disregarding sample-specific uncertainty, leading to significant calibration degradation. To address these limitations, we propose a new objective that replaces the conventional EM loss by aligning the original-view prediction with a target distribution derived from augmented views via cross-entropy, while adversarially incorporating the entropy of the target distribution to capture sample-specific uncertainty. Furthermore, to better construct this target distribution, we apply confidence-aware temperature scaling to each augmented-view prediction according to its confidence, sharpening confident predictions while softening uncertain ones. This formulation allows the model to increase confidence only when the target distribution is reliable, while preserving uncertainty when it reflects ambiguous or conflicting augmented-view predictions. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that our approach not only achieves state-of-the-art accuracy but also significantly improves model calibration.

</details>


### [135] [CoLa-ICD: A Knowledge-Enhanced Framework for Long-Tail Automated Medical Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30234)
*Yihang Cheng,Veronica Liesaputra,Andrew Trotman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoLa-ICD是一个面向长尾标签预测的知识增强型自动医疗编码框架，通过扩充ICD标签语义、建模代码依赖关系并强化标签与临床证据的对齐，提升稀有代码识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动医疗编码面临临床文本过长、标签分布不均衡和术语多样等问题；稀有ICD代码训练样本有限，且容易与语义相近的代码混淆，因此长尾预测性能不足。

Method: CoLa-ICD引入外部术语丰富ICD标签表示，建模相关代码之间的依赖关系，并增强标签语义与临床文本证据之间的匹配，以改善长尾代码预测。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够显著提升长尾标签预测效果，且在标签空间更大、分布更稀疏的场景中增益更明显；在AUC、F1和P@k指标上达到当前最佳性能。

Conclusion: 知识增强、代码依赖建模和语义—证据对齐能够有效缓解自动医疗编码中的长尾问题，CoLa-ICD在多种评价指标和稀疏标签场景下具有较强竞争力。

Abstract: Automatic medical coding assigns ICD codes to clinical notes, but it remains challenging due to long documents, imbalanced label distributions, and diverse terms. These challenges are especially severe for rare codes, which have limited training instances and are easily confused with semantically similar labels. We introduce CoLa-ICD, a knowledge-enhanced framework for long-tail prediction. CoLa-ICD enriches ICD labels with external terms, models dependencies among related codes, and learns stronger alignment between label semantics and clinical evidence for long-tail prediction. Experiments show that CoLa-ICD improves long-tail prediction with larger gains in larger and sparser label spaces and achieves state-of-the-art performance in AUC, F1, and P@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/youwillbethebest/Cola-ICD.

</details>


### [136] [LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Completion Combining Discrete Structural Coding with Similar Entity Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30235)
*Jiaqi Wang,Dongying Lin,Yang Yang,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoSC是一种面向知识图谱补全的LLM框架：先利用离散化结构编码生成候选实体排序，再引入与查询实体结构相似的实体信息进行重排序，从而联合利用图结构与相似实体线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识图谱补全方法主要分为两类：将知识图谱结构编码为离散标记，或仅对受限候选实体集合进行细化。这两类思路通常独立研究，因而未能充分结合初始结构推理与相似结构实体提供的补充证据。

Method: CoSC首先将知识图谱的结构信息表示为离散代码，并由LLM据此产生初始候选实体排名；随后检索或利用在结构上与查询实体相似的实体信息，对初始排名进行细化。该设计形成“结构代码初排—相似实体增强重排”的两阶段流程。

Result: 在FB15k-237数据集上，CoSC在MRR和Hits@10指标上优于摘要所称的现有基线方法；在Hits@1上保持竞争力。摘要未给出具体数值、基线名称、统计显著性或计算成本，因此无法进一步量化提升幅度与效率代价。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，离散结构编码可用于构建LLM的初始实体排序，而结构相似实体信息可作为有效的后续校正信号。其核心贡献是将此前相对割裂的两种LLM知识图谱补全范式整合到统一的候选排序流程中。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion requires models to use both textual descriptions and relational structure. Existing LLM-based methods either encode KG structure as discrete tokens or refine a restricted set of candidate entities, and these two directions have largely been studied separately. We propose CoSC for LLM-based KGC, which combines discrete structural coding with similar entity information. Specifically, an LLM generates an initial candidate entity ranking from discrete structural codes, after which information from entities with structures similar to that of the query entity refines the ranking. Experiments on FB15k-237 show that CoSC outperforms existing baselines on MRR and Hits@10 while remaining competitive on Hits@1.

</details>


### [137] [Generating Workflow DAGs from Natural Language with Non-Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30250)
*Anand Iyer,Bhanu Khetharpal,Srinivas Upadhya,Ramkumar Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向企业联络中心路由规则的神经符号生成框架：将自然语言规则转换为可执行的工作流有向无环图（DAG）。核心做法是让语言模型只生成紧凑的中间表示和相关词表选择，再由确定性编译器构造复杂图结构，以降低提示词成本并提高结构化输出可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接要求语言模型一次性输出包含条件动作、并行分支、优先级回退链及布尔谓词的完整工作流DAG，容易随节点数量和依赖关系增加而出现属性配置及布尔分组错误。论文将该现象概括为“输出密度瓶颈”：模型能较准确选择节点，但难以在单次生成中稳定配置大量相互关联的细节。因此，研究目标是在不依赖高成本长推理模型的前提下，实现生产可用的工作流生成质量。

Method: 作者在包含635条合成规则的基准上诊断单体式生成的失败模式，并采用神经符号分解。前端使用学习式注册表选择机制，将生成范围限制在与当前规则相关的动作、属性和词表；语言模型生成紧凑中间表示，而非完整平台JSON图；后端以确定性编译器将中间表示展开为目标商业路由平台所需的JSON DAG。实验比较四种模型，并以LLM裁判有效性、条件精确匹配率、JSON合法率和每条规则的提示词长度作为主要指标。

Result: 完整系统在四个模型上达到约89%的LLM裁判有效性、约90%的条件精确匹配准确率，以及99%至100%的合法JSON率；相较单体提示方案，每条规则使用的提示词令牌数约减少一半。在GPT-5.3-chat上，该方法使裁判有效性提升24个百分点，并在统计意义上达到某推理模型开箱即用质量的等效水平。不过，系统与更优性能前沿之间仍存在约8个百分点差距。

Conclusion: 论文表明，复杂结构化生成任务的关键不应完全依赖语言模型端到端地完成组合式图构造。通过将确定性、组合爆炸明显的部分移交给编译器，并让模型聚焦于语义解析、候选词表选择和紧凑表示生成，可以以较低成本获得更稳定的可执行工作流。该结论对其他需生成严格JSON、图结构或规则配置的应用具有可迁移价值；但由于评测基于制造的合成数据，真实业务规则、多样化表达及平台演化条件下的泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of translating natural-language routing rules written by business administrators into executable workflow graphs for enterprise contact centers. Each target is a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of conditional actions with parallel branches, hit-first fallback chains, and per-branch Boolean predicates, encoded in the JSON dialect of a commercial routing platform. We show that neuro-symbolic decomposition enables lower-cost, non-reasoning large language models to generate complex workflow DAGs at production-relevant quality without expensive extended-reasoning models. Our central diagnostic is an emission-density bottleneck: on a 635-rule benchmark of manufactured synthetic data, models select the correct graph nodes with high accuracy but increasingly misconfigure attributes and Boolean grouping as the number of interdependent nodes emitted in one pass grows. We therefore move combinatorial graph construction from the model into a deterministic compiler driven by a compact intermediate representation, with a learned registry-selection front end that focuses generation on relevant vocabulary. Across four models, the full system reaches approximately 89% LLM-judge validity, approximately 90% exact-match condition accuracy, and 99-100% valid JSON while using roughly half the per-rule prompt tokens of a monolithic prompt. On GPT-5.3-chat, the method improves judge validity by 24 percentage points and achieves statistical equivalence to a reasoning model's out-of-the-box quality, although an approximately 8-point frontier gap remains. We also present a deployment path and transferable lessons for structured-generation applications.

</details>


### [138] [SimCRAFT: Distilling Remote Sensing Agents via Synthetic Trajectories and Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30277)
*Haoran Wang,Jing Yao,Xu Yang,Zeqing Wang,Yang Zhang,Pedram Ghamisi,Zhengchao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SimCRAFT：一个模型无关的轻量化遥感智能体框架，通过约束验证的数据合成与检索增强微调，将复杂遥感工作流编排能力蒸馏至70亿参数级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感观测数据的规模与异构性快速增长，人工处理流程难以扩展。现有遥感智能体高度依赖通用大模型，但其领域知识不足、部署资源开销高，因此需要可高效部署且具备专业工作流规划能力的小型开放权重模型。

Method: 方法包含两部分：其一，利用多智能体合成引擎生成工作流样本，并以模拟执行引擎检查模式正确性、工具间依赖关系及传感器与工具的兼容性，构建约束验证语料SimRS-14k；其二，提出上下文检索增强微调CRAFT，使模型依据检索到的标准操作流程对新问题进行类比式适配，并通过抗噪目标避免对示例流程进行机械复制。该框架可迁移至三种70亿参数级骨干模型。

Result: 实验表明，SimCRAFT-7B优于开放权重通用大模型，并可与先进闭源模型及专用遥感智能体竞争。其性能可在三个不同的70亿参数级骨干网络上复现，说明数据构造和微调策略具有一定的模型无关性与稳定性。

Conclusion: SimCRAFT为轻量化遥感智能提供了有竞争力的开放权重基线。约束验证合成数据缓解了高质量领域工作流数据不足的问题，CRAFT提升了多步骤遥感规划的检索利用与泛化能力，适合计算、存储或能耗受限场景下的自主部署。

Abstract: The unprecedented surge in Earth observation data volume and diversity has exposed a critical bottleneck for traditional manual workflows, catalyzing the emergence of Remote Sensing (RS) Agents. However, the practical deployment of these advanced agents is severely hindered by their heavy reliance on large-scale general-purpose LLMs, which lack deep domain expertise and impose prohibitive infrastructure demands. To resolve this, we propose SimCRAFT, a model-agnostic framework that distills sophisticated RS orchestration capabilities into a compact 7B-scale model. Addressing data scarcity, we first pair a multiagent synthesis engine with a Mock Execution Engine that checks schema correctness, inter-tool dependencies, and sensor/tool compatibility, producing SimRS-14k, a large-scale, constraint-validated workflow planning corpus. Second, we propose Contextual Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning (CRAFT) that finetunes the model to reason analogically by adapting retrieved Standard Operating Procedures to novel queries under a noise-robust objective, generalizing RAFT to multi-step RS workflow planning without mechanical copying. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SimCRAFT-7B significantly outperforms openweights LLMs and rivals advanced closedsource models and specialized RS agents, while reproducing across three 7B backbones. This work contributes a competitive open-weights baseline for lightweight RS intelligence, enabling efficient autonomous deployment under resource-constrained or resource-conserving conditions.

</details>


### [139] [Ignorance or Incompetence? Constructing Knowledge-Gated, Verifiable Tasks for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30322)
*Hanlin Tian,Minhao Li,Yu Mi,Sihan Zhu,Zhao Yang,Yuxiang Wang,Hongquan Zhu,Qiufei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“知识门控”任务构造协议：将任务指令与承载私有约定、参考表和工具算子的紧凑工件分离，并通过对照实验验证模型只有获得正确工件时才能完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多专业智能体任务依赖公开语料中不存在的组织约定或领域规则，但现有基准通常无法严格控制模型是否获得这些知识，因而难以区分模型的真实推理能力、记忆泄漏与外部知识依赖。

Method: 协议要求在提供工件与隐藏工件两种条件下使用字节完全一致的任务指令；记录构造阶段来源，实施泄漏审计，并设置可执行见证以验证任务确实依赖工件。对于结构化任务，采用确定性求解器和规则语料给出精确真值；对于无法由单一程序判定的输出，采用具名、准则级评分量表。作者在15个校准任务上测试，并以每题5次试验的经验性知识门控筛选保留任务。

Result: 在15个校准任务中，一种前沿智能体配置在获得工件时通过率为68.0%，隐藏工件时为0%。在一个任务中，即使提供看似合理但实际错误的工件，5次试验也均未通过。经过配置相关的五次试验筛选后，保留了7个满足知识门控条件的任务。

Conclusion: 实验支持该构造协议能够显式、可检验地建立任务对私有知识工件的依赖，并可降低因提示差异或知识泄漏造成的混淆。论文的证据仅验证了任务构造与筛选行为，尚未证明这些保留任务能够提升后训练效果；作者公开了部分任务集与配套工具。

Abstract: Professional agent tasks often depend on conventions that are absent from public corpora, yet benchmarks rarely control whether an agent has access to those conventions. We introduce a knowledge-gated task-construction protocol that separates a task instruction from a compact artefact containing private conventions, reference tables, and utility operators. Construction-time provenance, byte-identical task instructions across the provided- and withheld-artefact conditions, leak audits, and executable witnesses make dependence on the artefact explicit and testable. Across fifteen calibration tasks, one frontier agent configuration achieves a 68.0% pass rate with the artefact and 0% without it; on one task, a plausible but incorrect artefact also yields 0% across five trials. Deterministic solvers and rule corpora provide exact ground truth for structured tasks, while named criterion-level rubrics support outputs that cannot be checked by a single executable oracle. A configuration-relative calibration screen retains seven tasks satisfying our five-trial empirical knowledge-gating screen. These experiments validate the behavior of the construction protocol; they do not establish that the retained tasks improve post-training. We publicly release part of the task suite and supporting tooling at https://github.com/DatagridsAI/Knowledge-Gated-Task-Construction.

</details>


### [140] [Answer Probing-Guided Search for Diverse Solution Exploration of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30345)
*Yi Fang,Que Shen,Chengpeng Li,Boyi Deng,Wei Shi,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Fengli Xu,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出答案探测引导树搜索（APTS）：在推理中间路径上探测模型可能生成的最终答案，并利用该答案的隐藏状态衡量路径差异、利用其困惑度估计正确性，从而在保持质量的同时提升多解生成的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时常收敛至单一高置信解，难以覆盖不同但有效的解题路径。现有测试时树搜索通常依据回复文本的语义嵌入剪枝，但该表示容易受到措辞、语言风格和表层相似性的干扰，无法可靠识别底层推理路径是否真正不同。

Method: 作者首先定义“答案探测”：针对某条中间推理路径，提示模型预测沿该路径最终会得到的答案。随后比较探测答案的隐藏状态相似度，以识别和保留具有不同潜在结论或推理走向的分支；同时以探测答案的困惑度作为路径正确性或可信度的近似指标。APTS据此在树搜索中联合执行多样性控制与质量导向的分支选择、扩展和剪枝。

Result: 在三个推理任务、两种大语言模型上的实验显示，APTS能够稳定提高所生成解的多样性。论文的核心实证结论是：相较于基于回复级语义嵌入的度量，探测答案隐藏状态更能区分实质不同的推理路径；探测答案困惑度则可作为实用的正确性代理信号。

Conclusion: 该工作将多路径推理的判别对象从中间文本表述转向“路径可能导向的答案表征”，缓解了表层语言相似性造成的误剪枝问题。APTS为测试时多解搜索提供了兼顾探索性与质量控制的机制，且实验表明其具有一定的跨任务、跨模型稳健性；其效果仍依赖于模型能否从中间路径可靠地产生可辨识的答案探测信号。

Abstract: Generating multiple diverse and high-quality solutions is valuable for many applications, such as code-test generation and drug discovery. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to converge on a single high-confidence solution during inference, limiting exploration of alternative valid solution paths. Existing test-time methods promote diversity through tree-like search and prune semantically similar branches using response-level semantic embeddings. However, we find that such embeddings are easily confounded by linguistic and stylistic similarities, making it difficult to distinguish genuinely distinct solution paths. To address this, we introduce Answer Probing, which probes the potential answer an LLM would reach from an intermediate reasoning path. We demonstrate that the hidden states of probed answers more effectively differentiate distinct solution paths than semantic embeddings, and the perplexity of probed answers serves as a practical proxy for reasoning correctness. Based on these findings, we propose Answer Probing-Guided Tree Search (APTS), which guides the tree search by the probed answers' hidden state similarity and perplexity. Experiments on three reasoning tasks across two LLMs show that APTS consistently enhances solution diversity, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [141] [Co-Annotator: Expert-Distilled ViT and VLM for Visual and Documentation Guidance in Age-Related Macular Degeneration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30352)
*Ziheng "Leo" Li,Benjamin Freeman,Akshay Raman,Kavin Aravindhan Rajkumar,Xinxin Fang,Rishabh Srivastava,Steven Feiner,Kaveri A. Thakoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-Annotator将专家眼动与口述信息转化为视觉搜索和生物标志物记录两类辅助，在视网膜OCT临床任务中同时提升诊断效率与文档完整性，且不降低诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床AI主要优化预测性能，却较少支持临床医生的视觉搜索路径和信息记录流程，导致视觉查找开销与文档负担成为独立的工作瓶颈。

Method: 系统包含两个模块：一是基于视觉Transformer的凝视对齐模型，用于生成与专家注视位置对应的感兴趣区域（AOI）；二是受本体约束的视觉语言模型，用于预填可编辑的生物标志物摘要。研究分三阶段开展：采集专家凝视和口述数据训练模型（US1）；在眼科住院医师中进行受控评估（US2）；在两所学术机构开展联合部署评估（US3）。

Result: US1显示模型可显著提升诊断准确率和生物标志物生成能力。US2表明两种辅助方式均安全且具有独立收益：AOI辅助带来可持续的视觉感知效率提升，VLM辅助使生物标志物记录广度增加一倍以上，但单独使用时在辅助过程中未提升即时效率。US3中同时提供两种辅助后，单位时间正确诊断数提高40%，评论编辑时间减少67%，效率增益显著超过任一单独模态，且诊断准确率未受损。

Conclusion: 专家知识蒸馏形成的多模态临床辅助能够同时缓解视觉搜索和文档记录两类工作流瓶颈。协同使用凝视引导与结构化文本预填充，比单一辅助方式更能提升临床效率，并保持既有诊断准确性。

Abstract: Clinical AI often optimizes predictive performance without engaging how clinicians decide where to look and what to write. We present Co-Annotator, which distills expert gaze and dictation into two guidance components: a gaze-aligned Vision Transformer producing fixation-aligned areas of interest (AOIs), and an ontology-bounded vision-language model (VLM) that pre-fills editable biomarker summaries for retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT). We first collect expert gaze and dictations (US1) to train the models, significantly improving diagnostic accuracy and biomarker generation. We then deploy the system with ophthalmology residents: a controlled resident study (US2) confirmed each modality is safe and independently beneficial, with AOI guidance producing lasting perceptual efficiency gains through post-guidance carryover and VLM guidance more than doubling biomarker documentation breadth. In a combined deployment across two academic institutions (US3), providing both modalities simultaneously produced efficiency gains that substantially exceeded either modality alone: correct diagnoses per minute increased by 40% and comment editing time fell by 67%, without compromising diagnostic accuracy. Notably, neither modality improved efficiency during guidance in US2, which makes the in-guidance efficiency gain under combined guidance in US3 the more striking result. Expert-distilled multimodal guidance can remove two distinct clinical workflow bottlenecks at once (visual search overhead and documentation burden) without compromising the diagnostic accuracy clinicians already achieve.

</details>


### [142] [Will the User Ever Know? Covert Indirect Prompt Injection on Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30362)
*Yunseok Lee,Yunji Kim,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以“隐蔽成功率”（CSR）和“显性成功率”（OSR）重新刻画间接提示注入攻击：前者指攻击执行后最终回复未留下可察觉痕迹，后者指最终回复暴露了攻击行为。作者进一步提出诱导隐蔽攻击（ICoA），通过在攻击执行后引导智能体回到原始用户任务，提高隐蔽攻击比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统攻击成功率（ASR）仅判断注入是否促使智能体执行攻击指令，未考虑用户能否从最终回复中发现异常。该缺陷会混淆两类风险不同的成功案例：执行攻击但不暴露的隐蔽成功，以及在回复中报告攻击行为、因而可被用户察觉的显性成功。

Method: 作者将成功攻击轨迹按最终回复是否暴露注入行为划分为隐蔽与显性两类，并将ASR分解为CSR和OSR。通过分析轨迹，论文发现决定两者差异的关键在于攻击后的行为序列：隐蔽轨迹会在攻击动作后返回并完成用户任务，显性轨迹则常在攻击动作处结束。基于ReAct框架中最终回复倾向总结最近动作的机制，作者设计ICoA，使智能体执行注入动作后继续处理原始任务，从而减少攻击痕迹在最终回复中的显现。

Result: 在AgentDojo基准的四个目标模型上，ICoA取得最高CSR，相比最强基线提升3.79至12.01个百分点。结果表明，仅使用ASR可能低估对用户而言更难察觉的攻击风险，而攻击后的轨迹控制能够显著影响最终回复中的可见性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是从用户可见性角度补充传统ASR，提出CSR与OSR作为区分攻击隐蔽性的指标，并说明ReAct式轨迹结构会导致攻击动作是否成为最终回复重点。ICoA的实验结果显示，评估和防御间接提示注入时，应同时考察攻击是否发生及其是否在最终输出中暴露。

Abstract: As LLM agents take real-world actions through tools, indirect prompt injection (IPI) has emerged as a serious threat. The standard metric, Attack Success Rate (ASR), counts whether an injection succeeds but ignores what the user notices in the agent's final response. Looking at successful injection traces, we find two distinct outcomes: the agent executes the injection while returning an otherwise normal response, or reports the injected action in its final response, giving the user a chance to notice. We call these covert and overt successes. From the user's perspective, we decompose ASR into the Covert Success Rate (CSR), counting successes leaving no trace in the final response, and the Overt Success Rate (OSR), counting successes the user can detect. To understand what drives the gap, we analyze successful trajectories and find that the agent's behavior after the injection separates covert from overt: covert traces hand control back to the user task before ending, while overt traces end at the attack itself. This split follows from the ReAct format, where the final response summarizes the most recent action. Building on this observation, we propose ICoA (Induced Covert Attack), an IPI attack designed to induce covert outcomes by steering the agent back to the user task after executing the injection. Across four target models on AgentDojo, ICoA achieves the highest CSR, with gains of 3.79-12.01 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [143] [Augmenting Human Performance with an XR Agent Learning from Online Behavior and BCI Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30369)
*Ziheng Li,Xichen He,Haoyan Chen,Charlie Zou,Sheng Bai,Benjamin Yang,Mengyuan Wu,Jake Ledner,Yi-Jie Cheng,Akito Yamauchi,Dishita G Turakhia,Steven Feiner,Paul Sajda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We present OLIVE, a framework for adapting a foundation model to provide real-time assistance in temporally demanding, high-stakes, and dynamic tasks. We show that passive EEG, fused online with behavioral evidence, can meaningfully extend the number of targets users detect and engage beyond their unaided action bandwidth. OLIVE learns from both explicit behavioral signals (the targets the user shoots down in an XR first-person shooter game) and implicit physiological signals (fixation-locked EEG) to provide timely guidance, continuously adapting a frozen vision-language model's inference on which items are task-relevant by jointly estimating per-source reliability without manual labels or offline training. Through three user studies, including two live deployments of an assistive agent driven by OLIVE in XR, we show that OLIVE Pareto-dominates prior test-time adaptation frameworks, achieving the highest convergence rate at comparable convergence speed. Combining implicit physiological and explicit behavioral signals, the OLIVE agent produces the largest and most reliable within-session improvement to a user's ability to detect and engage targets, largely independent of the individual's skill. When the target switches silently, the agent that uses both behavioral and physiological signals reconverges significantly faster than the behavior-only agent (1.27 times faster on average, p = .008), restoring trustworthy guidance at the moment the task changes, precisely when reliable assistance matters most.

</details>


### [144] [Scaffolding Foundation Models into Physical-World Agents Pushes the Frontier of Long-Horizon Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30396)
*Zixing Lei,Gengze Zhou,Xiong-Hui Chen,Jiazhao Zhang,Yiyang Huang,Hang Yin,Haoqi Yuan,Qi Wu,Weixin Li,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NavMCP是一种无需重训练的智能体脚手架，通过将视觉语言模型（VLM）的任务级推理与导航基础模型（NFM）的闭环执行结合，用于长时程具身探索与问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM擅长补全信息、调整高层计划，但在反复导航落地时效率低且不稳定；NFM能够稳健执行语义导航目标，却通常局限于彼此独立的有限回合，缺乏跨回合的持续推理与记忆。论文旨在弥合这两类能力之间的缺口。

Method: 框架由VLM作为推理代理，决定需要搜集的证据、搜索位置及停止时机；NFM作为执行器，将每个语义子目标转化为闭环导航。二者通过三类通道协作：意图通道将证据需求转换为导航调用；观测通道将导航轨迹转化为带来源依据的证据；记忆通道跨调用累计已发现信息、负面证据和未解决目标，从而形成持续的任务级交互。

Result: 在HM-EQA、MT-HM3D和EXPRESS-Bench具身问答基准上，NavMCP取得最优结果。在使用相同代理与执行器骨干的对比中，其在HM-EQA上比回合式接口高14.9个百分点。在Unitree Go2实机实验中，成功率达到78.3%；随着任务时程增加，相对最强基线的优势由10个百分点扩大至45个百分点。

Conclusion: NavMCP表明，通过脚手架式协同而非重新训练，可有效整合VLM的长程证据驱动推理与NFM的可靠导航能力。跨回合记忆、轨迹证据归纳和语义目标执行是提升长时程具身任务表现的关键设计。

Abstract: Long-horizon physical-world agents must reason over distant goals while grounding decisions in reliable closed-loop behavior. Today's foundation models split these capabilities: vision-language models (VLMs) infer missing information and adapt high-level plans but remain brittle and inefficient at repeated navigation grounding, while navigation foundation models (NFMs) robustly execute semantic goals but operate as bounded episodes without persistent task-level reasoning. We introduce NavMCP, an agentic scaffolding framework that couples a VLM reasoning agent with an NFM executor for long-horizon exploration. The VLM decides what evidence to seek, where to search, and when to stop, while the NFM grounds each semantic sub-goal into closed-loop navigation. Three channels structure their collaboration: intent translates evidence needs into navigation calls, observation converts rollouts into source-grounded trajectory evidence, and memory accumulates findings, negative evidence, and unresolved goals across calls. This design turns isolated navigation rollouts into persistent embodied interaction without retraining either model. On Embodied Question Answering, NavMCP achieves state-of-the-art results on HM-EQA, MT-HM3D, and EXPRESS-Bench. Under matched agent and executor backbones, it outperforms an episodic interface by 14.9 percentage points on HM-EQA. On a Unitree Go2, NavMCP reaches 78.3% success, with its margin over the strongest baseline growing from 10 to 45 points as the task horizon increases. These results demonstrate the potential of scaffolding complementary foundation models into long-horizon physical-world agents.

</details>


### [145] [Dense Clinical Contrasts Enhance Medical Knowledge Updating in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30405)
*Yangmin Huang,Shu Quan,He Geng,Xin Ye,Qianyun Du,Zhiyang He,Jiaxue Hu,Xiaodong Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究医学知识更新中的监督数据格式效应，提出时间锚定的肿瘤分期基准SEER-Bench，并比较四种等训练预算的监督微调格式。结果表明，EMQ在外部迁移与旧知识保持方面最稳定，说明知识更新效果显著依赖监督结构，而不只取决于更新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学指南和数据版本持续变化，语言模型容易依赖过时但表面合理的临床知识。现有更新研究通常关注算法或数据量，较少系统检验将同一知识更新事件组织为不同题型后，是否会影响更新质量、泛化能力及遗忘程度。

Method: 作者基于最新版版本化SEER Research Data构建SEER-Bench，用于时间锚定的肿瘤分期评测；再将来自NCCN指南的相同医学更新事件转换为EMQ、MSQ、FITB和SAQ四类监督格式。在训练预算匹配的监督微调设置下，比较各格式在SEER-Bench与HealthBench Professional上的性能、跨任务迁移和知识保留，并通过表示变化与临床对比信号密度进行诊断分析。

Result: EMQ在各同预算监督微调变体中表现出最稳定的外部迁移与保留能力。采用EMQ更新的4B模型在SEER-Bench上达到64.8%的答案准确率和59.6%的推理依据准确率。诊断结果显示，EMQ能够提供更密集的临床差异化信号，同时使模型表示相对基座模型发生更小幅度的移动。

Conclusion: 医学模型的知识更新不应仅优化更新算法，还应将监督格式作为核心设计变量。相较其他题型，EMQ更有利于将新增临床知识以可区分、可迁移且较少破坏原有表示的方式注入模型；但摘要未给出各格式的具体差异、统计显著性及更广泛医学领域的验证，因此其普适性仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Medical knowledge changes continually, making large language models vulnerable to relying on outdated yet clinically plausible information. We study whether the format of supervision affects medical knowledge updating under a matched training-budget setting. We introduce SEER-Bench, a temporally anchored oncology-staging benchmark curated from the latest versioned SEER Research Data release, and render identical medical update events from NCCN oncology guidelines into four supervision formats: EMQ, MSQ, FITB, and SAQ. Across SEER-Bench and HealthBench Professional, EMQ gives the most stable external transfer and retention among same-budget SFT variants. With EMQ supervision, the updated 4B model produces competitive results on temporally anchored oncology staging, reaching 64.8% answer accuracy and 59.6% rationale accuracy on SEER-Bench. Diagnostic analyses suggest that EMQ exposes denser clinical contrast signals while preserving discriminative representations with smaller movement from the base model. These results show that medical knowledge updating depends not only on the update algorithm, but also on how knowledge is structured as supervision.

</details>


### [146] [DERELAB: Probing Defeasible Reasoning and Confirmation Bias in LLMs with a Generative Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30413)
*Jayanta Sadhu,Sayem Shahad,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DeReLab：一个基于参数化图结构的生成式评测框架，用于构造带有逐轮真值标注的可撤销推理对话，并揭示多种大语言模型在证据更新中存在类似确认偏误的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可撤销推理数据集大多是静态的，且对非单调推理类别覆盖有限，难以细粒度测量模型面对支持性或反驳性新证据时是否能恰当地修正既有结论。作者希望构建可控、可验证的测试环境，以分离并评估具体推理能力。

Method: DeReLab从参数化图结构生成多轮信念更新对话，覆盖默认推理与继承推理。系统在每一轮提供经过形式化验证的标准答案，使研究者能够控制证据与原结论的一致或冲突关系，并据此设计隔离特定推理要求的实验。作者使用该框架评估了九个开放和专有大语言模型在确认与否认性证据下的更新表现。

Result: 实验显示，几乎所有受测模型都表现出系统性倾向：更容易接受与既有结论一致的证据，而对不一致证据引发的结论更新存在阻抗。部分模型能够识别新证据会削弱原结论，但仍未能相应地修正最终判断，表明其证据识别与结论更新之间存在脱节。

Conclusion: DeReLab为可撤销、非单调推理的可控评测提供了通用测试平台。研究结果表明，当前大语言模型在动态信念修正上仍存在显著局限，尤其难以将反驳性证据稳定地转化为结论撤回或调整；该框架可支持后续研究定位此类推理失效并改进评测与训练方法。

Abstract: Defeasible reasoning is a type of reasoning where inferences are drawn from plausible current evidence, but can be retracted upon the introduction of newer evidence. Although recent studies have examined language-model behaviors in defeasible reasoning, the datasets have been static and lack wide coverage of non-monotonic reasoning categories. We introduce DeReLab, a generative framework that produces multi-turn belief-updating conversations from parameterized graph structures across default and inheritance reasoning, with formally verified ground truth at every turn, enabling controlled measurement of how models respond to confirming and disconfirming evidence. This controlled generation process creates a testbed for experimental designs that isolate specific reasoning demands. Applying this capability to the study of confirmation bias, we evaluate nine open and proprietary large language models and find that nearly all exhibit a systematic tendency to accept congruent evidence while resisting incongruent updates, with several models correctly identifying a weakening update yet failing to revise their conclusion. We believe our work and findings will facilitate future research on evaluating language models in defeasible reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [From Metaheuristics to Exact Methods: A CP-SAT Approach for Multi-Objective Healthcare Workforce Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30419)
*Vipul Patel,Anirudh Deodhar,Dagnachew Birru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向多角色、多技能医疗排班的CP-SAT约束规划模型，在保证14项硬约束零违规的前提下，联合优化15项软目标。实验显示模型可处理跨午夜班次、细粒度时段与多周排班，并在若干基准上取得可行性、最优性和服务质量改进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗排班同时受劳动规则、覆盖需求、员工偏好与成本约束影响，属于NP难优化问题。现有遗传算法、整数规划和传统约束规划通常仅覆盖较少的班次级约束，难以同时处理多角色多技能差异、强制休息及其时间位置、按病情加权的工作量公平性、子班次粒度、跨周稳定性和跨午夜班次，因此需要更完整且可验证合规的统一建模框架。

Method: 作者构建CP-SAT公式，将29项约束分为14项硬约束和15项软目标：硬约束用于保证法规与排班可行性，软目标通过统一加权惩罚函数优化。核心设计包括：以班次窗口分解支持带中点控制的休息安排；以病情严重度加权衡量工作负荷公平性；支持15分钟至1天的多粒度时间分辨率；引入跨周稳定性；通过网格偏移预处理将跨午夜班次映射至同一排班日，从而无需修改求解器主体。

Result: 模型在18个实例上均实现硬约束零违规，包括5个合成医院单元实例、10个INRC-II基准实例和3个含跨午夜夜班的NRP-23兼容实例。在INRC-II n005w4上，模型以104秒证明目标值118为最优，最优性缺口为0.0%。最大测试规模达到80名员工、179,800个变量和351,425条约束；相对MOGA，服务质量指标提升50%至67%。模型规模近似线性增长，约为每名员工增加4,400个变量。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要价值在于以统一CP-SAT框架覆盖更全面的医疗排班要求，并通过硬约束实现结构性合规保证。实验表明其具有处理多周、多技能、跨午夜及细粒度排班的扩展能力。但摘要中的“行业平均”比较缺少具体统计口径，软目标权重设定、不同实例间的求解时间分布以及与更多强基线方法的公平对比仍需结合全文进一步评估。

Abstract: Healthcare workforce scheduling is an NP-hard optimization problem requiring simultaneous satisfaction of labor regulations, coverage requirements, employee preferences and cost objectives. Existing approaches (genetic algorithms, integer programming, constraint programming) model 6-12 constraints at shift-level granularity and cannot guarantee regulatory compliance. They also lack support for multi-role, multi-skill heterogeneity, mandatory break scheduling with midpoint control, acuity-weighted workload equity, sub-shift granularity, inter-week stability, and cross-midnight shifts.
  This paper presents CP-SAT: a Constraint Programming formulation for multi-role, multi-skill healthcare scheduling. CP-SAT enforces 14 hard constraints guaranteeing zero regulatory violations, while optimizing 15 soft objectives via a unified weighted penalty function. Contributions include a shift-window decomposition enabling break scheduling with centrality control, acuity-weighted workload equity, multi-granularity resolution from 15 minutes to 1 day, inter-week stability, and grid-offset preprocessing mapping cross-midnight shifts into a single scheduling day without solver changes.
  CP-SAT is evaluated on 18 instances: five synthetic hospital units (10-33 nurses), 10 INRC-II benchmarks (5-80 nurses, up to 8-week horizons) and 3 NRP-23 compatible instances (10-25 nurses) with cross-midnight Night shifts. Results: zero hard-constraint violations across all 18 instances by construction; proven optimality on INRC-II n005w4 (objective 118, gap 0.0%, 104s); feasible schedules scaling to 179,800 variables and 351,425 constraints (80 nurses); service quality improved 50-67% over MOGA; and model size scaling near-linearly at approximately 4,400 variables per employee. The formulation enforces 29 total constraints (14 hard, 15 soft), nearly three times the industry average.

</details>


### [148] [EvoSkill Injection: Red-Teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30429)
*Doyun Kim,Chanwoo Kim,Sugyeong Eo,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSkill Injection威胁模型，研究自演化智能体在自主生成、存储和复用技能过程中被植入恶意技能的风险，并通过SARGE框架与两个基准数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于技能的智能体能够降低重复推理成本并提升任务执行稳定性，但技能的自主演化也可能成为能力污染的新攻击面，使不安全行为被保存为正常技能并在后续任务中持续复用。

Method: 提出SARGE红队测试框架，通过迭代式的技能生成、升级和强化交互诱导智能体形成恶意技能；构建EvoSkillBench用于评估恶意技能形成过程，并构建EvoSkillSafetyBench用于测试攻击后技能是否被检索和激活。

Result: 实验表明，SARGE能够诱导自演化智能体形成恶意技能；这些技能会被持久化存储，并在后续交互中反复检索和激活，显示出持续性能力污染风险。

Conclusion: 自演化技能机制不仅带来能力扩展，也引入了持久化安全风险。未来需要针对技能生成、存储、检索和激活全流程建立检测、隔离与审计机制，以防止不安全能力长期保留和复用。

Abstract: LLM-based agent systems increasingly adopt skill-based architectures to reduce repetitive reasoning costs and improve stable, efficient task execution. Recent studies propose self-evolving agents that autonomously generate, refine, and reuse skills from past experiences to enable continuous capability evolution. However, autonomous skill evolution introduces a new attack surface in which malicious capabilities are generated, stored, and reused as legitimate skills. In this paper, we define EvoSkill Injection as a threat model targeting the autonomous skill generation and evolution pipeline of self-evolving agents. We further propose SARGE (Red-teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in self-evolving agents), a red-teaming framework for evaluating this threat model through iterative generation, escalation, and reinforcement interactions. To support our framework, we construct EvoSkillBench, a benchmark dataset of malicious interaction trajectories for inducing malicious skill formation in self-evolving agents, and introduce EvoSkillSafetyBench, a post-attack benchmark for evaluating whether injected malicious skills are subsequently retrieved and activated as harmful behaviors. Our evaluation shows that SARGE induces malicious skill formation and that injected skills are persistently stored and repeatedly activated, highlighting the risk of persistent capability corruption.

</details>


### [149] [CHASE: How Content Ecosystems Are Reshaped When Ranking Is the Only Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30466)
*Qianwen Gao,Zichang Su,Yiwen Hou,Arlen Kumar,Leanid Palkhouski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CHASE仿真框架，研究创作者持续针对固定的大语言模型排序信号优化内容时，内容生态与质量激励如何变化。结果表明，经过20轮迭代后，六个领域的质量—排序一致性均下降，且效应具有显著领域差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎优化旨在提升内容在大语言模型检索与生成场景中的可见性，但现有研究较少考察大量创作者反复适应同一排序机制后，对整体内容分布、独立质量与创作激励的长期影响。

Method: 作者将排序作为来源可见性的代理指标，并以生成回答中的引用情况验证该抽象：六个领域的排序—引用AUC为0.853±0.093。CHASE在20轮中循环执行文档排序、区分排序特征、内容重写与评估；同时设置随机目标对照，以区分“针对排序信号的适应”与“单纯反复改写”的影响。

Result: 从第0轮到第20轮，六个领域的质量—排序Spearman相关系数变化均为负，范围为-0.107至-0.018，平均为-0.068。这意味着越接近排序特征画像的文档，其排序表现与独立质量评价的一致性反而越低。随机目标对照表明，该现象主要与对排序导出激励的适应有关，而非迭代改写本身；不同领域的内容生态演化模式存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 固定排序信号在被持续利用时，可能诱导内容向易获得排名的特征收敛，并削弱排序结果对独立质量的表征能力。该研究强调，面向生成式检索系统的排序设计与评估不应只关注短期可见性，还应监测长期生态效应、领域异质性及抗操纵能力。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is increasingly used to improve content visibility in LLM-based retrieval systems, yet its population-level effects under repeated optimization remain poorly understood. We introduce Content Homogenization under rAnking Signal Exploitation (CHASE), a controlled simulation framework for studying how content ecosystems are reshaped when creators repeatedly adapt documents to an LLM ranking signal. We use ranking as a proxy for source visibility and validate this abstraction against citations in grounded generated responses, obtaining a rank-citation AUC of 0.853 $\pm$ 0.093 across six domains. CHASE then iterates ranking, feature discrimination, rewriting, and evaluation over 20 rounds across different domains. Quality-ranking alignment decreases in all six domains: from R0 to R20, the change in Spearman's rho ranges from -0.107 to -0.018, with a mean change of -0.068, which means documents closer to the ranking feature profile become less aligned with independently judged document quality over the simulation horizon. A random-target control has shown that it is associated with adaptation toward ranking-derived incentives rather than iterative rewriting alone. The resulting ecosystem dynamics are strongly domain-dependent. Together, these findings show how repeated optimization against a fixed LLM ranking signal can reshape both content populations and the incentives faced by content creators.

</details>


### [150] [CM2: Multimodal Cultural Reasoning via an Integrated Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30498)
*Qi Li,Zhaojie Kang,Yingjie He,Zheng Lin,Hao Zhang,Guangxin Wu,Yan Gong,Rong Fu,Jianyuan Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CM2多智能体框架，用于提升多模态大语言模型在跨学科文化推理任务中的表现，并在CM2D基准及多种模型骨干上取得相对CoT等常规推理范式的稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在STEM任务中表现突出，这类任务通常依赖较稳定的符号体系与纵向、逐步推导；但对于需要整合视觉、文本、外部知识及跨领域语境的横向文化推理，其能力尚缺乏充分研究。因此，论文旨在构建更贴近人类文化解释认知路径的推理框架。

Method: CM2采用多智能体设计，并组合五类关键机制：多模态感知用于提取不同模态证据；检索增强生成用于补充外部知识；网络化推理用于关联多来源信息；门控融合用于动态整合并筛选不同模块或模态的证据；奖励驱动反馈用于优化推理与决策过程。论文还通过消融实验评估各模块贡献，并通过冲突分析检验模型是否具备跨模态证据仲裁能力。

Result: 在CM2D数据集上，CM2在多个多模态大语言模型骨干上均优于链式思维及其他典型推理方法。消融结果表明，所提出的各组成模块均对最终性能具有贡献；冲突分析进一步显示，模型并非仅进行表层模态拼接，而能够在存在证据冲突时实施跨模态权衡与裁决。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将多智能体协作、检索增强、网络推理、门控融合与反馈学习结合，能够有效改善多模态模型的跨领域文化解释与推理能力。其主要价值在于拓展了MLLM评测与推理研究从结构化STEM推导向复杂语境理解的范围；但摘要未提供CM2D的任务构成、评价指标、具体增幅及误差案例，模型的泛化性、成本和鲁棒性仍需结合正文进一步判断。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown remarkable success in STEM domains, where progress is often driven by vertical, step-by-step deduction under relatively stable symbol systems. Their horizontal, interdisciplinary cultural reasoning, however, remains underexplored.We propose CM2, a multi-agent framework grounded in the cognitive pathway of human cultural interpretation. CM2 integrates multimodal perception, retrieval-augmented generation, networked reasoning, gated fusion, and reward-driven feedback.Experiments on CM2D across multiple MLLM backbones show consistent gains over CoT and typical reasoning paradigms; ablations validate each module's contribution, and conflict analyses confirm genuine cross-modal arbitration.

</details>


### [151] [ScienceArena: Benchmarking LLMs on Latest Scientific Olympiad Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30517)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Wenpu Liu,Yaoming Li,Linfeng Hao,Shuyang Hou,Zijian Guo,Xinrui Zhang,Yuntian Zhao,Zhengyang Wang,Wenrui Liu,Yuhan Wu,Tong Yang,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ScienceArena：一个面向前沿大语言模型科学推理能力的开放式竞赛题基准，覆盖物理、化学和生物学。实验显示，部分顶尖模型在若干公开国际竞赛中可达到奖牌级评分，但化学推理与长程一致性仍是主要短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准日益出现饱和与训练数据污染问题，难以可靠衡量模型的真实科学推理。传统选择题也不足以评估多步骤解题、图像理解、推导结构和过程性得分等能力，因此需要更接近高水平科学竞赛、且具备细粒度评分依据的新基准。

Method: 作者从13项公开科学竞赛中收集并数字化题目，构建包含题干、图示、官方解答和过程评分细则的结构化数据集，并由竞赛获奖者审核。针对开放式答案人工评分成本高的问题，作者以物理和化学竞赛中五个模型的历史答案为样本，用获奖者评分作为真值校准LLM裁判；随后使用表现较强的裁判模型，对14个近期LLM进行交错式作答评测。

Result: 经校准后，两种LLM裁判给出的总分与专家评分的差距可控制在1分以内。获奖者评注表明，模型失误更常来自图示定位、解答结构保真和全局问题控制不足，而非单纯缺少学科术语。总体上，领先模型在部分公开国际竞赛中取得了相当于奖牌水平的过程评分；但在化学题以及需要长期连贯规划的任务上，性能仍显著受限。

Conclusion: ScienceArena为检测大语言模型在真实、高难度科学问题上的推理能力提供了更具挑战性和可解释性的评测框架。结果表明，顶尖模型已具备一定竞赛级科学解题能力，但其视觉—符号整合、复杂化学推理和长程解题稳定性尚未达到可靠的专家水平。

Abstract: Benchmark saturation and data contamination increasingly obscure genuine scientific reasoning in frontier LLMs. We introduce \textsc{ScienceArena}, an olympiad-style benchmark from thirteen public science competitions in physics, chemistry, and biology, including IPhO and IChO 2025--2026, IBO 2023, USAPhO 2026, and USNCO 2025. Its open-ended, multi-step problems use process-credit rubrics, making faithful scoring difficult. We build ScienceArena through an expert-audited digitization pipeline that converts official exams, figures, solutions, and rubrics into structured items verified by olympiad medalists. To scale evaluation beyond costly human grading, we calibrate LLM-as-judge against medalist ground truth on archived answers from five models across IPhO and IChO; two strong judges stay within one point of expert total scores. Medalist notes show that failures often stem from visual grounding, structure fidelity, and global problem control rather than missing terminology. Evaluating fourteen recent LLMs with interleaved solving, we find that top models obtain medal-equivalent rubric scores on several public international exams, while chemistry and long-horizon consistency remain key bottlenecks. We provide an interactive \href{https://science-arena.onrender.com/}{demo}.

</details>


### [152] [Learning-Assisted Congestion-Aware Route Scheduling for Semiconductor Fab Material Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30520)
*Hao Yin,Meiqi Tu,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TN-DCR（运输网络感知动态拥堵表征），用于半导体晶圆厂自动物料搬运系统中的在线中继路径调度。该方法同时预测运输时间与极端拥堵风险，并在风险约束下选择路径；闭环评估显示平均交付时间下降16.4%，内部资源等待时间下降22.6%，吞吐量基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物料控制系统需在任务执行前实时为每条运输指令规划中继路径，但路径成本的高分位部分主要受异构、部分可观测中继设备的排队影响。仅优化平均运输时间不足以避免严重拥堵，因此需要在决策时刻联合估计交付时长、排队时间及极端拥堵概率，同时严格避免使用预测时刻之后的信息。

Method: 方法以历史中继片段构建静态有向运输图，并设计TN-DCR特征表示：融合路径结构先验、多时间窗口的全网拥堵上下文、路径级瓶颈暴露度，以及可归纳的图感知路径嵌入。全部特征遵循预测时刻安全约束，仅使用预测时刻之前已观测的数据。随后分别训练排队时间与转运时间回归器，并采用序数多标签分类器输出经校准的多阈值超限概率；再以经验贝叶斯库存键残差校正缓解排队时间系统性低估。最终将预测结果嵌入轻量级运筹决策模型，在极端拥堵概率受限条件下最小化预测交付时间。

Result: 受控闭环实验表明，采用该调度框架后，平均交付时间降低16.4%，内部资源等待时间降低22.6%，系统吞吐量基本保持不变。这说明模型不仅改善了平均效率，也降低了由拥堵造成的资源等待，而未以产出能力为代价。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将图结构、动态拥堵状态、路径瓶颈风险和概率校准预测结合到安全的在线特征构造流程中，并通过风险约束优化将预测直接转化为调度决策。结果表明，该方案适合处理具有部分可观测排队设备和尾部拥堵风险的实时运输网络调度问题。

Abstract: Automated material handling systems in semiconductor fabs are operated by a material control system (MCS) that must schedule a relay route for every transport command online, before execution. This is a data-driven scheduling problem in which route cost is dominated in the upper tail by queueing at heterogeneous, partially observable relay equipment, so route selection requires estimating both delivery time and congestion risk at the decision moment. This paper proposes a transport-network-aware dynamic congestion representation (TN-DCR). Built on a static directed transport graph induced by historically observed relay segments, TN-DCR combines structural route priors, multi-window network-wide congestion context, route-level bottleneck exposure, and an inductive graph-aware route embedding, all constructed under a prediction-time-safety invariant that admits only information observed strictly before the prediction moment. The representation feeds separate queue- and transfer-time regressors and an ordinal multi-label classifier producing calibrated multi-threshold exceedance scores, with an empirical-Bayes stock-key residual correction reducing systematic queue-time underprediction. The predictions serve as costs in a risk-constrained route-scheduling rule that minimizes predicted delivery time subject to a bound on extreme-congestion probability, embedding the learned predictors within a lightweight operations-research decision model. In a controlled closed-loop evaluation, mean delivery time falls by 16.4\% and internal resource waiting time by 22.6\% while throughput remains essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [153] [DiffPDE: Masked Diffusion Language Models as PDE Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30532)
*Wenxuan Guo,Yuyang Hong,Lubin Fan,Zhaojin Fu,Lin Chen,Kun Ding,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiffPDE采用离散扩散语言模型进行偏微分方程求解器代码修复，通过局部重掩码与填充仅重生成错误片段，并以ID-GRPO支持多轮调试，从而在PDEBench上兼顾准确性与修复速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器合成方法多使用自回归模型，其从左至右的全局解码会在局部错误场景中重复生成大量正确代码，造成冗余计算。论文旨在使生成范围与PDE代码错误通常具有的稀疏、局部特征相匹配。

Method: 框架核心为DiffPDE：先定位或保留正确上下文，再对疑似错误区域进行局部重掩码，并利用离散扩散式填充生成替换代码。针对存在依赖关系、需按顺序修复的耦合错误，论文提出Iterative Debugging GRPO（ID-GRPO），在单条轨迹中执行多轮调试，并利用中间奖励优化各轮修复决策。

Result: 在PDEBench实验中，DiffPDE取得了具有竞争力的准确率，优于同等规模的自回归模型，并显著缩短代码修复时间。摘要未提供具体指标、模型规模、错误定位方式及统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，扩散式局部填充比全局自回归解码更适合局部代码修复任务；结合多轮奖励优化后，模型还可处理需要连续修改的复杂错误。其主要贡献是将局部生成和迭代调试机制引入PDE求解器代码修复。

Abstract: Existing approaches for synthesizing Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers predominantly rely on autoregressive models, yet their global left-to-right decoding incurs substantial redundancy when addressing inherently localized bugs. In this work, we challenge this inefficient paradigm and propose DiffPDE, a framework leveraging discrete diffusion language models for targeted code repair. By introducing a localized re-masking and infilling strategy, DiffPDE regenerates only erroneous regions while preserving correct context, naturally aligning generation with the sparse nature of PDE errors. Furthermore, to handle coupled bugs requiring sequential interventions, we present Iterative Debugging GRPO (ID-GRPO), a reinforcement learning scheme that enables multi-round debugging within single trajectories via intermediate rewards. Experiments on PDEBench show that DiffPDE achieves competitive accuracy, outperforms same-scale AR models, and significantly accelerates repair.

</details>


### [154] [GarmentWeaver: Schema-Aware Structured Synthesis for Multimodal Sewing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30550)
*Yinwen Lu,Weihao Luo,Yueqi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GarmentWeaver提出一种模式感知的多模态缝纫版型生成框架，通过层次化结构建模和可行性约束，从草图与文本等设计线索生成更准确、可执行且适合仿真的缝纫版型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将服装规格表示为扁平的长序列，导致服装结构与细节参数相互纠缠，容易产生冗余部件、局部细节不准确以及仿真兼容性较差等问题。因此，需要一种能够显式建模服装结构并保证输出可执行性的生成方法。

Method: 方法包括三部分：1）设计模式感知的目标构建策略，根据服装相关性激活层次化结构分支，形成紧凑的生成目标；2）以预训练视觉语言模型为基础构建生成器，实现草图、文本等多模态服装理解；3）引入可行性感知正则化，约束生成结果满足结构有效性与仿真兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，GarmentWeaver相较于具有竞争力的基线方法能够生成更准确、更具可执行性的缝纫版型，并取得更好的服装仿真效果。

Conclusion: 研究验证了模式感知的结构化生成对于可靠多模态缝纫版型预测的有效性，为数字服装设计中的可解释生成与可仿真建模提供了新方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Sewing pattern generation aims to infer executable sewing patterns from design cues such as sketches and textual descriptions. As an interpretable and simulation-compatible representation, sewing patterns are particularly valuable for digital garment creation. However, existing methods often model garment specifications as flat long sequences, which entangles garment structure with detailed parameters and leads to redundant components, inaccurate local details, and poor simulation compatibility. In this paper, we present GarmentWeaver, a schema-aware framework for multimodal Sewing pattern generation. GarmentWeaver constructs compact hierarchical targets by activating garment-relevant structural branches and predicts executable Sewing patterns in a structured manner. Specifically, we introduce a schema-aware target construction strategy, build the generator on top of a pretrained vision-language model for multimodal garment understanding, and impose feasibility-aware regularization to encourage structurally valid and simulation-compatible outputs. Extensive experiments show that GarmentWeaver produces more accurate and more executable sewing patterns than strong baselines, while also yielding better simulation results. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of schema-aware structured generation for reliable multimodal Sewing pattern prediction.

</details>


### [155] [AdaPath: Query-Adaptive Path-Finding via Path-Bank for Multi-Hop Implicit Biomedical KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30556)
*Jun Hyeong Kim,Dongki Kim,Yinhua Piao,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AdaPath，用于生物医学知识图谱问答中的多跳路径检索。其核心是从Path-Bank中检索与当前问题自适应匹配的元路径，以补足问题中未显式给出的中间推理线索，并在稠密图中约束搜索方向。作者同时发布BioStrat-QA基准，用于按问题暴露的中间推理信息程度分层评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用知识图谱路径搜索方法难以直接适用于生物医学问答：其一，多跳问题通常不明确给出中间推理实体或关系，且同一问题可能存在多条正确推理路径；其二，生物医学知识图谱连接稠密，局部邻域候选众多，易导致路径搜索偏离目标。因此，需要一种能结合问题语义与图结构、并动态缩小搜索空间的方法。

Method: AdaPath预先构建Path-Bank，将可复用的路径模式作为候选元路径集合。面对具体查询时，模型检索与查询语义及知识图谱结构相适应的元路径，并将其作为路径搜索的结构性先验：一方面为缺少显式中间步骤的查询提供潜在推理线索，另一方面在每跳搜索中筛除不符合元路径约束的邻居节点与关系。BioStrat-QA则依据多跳问题中中间推理信息的显露程度进行分层，以检验方法在不同线索缺失条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 摘要声称，AdaPath在多个生物医学知识图谱问答基准上稳定优于对比方法；尤其当多跳问题包含较少表层中间线索时，仍能维持具有实际意义的路径搜索能力。这表明查询自适应元路径在降低稠密图搜索歧义、改善隐式多跳推理方面有效。摘要未提供具体数据集名称、评价指标、绝对分数、统计显著性或计算开销。

Conclusion: 该工作将元路径检索作为生物医学知识图谱多跳推理的自适应导航机制，针对隐式推理线索不足与图结构稠密两类问题提出了统一解决方案。其贡献包括AdaPath框架和按推理线索可见性分层的BioStrat-QA基准。论文的关键待验证点包括Path-Bank构建方式、对未见关系模式的泛化能力、路径正确性与最终答案正确性的对应关系，以及在大规模知识图谱上的效率。

Abstract: Path-finding over knowledge graphs has become an effective way to ground LLM reasoning on multi-hop questions. However, biomedical QA introduces two distinct challenges that general-domain methods are not designed for: (i) queries do not expose intermediate reasoning and can be answered through multiple valid pathways, and (ii) biomedical knowledge graphs are densely connected, so path-finding methods easily take wrong turns. To address these challenges, we propose AdaPath, a path-finding framework that retrieves query-adaptive meta-paths from Path-Bank, which captures both query semantics and biomedical knowledge graph structure. AdaPath provides the missing cues in biomedical queries while effectively pruning dense knowledge graph neighborhoods during multi-hop reasoning. We further release BioStrat-QA, a biomedical KGQA benchmark that stratifies multi-hop queries by how much intermediate reasoning they expose. Across biomedical KGQA benchmarks, AdaPath consistently outperforms baselines, sustaining meaningful path-finding even when multi-hop queries expose less surface information. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jun-Hyeong-Kim/AdaPath.

</details>


### [156] [TuringLLM: Efficiently Scaling Foundation Models Toward Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30567)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yizhao Wang,Da Zhu,Hua Zhou,Yue He,Jiahui Hu,Shaman Tang,Hanlin Chen,Yuhua Wei,Anhua Liu,Shuang Su,Rui Xin,MingYuan Wang,MingHao Li,HaoJie Yang,Siqi Liu,Jianlei Zheng,WeiChao Huang,Qiman Wu,Hang Zhang,HongGou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要提出Turing-20B-A2B：一个总参数量200亿、每个词元约激活20亿参数的专家混合语言模型，目标是在长上下文与低预填充延迟场景中兼顾能力、计算成本和部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密模型在长上下文推理时面临计算与延迟压力，而传统专家路由又可能造成专家负载不均、计算预算波动及部署执行不规则。作者旨在构建一个激活参数较少、上下文可扩展且具备稳定实际推理效率的模型。

Method: 模型采用动态top-k的分位数路由，根据词元自适应分配专家，并控制平均计算预算及专家负载均衡；训练时使用无丢弃路由，部署的提示词预填充阶段改用容量受限路由以提高执行规则性。注意力层使用Lightning Attention与少量全注意力层的混合架构。训练采用三阶段渐进式课程，并通过继续预训练将原生上下文扩展至128K，推理时借助YaRN进一步扩展到512K。

Result: 在基础模型阶段，Turing-20B-A2B的综合通用能力超过Qwen3-8B Base，并接近Qwen3.5-9B Base；同时保持较强的长上下文表现和较优的预填充延迟扩展特性。摘要未提供具体基准分数、硬件配置、吞吐量或延迟数值，因此性能增益的统计显著性与跨平台可复现性尚无法据此判断。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合动态专家路由、部署阶段容量约束及混合注意力，可以在仅约20亿激活参数的预算下获得接近更大基础模型的能力，并改善长上下文推理的实用效率。其核心贡献是展示能力、上下文长度和推理成本之间的工程化折中方案。

Abstract: We present Turing-20B-A2B, a 20B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts language model that activates approximately 2B parameters per token, designed for long-context and latency-sensitive physical AI applications. The model adopts Quantile Routing in a dynamic top-k configuration, enabling token-adaptive expert allocation while maintaining balanced expert utilization and a controlled average compute budget. During deployment, we further apply capacity-constrained routing to prompt prefill for more regular and efficient expert execution, while retaining dropless routing during pretraining. Turing-20B-A2B also employs a hybrid attention architecture that combines Lightning Attention with a small number of full-attention layers for efficient long-context modeling. The model is pretrained with a progressive three-stage curriculum and extended to a native context length of 128K through continued pretraining, with further inference-time extension to 512K using YaRN. Despite its compact active-parameter budget, Turing-20B-A2B achieves, at the base-model stage, overall general capability exceeding Qwen3-8B Base and approaching Qwen3.5-9B Base, while maintaining strong long-context performance and favorable prefill-latency scaling. These results demonstrate an effective balance among model capability, long-context scalability, and practical inference efficiency.

</details>


### [157] [Automated Testing of LLM-Based Post Hoc Explainers Using Model Checking as an Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30581)
*Dennis Gross,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于检验大语言模型对序贯决策策略进行事后自然语言解释是否忠实的方法：以概率模型检测作为自动化正确性预言机，并以查询类别体系和难度优先级生成测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成看似合理的策略解释，但这些解释可能与环境事实不一致。现有方法缺乏系统验证机制，主要面临两项软件测试难题：解释正确性缺少可自动判定的预言机，以及自然语言查询缺乏结构化表示，难以系统地产生高价值测试用例。

Method: 方法首先利用概率模型检测计算MDP环境中的精确参考结果，将其作为对LLM回答进行自动评分的测试预言机。其次，构建面向环境层事实的事后查询类别体系，以结构化查询空间并生成测试案例；再依据针对具体问题的诊断难度分数，对测试案例进行优先排序，从而更有效地暴露模型在不同解释类型上的失效情形。

Result: 在七个MDP环境中，该测试框架区分了三种开放权重LLM：推理模型通过85%的测试案例，中等规模模型通过70%，而1B参数模型的表现低于随机基线。此外，基于难度的优先排序比随机抽样能够发现显著更多的困难测试案例。

Conclusion: 概率模型检测结合结构化查询生成，可为LLM生成的策略解释提供可扩展、可自动化的忠实性测试。实验表明，不同LLM的解释可靠性存在明显差异；该框架还能为无模型、无法获得验证预言机的实际场景提供关于解释可信度的经验性参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used as post hoc explainers of sequential decision-making policies, producing natural-language explanations of why an action was chosen. However, LLMs often generate plausible but incorrect statements, and no existing approach systematically tests whether such explanations are faithful to the underlying environment. Two classic software testing challenges stand in the way: there is no oracle for the correctness of an explanation, and the test inputs, natural language queries about a policy's behavior, lack the structure needed for systematic test case generation. We address both. Probabilistic model checking provides the test oracle, computing exact reference results against which LLM answers are graded automatically. A taxonomy of post hoc query categories structures the input space around the environment-level facts from which policy explanations are composed; test cases generated from it are prioritized by question-specific diagnostic difficulty scores. Across seven MDP environments, the testing separates three open-weight LLMs: a reasoning model passes 85% of test cases, a mid-size model 70%, and a 1B model falls below the random baseline, while prioritization surfaces significantly harder cases than random selection. Our results indicate how trustworthy LLM-generated explanations are in model-free settings, where the same LLMs are used but no oracle exists to verify them.

</details>


### [158] [Geometry of Divergence: Tracking Hidden-State Trajectories for Adaptive Multi-Turn Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30650)
*Jie Liang,Zhengxin Yu,Hamid Nasiri,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将多轮推理建模为LLM隐藏状态轨迹，并利用时间曲率与方差斜率识别表示漂移和关键转折点，从而在较低资源消耗下区分正确与错误推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互中，持续累积的上下文可能导致模型对早期任务相关信息的内部表示发生漂移，进而破坏目标一致性。传统方法难以在任务完成前判断推理是否正在偏离，因此需要可解释、低成本的轨迹级诊断信号。

Method: 将每轮隐藏状态表示为轨迹，提取时间曲率以衡量相邻轮次更新方向的一致性，提取方差斜率以衡量探索空间的扩张或收缩。在四项任务和三种底层LLM上进行评估，并将每个 episode 分解为由 Read、Write、Respond、Transfer 四类动作构成的三动作链，分析不同动作模式下信号的可分性及其对关键轮次识别的作用。

Result: 时间曲率和方差斜率能够在任务完成前区分正确与错误 episode，且不同动作链模式对应不同的判别信号。在τ-Bench上，基于轨迹几何的干预将任务成功率从24.1%提升至39.6%，同时将token成本降低11.2%。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态轨迹几何为监测多轮推理中的表示漂移、定位关键转折点和优化资源分配提供了有效工具。时间曲率与方差斜率具有互补性，其作用取决于具体动作链结构，说明面向动作模式的动态诊断与控制具有应用价值。

Abstract: LLM agents need to sustain goal-consistent reasoning across long multi-turn interactions under strict resource constraints. However, as the multi-turn context accumulates, it can destabilize the underlying LLM's internal representation of task-relevant information from earlier turns, blurring the boundary between constructive reasoning and representation drift. We formulate multi-turn reasoning as a hidden-state trajectory of the underlying LLM that is characterized via two complementary signals: temporal curvature that captures the directional consistency of turn-to-turn updates, and variance slope which measures the expansion or contraction of the exploration space. Across four tasks and three underlying LLMs, we observed that these geometric signals distinguish between correct and incorrect episodes prior to completion. We further decompose each episode into three-action chains formed from four actions (Read, Write, Respond, Transfer) and show that separability is action-dependent, with different signals distinguishing various chain patterns. Our experiments demonstrate that trajectory geometry can identify critical turns in the reasoning process, increasing task success rates on $τ$-Bench from 24.1% to 39.6% while reducing token cost by 11.2%.

</details>


### [159] [PyKEEN-NSX: A Modular Framework for Static, Dynamic and Schema-Aware Negative Sampling in PyKEEN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30652)
*Ivan Diliso,Nicola Fanizzi,Claudia d'Amato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PyKEEN-NSX，一个面向知识图谱嵌入负采样的PyKEEN扩展框架。其核心是将“候选负例池生成”和“负例选择”解耦，以统一支持随机、模式感知及动态负采样策略，并通过实验揭示约束型策略常受候选负例不足问题限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱通常仅包含正事实，嵌入模型训练所需的负三元组必须人工构造。现有KGE工具多仅支持基础随机扰动，难以实现依赖图结构、模式语义或当前嵌入状态的高级负采样器，且缺乏可复用、可组合的开发抽象。因此，需要一个兼容现有训练流程且便于扩展的统一负采样框架。

Method: 作者扩展PyKEEN并设计模块化负采样架构：首先在显式上下文条件下生成候选负例池；随后由独立的选择策略从候选池中选取训练负例。该解耦使候选池构造规则与筛选准则可独立替换和组合，从而覆盖静态、模式约束和动态方法。基于该抽象实现六种负采样器，并保持对既有PyKEEN工作流和管线的兼容性。

Result: 在四个数据集上的概念验证表明，采用约束条件生成的候选负例池经常小于用户请求的负例数量。为满足训练批次的数量要求，系统需要以随机回退策略补充缺失负例，导致原先编码的结构或语义约束在实际采样结果中的影响被明显削弱。

Conclusion: PyKEEN-NSX为高级和定制化知识图谱负采样提供了统一、模块化且工程可集成的实现基础。研究同时指出，评价或设计受约束负采样方法时，不能只关注其理论筛选标准，还应量化候选负例可用性及随机回退比例，否则实际训练信号可能与预期策略不一致。

Abstract: Embedding methods have become popular due to their scalability on link prediction and/or triple classification tasks on Knowledge Graphs (KGs). Embedding models are trained relying on both positive and negative samples of triples. However, since KGs generally contain only positive assertions, negative samples are artificially generated through negative sampling strategies, ranging from simple random corruption to more sophisticated approaches that exploit structural, semantic, or embedding information. The design and implementation of advanced negative samplers remains challenging, as most popular Knowledge Graph Embedding (KGE) libraries provide support only for basic strategies and lack a unified framework for developing more advanced and customized solutions. To address this gap, we introduce PyKEEN-NSX, an extension of PyKEEN, the popular KGE framework, that provides a modular engineered abstraction for negative sampling. The proposed architecture separates the generation of candidate negative pools, conditioned on an explicit context, from the selection strategy, enabling the development and integration of static, schema-aware and dynamic approaches within a consistent framework. Based on this abstraction, we implement six negative samplers, while remaining fully compatible with existing PyKEEN workflows and pipelines. As a proof of concept, we study negative availability across four datasets, showing that constrained pools frequently fall below the requested number of negatives, so that the encoded criterion is to a large extent replaced by the random fallback that supplements them.

</details>


### [160] [HiRS-Agent: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Reliable Long-Horizon Remote Sensing Task Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30672)
*Boyang Mu,Zhiwei Wei,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiRS-Agent提出一种面向长时域遥感任务的分层多智能体系统，通过管理层与专家层协同，以及监督微调和验证引导的层次强化学习，提升工具调用能力与任务完成正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感智能体多采用单体式决策框架，难以处理多阶段、强依赖任务，容易出现执行不稳定、工具误用和跨阶段错误传播等问题，因此需要更结构化、可验证的协作机制。

Method: 系统采用两层架构：管理层负责动态路由、步骤级验证、重规划和终止控制；专家层依据遥感工作流组织领域工具，并执行子任务推理与工具调用。训练过程包括两阶段监督微调，以及验证引导的层次强化学习，用于联合优化协同策略和工具使用策略。

Result: 在Earth-Agent Benchmark和ThinkGeo数据集上的实验表明，HiRS-Agent显著提升了长时域工具使用能力和最终任务正确率，验证了分层多智能体协作对提高遥感智能体可靠性的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将全局任务管理、局部领域执行与过程验证相结合，HiRS-Agent能够缓解单体式系统中的错误累积和工具调用失误，为复杂长时域遥感任务提供了更可靠的智能体解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and multimodal models have pushed remote sensing (RS) processing from simple perception models to agentic systems designed to tackle complex, long-horizon RS tasks. However, existing systems often rely on monolithic decision-making frameworks, which fail to accommodate the multi-stage, interdependent nature of RS tasks. This centralized approach leads to challenges such as unstable task execution, incorrect tool usage, and error propagation across stages. To address these issues, we propose HiRS-Agent, a hierarchical multi-agent system for long-horizon RS task solving. HiRS-Agent adopts a two-level collaborative architecture: the Manager Layer handles dynamic routing, step-level verification, replanning, and termination control, while the Specialist Layer organizes domain-specific tools according to the RS workflow and is responsible for subtask reasoning and tool execution. To further enhance the system's capability, we introduce a two-stage supervised tuning strategy and a verification-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning stage to jointly optimize coordination and tool-use policies. Experiments on Earth-Agent Benchmark and ThinkGeo show that HiRS-Agent substantially improves long-horizon tool-use capability and final-task correctness, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured multi-agent collaboration for reliable RS agents. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/IntelliSensing/HiRS-Agent.

</details>


### [161] [MedAgent-R1: Faithfulness-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30676)
*Jiangwang Chen,Chenghao Zhang,Hengxing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，经强化学习训练的医疗检索智能体若仅优化答案结果，会出现“自信型幻觉”：准确率提升，但引用和推理依据更容易被事后编造。作者提出以证据忠实性为硬门控条件的奖励设计，显著降低伪造引用并提高证据覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景不仅要求答案正确，还要求临床人员能够依据可核验的文献证据审查推理过程。传统仅按最终答案给奖赏的训练目标可能鼓励模型依赖参数记忆作答，再生成看似合理但无证据支持的解释，从而造成准确性与忠实性脱钩。

Method: 作者分析了结果导向强化学习下的系统性失效模式，并提出“忠实性门控奖励”：只有回答被检索证据充分支撑时，模型才能获得准确性奖励；同时加入检索有效性和表达简洁性信号，以减少智能体通过无效检索、冗长表述或事后拼接引用来规避约束。该方法训练得到MedAgent-R1，并在相同的智能检索设定下与监督基线及GPT-4o比较。

Result: 相对监督基线，结果导向强化学习使准确率提高约5个百分点，但伪造引用率由16.5%升至31.8%。采用忠实性门控后，MedAgent-R1将伪造引用率降至4.7%，证据完整性由58.7提升至82.6，并保持75.1%的准确率；其HealthBench Safety得分提升13.2点。在忠实性相关人工评测维度上，MedAgent-R1优于GPT-4o：事实支持度为4.55对4.25，过度主张控制为4.40对4.15；但整体准确率仍低于GPT-4o。
