Method: 研究开展了为期三个月的用户研究，参与者为22名计算机科学专业高年级本科生和研究生。参与者需独立配置提交签名，在四个课程项目中使用签名，将配置扩展至第二台设备，检查含异常提交的外部代码仓库，并完成安全推理问题。研究据此观察任务完成情况、使用体验、异常识别能力及概念理解。

Result: 几乎所有参与者都成功为整个学期的每次提交签名，并对常规签名体验给予积极评价。然而，许多人在初始设置、多设备配置和仓库验证环节遇到阻碍。即使已持续签名一学期，参与者在验证时仍难以发现异常提交，超过四分之一未发现任何异常。近半数参与者至少表现出一种误解，涉及签名能够提供的保证或密钥管理方式。

Conclusion: 提交签名的有效采用不能仅依赖更易用的签名工具。工具设计与教育支持还应帮助开发者：将签名验证与已授权身份建立对应关系，正确解释缺失签名和未知密钥，并准确理解密钥生成、迁移、更新及撤销等生命周期操作。研究未进一步区分问题究竟主要源于工具、教育还是用户理解。

Abstract: Git commit signing, introduced in 2012, is one mechanism for establishing commit provenance in software supply chains, yet developer-controlled adoption remains rare and developers' experiences using it are understudied. To examine this experience, we conducted a three-month study with senior undergraduate and graduate computer science students (n = 22), whom we treat as proxies for junior developers. Participants configured commit signing independently, used it across four coursework projects, extended it to a second device, examined an external repository containing anomalous commits, and answered security-reasoning prompts. We found that while almost all participants successfully signed every commit and rated routine signing positively, many faced friction during setup, multi-device configuration, and repository verification. Despite signing all semester, they struggled to spot anomalous commits during verification, with over a quarter finding none. Additionally, nearly half expressed at least one misconception regarding signing guarantees or key management. Without isolating whether these difficulties stemmed from tooling, education, or understanding, we conclude that making signing easier is not enough to ensure effective security use. Rather, secure adoption also requires tools and education that support signature verification against authorized identities, interpretation of missing signatures and unknown keys, and correct reasoning about key-lifecycle operations.

</details>


### [196] [Safe to Resume? Breaking Execution Continuity of Agent Execution via Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29381)
*Guanlong Wu,Dahui Li,Ke Jiang,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统研究智能体系统中的检查点与回滚（C/R）安全问题，指出“恢复正确”不等于“恢复安全”：即使状态被准确还原，恢复后的内部状态、外部依赖与已产生副作用也可能无法对应任何合法执行历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化、状态化智能体在故障后需要依赖C/R避免高成本重建执行过程，但现有研究主要关注可用性和正确性，缺乏对安全恢复条件的系统分析。论文旨在填补检查点保存状态与安全继续执行所需依赖之间的认知与工程缺口。

Method: 作者考察代表性智能体C/R系统，归纳现有机制的设计空间，并构建描述恢复边界和状态依赖的通用执行模型。基于该模型，提出五类基础失效模式，覆盖内部状态不完整或不一致、外部依赖陈旧、非确定性重放以及未记录的外部副作用。随后构建多智能体分析流水线，用于重建执行语义、检测五类条件违反，并通过实际回滚进行验证。

Result: 论文在Hermes、Cline和LangGraph上展示三类端到端安全影响：恶意软件验证绕过、未授权邮件转发和重复支付。对五个代表性框架的评估表明，不同C/R架构中均会重复出现这些问题，说明其并非个别实现缺陷，而是普遍的恢复语义与依赖管理问题。

Conclusion: 安全的检查点恢复不能仅保证已保存状态的保真性，还必须保证内部状态、外部依赖、重放行为和外部副作用之间具有可验证的一致性。论文的核心贡献是给出统一分析模型、五类失效模式及自动化分析流程，为构建具备安全恢复能力的智能体C/R机制提供了系统性依据。

Abstract: AI agents are moving toward persistent, stateful execution across various applications, accumulating execution state and external effects that are costly to reconstruct after failures. Checkpoint and rollback (C/R) are becoming essential for recovery, yet their security implications remain largely unexplored. Correct rollback does not imply secure recovery: a faithfully restored checkpoint may resume an execution whose states, assumptions, and external effects never coexisted in any valid history. In this paper, we present the first systematic security study of checkpoint and rollback in existing agent systems. By examining representative agent C/R systems, we characterize the design space of existing C/R mechanisms and develop a general execution model that captures their recovery boundaries and state dependencies. From this model, we identify five fundamental failure modes spanning incomplete or inconsistent internal state, stale external dependencies, nondeterministic replay, and unrecorded external effects. We further demonstrate their security impact through three end-to-end attacks on Hermes, Cline, and LangGraph, enabling malware-verification bypass, unauthorized mail forwarding, and double payment. To systematically study these failures in practice, we develop a multi-agent analysis pipeline that reconstructs execution semantics, identifies violations of the five failure conditions, and validates them through actual rollback. Across five representative frameworks, our evaluation shows that these failures recur across heterogeneous C/R designs and stem from a common gap between the state restored by a checkpoint and the dependencies required for secure continuation.

</details>


### [197] [Benchmark Contamination: A Taxonomy Organized by Defeated Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29463)
*Johanna Angulo,Víctor Yeste,Hector Espinos-Morato*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向评测发布环节的数据污染分类法与披露协议，强调基准分数不仅反映模型能力，还受评测工具、提示预算、样本分布及污染状态影响。对41份文档的预注册分析显示，现有披露不完整，编码一致性总体较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜通常只发布模型和分数，导致真实能力与数据泄漏在观察上难以区分。既有分类法主要服务于自动检测，未直接回答在已采取缓解措施后仍有哪些效度威胁，因此需要一种围绕缓解措施和发布决策组织的分类与报告框架。

Method: 将污染划分为直接、衍生、时间、分布和评测中获取五类，覆盖训练阶段与评测阶段的泄漏。提出四字段披露协议，将“未知”设为合法值，并提供JSON Schema、验证器和示例。两名设计团队之外的编码员依据预注册量表，对41份文档进行编码，并使用线性加权κ评估变量级一致性。

Result: 在29份主要文档中，各变量κ值为0.00至0.35，中位数0.21，明显低于单编码员重测上限0.84；合并分析后的κ为0.46，提升主要来自机会一致性校正而非实际一致性改善。分层报告和评测中获取的污染类型低于预先注册的稳健阈值。分歧主要源于变量何时适用，而非文档内容本身。仅13%的文档报告提示预算，没有文档覆盖全部五类污染。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将污染分类与具体缓解措施及分数发布责任相连接，并提出应随单次评测分数记录评测中获取的污染信息。当前披露实践覆盖不足、测量一致性有限，未来需要更明确的适用判定规则和更全面的标准化报告。

Abstract: A benchmark score is a joint property of the model, the evaluation harness, the elicitation budget, the sampled population, and contamination status. Leaderboards publish the model and the score, so capability and leakage stay observationally equivalent. Existing taxonomies classify contamination for automated detection, not the question a reporter faces at publication: given the mitigations already applied, which validity threats remain open? We introduce a taxonomy organized by the mitigation each type defeats -- direct, derivative, temporal, distributional, and acquired -- spanning training-time and evaluation-time leakage. Holding out a private test set closes the first alone. The fifth is acquired during the evaluation itself; because it is a property of one run, it must be recorded with the reported score rather than with the benchmark release. We operationalize it as a four-field disclosure protocol in which "unknown" is a valid entry, released under CC BY 4.0 with a JSON Schema, a validator, and worked examples. Two coders external to the design team applied a pre-registered instrument to 41 documents. Per-variable linear-weighted $κ$ runs from 0.00 to 0.35 (median 0.21) over 29 main-pass documents against a single-coder test-retest ceiling of 0.84, collapsing under the class skew the registration anticipated; pooling raises it to 0.46 through chance correction rather than better agreement. Two variables fall below the prevalence-robust threshold registered in advance: strata reporting and the acquired type introduced here. Disagreement concentrates on when a variable applies rather than on what a document states. Elicitation budgets are reported in 13% of documents, and no document addresses all five types. The contribution is the taxonomy, the score-side artifact that follows from it, and a pre-registered measurement of instrument reliability and current disclosure.

</details>


### [198] [SpatialTrust: A Benchmark for Environmental Risk Recognition in Secure Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29489)
*Junbin Lu,Hsiang-Wei Huang,Saesha Wadhwa,Yu Ting Hsu,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialTrust基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在空间化安全认证场景中的环境风险识别能力，并提出SpatialTrustGuard结构化问答与审计流程以提升模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型评测较少关注模型能否在认证场景中可靠地识别、定位并解释视觉环境中的敏感因素与潜在风险，尤其缺乏对间接风险理解能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建SpatialTrust问答基准，从敏感因素检测、直接因素识别、间接因素识别、直接因素解释和间接因素解释五个维度评估专有及开源多模态大语言模型；同时设计SpatialTrustGuard，通过结构化问答和审计流程增强模型推理。

Result: 实验表明，当前多模态大语言模型整体表现有限，在间接风险的识别与解释方面尤为薄弱。SpatialTrustGuard将Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct的总体得分从36.78%提升至41.12%。

Conclusion: 空间风险感知仍是多模态大语言模型面临的关键挑战。专门化基准与结构化推理、审计机制有助于提升模型在安全认证场景中的可靠性与可信度。

Abstract: Visual environmental risk recognition plays an important role in secure authentication, where a user's surroundings may reveal sensitive information or introduce potential security risks. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) rarely examine whether models can reliably recognize, localize, and explain such risks in spatially grounded authentication scenarios. We present SpatialTrust, a question-answering benchmark for evaluating environmental risk recognition in secure authentication. SpatialTrust assesses five complementary abilities: sensitive factor detection, direct factor identification, indirect factor identification, direct factor explanation, and indirect factor explanation. We evaluate both proprietary and open-source MLLMs and find that current models show limited performance, especially in understanding and explaining indirect risks, indicating that spatial risk awareness remains a challenging capability for MLLMs. In addition, we introduce SpatialTrustGuard, a structured QA-and-audit pipeline that improves Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B-Instruct from 36.78% to 41.12% overall. Our findings highlight the need for dedicated benchmarks and structured inference methods to improve the trustworthiness of MLLMs in secure authentication.

</details>


### [199] [Context or Digits? Balancing Memorability and Efficiency in Virtual Reality Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29531)
*Yuxuan Huang,Qiao Jin,Tongyu Nie,Victoria Interrante,Evan Suma Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向虚拟现实的自适应方向式认证（ADBA）。在66人、持续2至3周的远程纵向对比实验中，ADBA相较6位PIN表现出更好的记忆性和更低的主观任务负荷，但其客观输入时间更长；系统还通过随机数字映射增强安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实认证需要在输入效率、密码可记忆性与安全性之间权衡。传统PIN输入直接但可能难记，而依赖虚拟环境语境的密码更易形成记忆线索，却可能不适合所有使用时机。作者旨在设计一种能随用户需求变化而切换认证方式的方案。

Method: ADBA要求用户在密码创建阶段依据虚拟环境上下文设定方向序列；认证阶段同时支持基于环境语境的方向输入和基于数字的输入，从而将创建时的记忆需求与使用时的效率需求解耦。研究采用远程纵向设计，招募66名参与者，在2至3周内将ADBA与6位PIN进行比较，评估记忆性、任务负荷、输入时长、使用偏好及密码分布特征。

Result: ADBA在密码记忆表现和感知任务负荷上优于6位PIN。尽管ADBA的实际输入耗时更长，参与者仍认为其效率较高。研究期间没有参与者选择数字输入路径，表明环境语境驱动的方向输入具有较强可接受性，或数字路径的实际需求未被充分激发。随机生成的数字表示可降低直接观察或固定映射带来的风险；但部分虚拟环境中出现密码同质化，提示上下文可能诱导相似选择。

Conclusion: ADBA在所测试的VR使用场景中较好地平衡了记忆性、主观使用负担与安全性，并优于传统6位PIN。其核心价值在于允许用户以环境线索创建易记密码，同时保留替代输入方式以适应后续需求。后续工作应进一步验证更广泛场景下的真实使用行为，分析数字路径未被采用的原因，并通过密码策略或环境设计缓解特定场景中的密码同质化问题。

Abstract: We present Adaptive Direction-Based Authentication (ADBA), a knowledge-based authentication method for Virtual Reality that decouples users' needs temporally by enforcing password creation based on virtual environment context while supporting both context- and digit-based entries during authentication. This design prioritizes memorability for new passwords and offers both efficient and memorable options to support users' evolving needs. We conducted a remote longitudinal study with 66 participants comparing ADBA against 6-digit PINs over 2-3 weeks. The results demonstrated that ADBA achieved superior memorability and lower perceived task load. Interestingly, no participant chose to enter via digits in the study, yet they still perceived ADBA to be highly efficiency despite longer objective entry times. ADBA also provided security benefits through randomly-generated digit representations, though some degree of password homogeneity was observed in specific virtual environments. Our findings suggest that ADBA offers solid advantages to the traditional PINs, and successfully addresses the tradeoffs between efficiency, memorability, and security under the usage scenarios considered in the study.

</details>


### [200] [OASIS: Optimizing Attacker Sequences for Hard-Label Black-Box Text Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29568)
*Qian Chen,Shiliang Xiao,Yuzhi Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于硬标签黑盒文本攻击的攻击者序列优化方法，通过自动选择攻击者组合，在攻击成功率与扰动程度之间取得更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同攻击方法具有不同的搜索轨迹和样本成功子集；现有方法主要优化单个攻击者或依赖人工组合，难以系统利用攻击者之间的互补性。

Method: 首先在候选攻击者序列上执行一次双目标攻击链搜索，同时优化攻击成功率和扰动程度；随后将选定的固定全局攻击链复用于实际攻击执行，以降低重复搜索开销并保持一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集、受害模型和大语言模型上的实验表明，该方法持续优于强单一攻击基线及人工构造的攻击链。

Conclusion: 攻击者组合并非单纯的实现选择，而是提升硬标签黑盒文本攻击性能的有效优化目标；自动化的序列设计能够增强攻击效果并兼顾扰动控制。

Abstract: Different attack methods follow different search trajectories, they succeed on different subsets of samples, whereas existing hard-label black-box text attacks mainly focus on improving individual attackers or manually combining them. We present {\OURS}, a method for optimizing attacker sequences in hard-label black-box text attacks. {\OURS} first performs a one-time bi-objective attack chain search over candidate sequences to balance attack success rate and perturbation, and then reuses the selected fixed global chain during attack chain execution. Experiments across multiple datasets, victim models, and large language models show that {\OURS} consistently outperforms strong standalone baselines and simple manually constructed chains. These results suggest that attacker composition is not merely an implementation choice, but a practical optimization target for improving hard-label black-box text attacks.

</details>


### [201] [Reactive Peripheral Modeling for Faithful Firmware Rehosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29737)
*Qinying Wang,Florian Hofhammer,Eduard Vlad,Jianqiang Wang,Marcel Busch,Shouling Ji,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出反应式外设建模（RPM）及其实现Bluezz，通过事件—条件—动作语义统一建模中断、MMIO、DMA、外设隐式状态及跨外设交互，从而提升固件重宿主测试对深层协议状态的可达性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固件重宿主方法通常采用被动、近似的外设模型，难以准确表达中断、内存映射I/O与DMA之间的调度关系、外设内部的隐式状态转换以及多个外设之间的依赖，因而大多只能覆盖协议初始化、广播和扫描等浅层状态。

Method: 设计RPM，将外设抽象为具有状态和反应能力的系统，并使用事件—条件—动作规则描述外设响应、状态变化及外设间交互。研究团队在Bluezz中实现RPM，面向BLE固件重宿主与模糊测试，并在NimBLE、Zephyr和Nordic SoftDevice等代表性协议栈及18个测试目标上进行评估，同时验证其在其他MCU固件上的可迁移性。

Result: 在18个目标上，Bluezz获得的平均基本块覆盖率超过现有先进重宿主方法的2.6倍。与以广播和扫描逻辑为主的既有方法相比，Bluezz能够稳定执行已建立连接后的BLE状态，并发现5个仅在连接建立后才会出现的此前未知缺陷。实验还表明，RPM可推广到BLE之外、运行于不同MCU的嵌入式固件。

Conclusion: 复杂外设语义是实现深层固件状态重宿主的关键。RPM通过显式建模外设反应性、状态性及跨外设依赖，显著增强了协议状态探索和缺陷发现能力，证明了反应式外设建模在嵌入式固件测试中的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Rehosting enables tight control and introspection for firmware testing, but existing approaches largely fail to reach deeper application states and cannot drive embedded protocol stacks beyond early-stage initialization. This limitation reflects a broader weakness in current rehosting techniques: their inability to faithfully model complex peripheral semantics and dependencies. In particular, existing work typically relies on passive approximations of peripheral behavior and overlooks three key aspects: (i) the interplay among interrupts, MMIO, and DMA; (ii) implicit state transitions within peripherals; and (iii) interactions across multiple peripherals.
  To address this challenge, we propose Reactive Peripheral Modeling (RPM), an abstraction that models hardware peripherals as reactive and stateful systems. RPM captures peripheral behavior using event-condition-action semantics, enabling faithful representation of interrupt, MMIO, and DMA scheduling, implicit state transitions, and cross-peripheral interactions. We implement RPM in Bluezz for BLE firmware rehosting and fuzzing, and show that reactive modeling is necessary to reach deep protocol states. We evaluate Bluezz on representative BLE stacks, including NimBLE, Zephyr, and Nordic SoftDevice, a closed-source commercial stack. Across 18 targets, Bluezz achieves an average basic-block coverage more than 2.6 times that of prior state-of-the-art rehosting approaches. Unlike prior approaches, which remain largely confined to advertising and scanning logic, Bluezz reliably exercises connected BLE states and uncovers five previously unknown vulnerabilities that manifest only after connection establishment. Finally, we show that RPM generalizes beyond BLE to other embedded firmware running on different MCUs.

</details>


### [202] [JITterFlip: Uncovering Fault Attack Surfaces in JIT-Compiled LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29745)
*Tairui Wang,Zhi Zhang,Yansong Gao,Xin Zhang,Qingni Shen,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: JITterFlip提出一种针对GPU云端LLM推理JIT服务控制面的位翻转攻击，通过篡改CPU侧服务决策而非模型权重或GPU计算代码，实现乱码输出和保持正确输出但显著增加延迟两类攻击效果，并通过Rowhammer验证端到端可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位翻转攻击主要针对模型参数，依赖模型特定知识；针对可执行代码的方法又通常破坏直接执行模型计算的代码，效果主要局限于推理耗尽。JIT服务的CPU侧控制面负责编译产物选择和GPU执行编排，因此可能成为新的、跨模型的攻击面。

Method: 研究提出JITterFlip及决策引导的故障易感代码分析方法，在大规模JIT编译器栈中定位会影响服务决策的脆弱代码，并对CPU驻留的分支及控制逻辑实施位翻转。方法评估了乱码输出攻击、正确输出海绵攻击，以及从CPU代码故障跨越CPU-GPU边界影响GPU推理的端到端Rowhammer场景。

Result: 在四个文本和多模态LLM工作负载上，脆弱代码故障表现出跨模型迁移性：乱码输出的困惑度比率为15.45至2.48×10^6，正确输出海绵攻击的延迟放大为2.03至181.90倍。该方法能够绕过近期LLM位翻转攻击防御，并保留两类攻击效果。端到端Rowhammer实验在四个LLM上实现了单比特翻转，最高达到7.23×10^6倍困惑度放大或124.97倍延迟放大，同时保持生成内容完全一致。

Conclusion: JITterFlip表明，JIT服务控制面是GPU云端LLM推理中独立且具有跨模型影响的安全关键面。即使不直接访问GPU内存或修改模型权重，CPU侧控制逻辑的单比特故障也可能导致输出破坏或隐蔽的性能退化，因此需要针对JIT控制面、跨CPU-GPU执行链路和故障传播机制设计专门防护。

Abstract: LLMs are widely deployed through cloud-hosted inference services, where Just-in-Time (JIT) compilation is used to reduce recurring framework and GPU-launch overhead. JIT serving introduces a host-side control plane that selects compiled artifacts and orchestrates their execution on the GPU. Meanwhile, the shared cloud setting has motivated a growing body of bit-flip attacks (BFAs) against LLM/DNN inference. Most existing BFAs target model parameters or weights and require model-specific knowledge. A smaller body of work reduces this dependency by faulting executable code, yet still corrupts code that directly implements model computation, limiting their attack effect to inference depletion.
  We present JITterFlip, the first BFA targeting the host-side JIT serving control plane of GPU-based LLM inference. By faulting CPU-resident serving decisions rather than model computation, JITterFlip enables both gibberish output generation and a correct-output sponge attack. To identify exploitable targets in a large JIT compiler stack, JITterFlip develops a decision-guided fault-vulnerable code analysis.
  Across four text and multimodal LLM workloads, the identified vulnerable code faults exhibit cross-model transferability, produce gibberish outputs with PPL ratios of $15.45\times$ to $2.48{\times}10^{6}\times$, and demonstrate correct-output sponge attacks with latency amplification of $2.03\times$ to $181.90\times$. JITterFlip also bypasses recent BFA defenses for LLMs while retaining both attack effects. Last, we demonstrate end-to-end Rowhammer attacks across four LLMs: a single bit flip in CPU-resident branch code propagates across the CPU-GPU boundary to disrupt GPU-executed inference without direct access to GPU memory, reaching up to $7.23{\times}10^{6}\times$ PPL amplification or $124.97\times$ latency amplification while preserving the exact generated output.

</details>


### [203] [Building the Truman Show: A TrustZone-Based Framework for Lightweight Out-of-band Kernel Security Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29758)
*Zhenling Duan,Pan Dong,Renshuang Jiang,Xiaoxiang Fang,Bao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LOOM是一种基于ARM TrustZone的带外操作系统监控架构，通过安全世界中的语义重构、双阶段风险防护和地址转换缓存，实现对内核级威胁的高效检测与缓解，并保持较低运行开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内核内防护机制与内核处于相同特权级，内核遭到攻陷后可能被绕过；而严格隔离方案又存在语义鸿沟，导致系统可见性不足和威胁检测延迟。

Method: LOOM利用TrustZone建立独立且抗篡改的安全监控环境，在Secure World中选择性重构进程控制块、内核模块等关键内核对象的状态与行为。系统采用原子内存保护与中断驱动自适应代理组成的双阶段防护机制，并通过地址转换缓存优化重复地址访问；整体架构分为平台层、功能层和辅助层。

Result: LOOM已在Phytium D2000平台上实现。实验结果表明，该系统具有较低、近乎可忽略的性能开销，同时保持较强的监控能力；基于CVE案例的安全能力分析显示，其能够检测并缓解多类内核级攻击。

Conclusion: 通过硬件隔离与轻量级语义重构相结合，LOOM在避免内核同级防护弱点的同时缓解了严格隔离造成的语义鸿沟，为低开销、实时的内核安全监控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The increasing number of vulnerabilities in operating systems, together with sophisticated kernel-level threats (e.g., rootkits), has weakened the effectiveness of traditional in-kernel protection mechanisms. Since these defenses operate at the same privilege level as the kernel, they share the same attack surface and can be bypassed once the kernel is compromised. Isolation-based security approaches provide stronger protection by separating security logic from the kernel, but strict isolation often introduces semantic gaps that limit system visibility and hinder timely threat detection. In this paper, we present LOOM, a lightweight out-of-band operating system monitoring architecture built on ARM TrustZone. By leveraging TrustZone's hardware-enforced isolation, LOOM establishes a tamper-resistant monitoring environment independent of the kernel. To bridge the semantic gap, we design a lightweight semantic reconstruction mechanism in the Secure World. It selectively captures the states and behavioral patterns of critical kernel objects, such as process control blocks and kernel modules. Additionally, LOOM introduces a dual-stage hazard prevention mechanism that combines atomic memory protection with an interrupt-driven adaptive agent to detect and mitigate kernel rootkit activities. An address translation cache is further incorporated to optimize repeated address access and reduce monitoring overhead. Overall, we develop a multi-layered collaborative architecture with platform, functional, and auxiliary layers for secure and efficient kernel monitoring. A prototype of LOOM has been implemented on the Phytium D2000 platform. Experimental results indicate that LOOM incurs negligible overhead while maintaining a strong monitoring capability. Furthermore, a security capability analysis based on CVE cases demonstrates that LOOM can detect and mitigate various kernel attacks.

</details>


### [204] [HSMLog: Small Language Model-Assisted Hardware Security Module Log Anomaly Detection with Behavioral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29773)
*Chia-Hsuan Wu,Dar-Hsin Dustin Wu,Rui Fang,Yi-Ting Lee,Chia-Chih Lin,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMLog：一个面向硬件安全模块日志的两阶段异常检测与分析框架。其核心特点是先由小语言模型筛选告警候选，再结合检索到的策略、历史可疑密钥记录和上下文日志进行保守复核与事件分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSM日志中的异常通常体现在事件序列、密钥、对象状态、会话及时间模式之间的关联，而非单条事件本身。现有方法往往将异常检测与面向HSM领域的证据验证、告警解释和报告相分离，导致检测结果难以获得充分、可追溯的业务规则支撑。

Method: 框架包含两个阶段：第一阶段对结构化HSM事件采用滑动时间窗，由小语言模型识别候选告警，并依据HSM专属操作规则进行策略引导评估；第二阶段检索告警窗口之前严格发生的相关策略与历史可疑密钥记录，同时引入与候选告警相关的日志上下文，用于审慎复核及事件分析。时间先后约束旨在避免使用告警发生后的信息，从而降低信息泄漏风险。

Result: 在真实工业HSM背景日志及与工业合作伙伴共同定义的异常场景上，HSMLog取得98.97%的精确率、96.00%的召回率、98.66%的异常事件覆盖率和97.46%的F1分数。结果表明，该方法在所研究数据与场景中能够较准确地生成异常告警，并支持后续的事件分诊。

Conclusion: HSMLog将语言模型驱动的候选检测、HSM规则约束和检索增强的历史证据复核结合起来，能够同时改善异常识别与告警可分析性。摘要中的高指标支持其在研究设定下的有效性，但其对不同厂商设备、未见攻击模式、日志质量变化及在线部署成本的泛化能力，仍需通过更广泛的独立测试进一步验证。

Abstract: Hardware Security Module (HSM) logs capture security-critical behavior, but anomalies emerge from relationships across event sequences, keys, object states, sessions, and temporal patterns rather than isolated events. Existing methods separate detection from HSM-specific evidence validation and reporting. In this paper, we present HSMLog, a two-stage framework for HSM log anomaly detection with retrieval-grounded behavioral analysis. In Stage 1, a small language model (SLM) identifies candidate alerts from sliding windows of structured HSM events and performs policy-guided assessment using HSM-specific operational rules. In Stage 2, retrieved policies and historical suspicious-key records strictly predating the alert window, together with candidate-related log context, support conservative candidate review and incident analysis. Evaluated on real industrial HSM background logs augmented with anomaly scenarios co-defined with industrial partners, HSMLog achieves 98.97% precision, 96.00% recall, 98.66% anomalous-event coverage, and a 97.46% F1 score, demonstrating effective anomaly alerting and incident triage in the studied setting.

</details>


### [205] [Influence Is Not Authority: When Causal Guardrail Signals Make Legitimate Tool Use Look Like an Attack in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29942)
*Tanzim Ahad,Ismail Hossain,Md Jahangir Alam,Sai Puppala,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有基于影响/因果信号的智能体护栏难以稳定区分“用户已授权且使用工具信息”的正常操作与“未授权操作”。其核心原因是：信号更敏感于行动信息的来源，而非行动是否获得授权，因而会造成正常请求被误拦截或额外核验，并削弱整体效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型智能体需要在保障授权边界的同时维持可用性。现有护栏通常依据外部信息对最终行动的因果影响进行判断，但当同一数值既可能由用户直接提供，也可能来自合法工具返回时，护栏可能将来源变化误判为风险变化。论文旨在系统检验这一“授权等价”条件下的判别失配。

Method: 作者构造了24个基础案例扩展出的96个条件，并进行授权等价审计。在配对比较中，固定授权状态、实际提交的行动及其预期效果，仅改变所需参数是由用户提供还是由合法工具结果提供。随后使用Llama与Gemma评分器评估信号变化，并设置配对的未授权对照以验证信号是否仍具有风险敏感性。最后在语义监控器和影子式护栏两种架构中测量自动通过率、拦截效果与任务效用。

Result: 在全部24个配对案例中，仅将参数从用户输入迁移为合法工具输出，就使Llama和Gemma的因果信号均向高风险区域移动，尽管行动本身及授权状态未变。该无害来源迁移造成的平均分数变化甚至大于真正改变授权状态带来的变化。架构评估中，语义监控器将不当操作成功率降至0%，但效用仅为28%；不使用该监控器时相应数值为16%和60%。影子式护栏允许所有测试中的无害运行通过，却未能更有效地阻止未授权操作：57.5%的未授权运行在进入后续安全检查前自动通过，而已授权运行的该比例为29.2%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，被考察的因果影响信号能够反映“哪些信息塑造了行动”，却不能可靠编码“该行动是否获得授权”。因此，不能将该信号直接等同于授权判定；护栏的参考样本构造、路由策略及后续核验机制共同决定实际安全性与可用性。未来设计应显式建模授权主体、授权范围和工具结果的可信来源，而非主要依赖信息来源对行动的影响强度。

Abstract: The key limitation of current state-of-the-art influence-based guardrails is that they do not reliably distinguish a legitimate, user-authorized action from a malicious, unauthorized action when both rely on external tool information. This ambiguity can cause benign actions to trigger unnecessary verification and intervention, reducing utility and adding latency. We expose this limitation through an authorization-equivalence audit of 96 conditions derived from 24 base cases. Within matched source comparisons, we hold authorization, the exact committed action, and its intended effect fixed, changing only whether a required value comes from the user or a legitimate tool result. Although the action remains unchanged, this harmless relocation shifts the causal signal toward the attack region in all 24 cases under both Llama and Gemma scorers. Matched unauthorized controls show that the signal remains attack-sensitive, yet the benign relocation produces a larger average score shift than the actual change in authorization. Architecture-level evaluation shows how this mismatch propagates through guardrail designs. With a semantic monitor, attack success is 0% and utility is 28%, compared with 16% and 60% without it. A shadow-based guardrail allows every tested harmless run, yet does not reject matched unauthorized actions more often overall: 57.5% of unauthorized runs pass automatically before reaching the later security check, compared with 29.2% of authorized runs. These results show that the studied causal signal reveals what shaped an action without reliably encoding whether the action was authorized, and that reference construction and routing are integral to the effective security decision.

</details>


### [206] [A New Algebraic Algorithm for LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29977)
*Luca Campa,Massimo Fumiani,Arnab Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种求解搜索型学习含误差问题（Search-LWE）的新代数算法，将线性代数与Groebner基计算中的S-多项式方法结合，并给出不依赖半正则性假设或正则度复杂度界的直接复杂度分析；相较已有基于Groebner基的方法，获得多项式级复杂度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Search-LWE是现代密码学中的基础计算问题。现有求解方法可分为代数、组合与格方法，其中Groebner基类代数方法的分析常依赖半正则性等启发式假设，或借助正则度推导复杂度界。论文旨在构造更高效且分析更直接的代数求解算法。

Method: 算法在代数建模框架下，将线性代数技术与Groebner基计算中的S-多项式生成和消元思想结合。作者直接分析该过程所需的计算资源，而非通过多项式系统的正则度间接估计复杂度，因此避免引入半正则性假设。

Result: 作者声称该算法对Search-LWE具有直接的复杂度上界，并且相对于此前使用Groebner基方法的结果，实现了多项式因子的复杂度降低。摘要未给出具体参数范围、复杂度公式、适用条件或实验数据。

Conclusion: 该工作为Search-LWE提供了一种改进的代数求解路径，其主要贡献是线性代数与S-多项式方法的结合，以及不依赖常见启发式结构假设的复杂度分析。其实际影响仍需结合全文中的精确渐近界、参数实例和与其他类别算法的比较进一步评估。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem, introduced by Regev in 2005, is central to modern cryptography and post-quantum security. The algorithms to solve the search version of the problem, Search-LWE, can be broadly categorised into algebraic, combinatorial and lattice-based.
  In this work we propose a new algebraic algorithm for the Search-LWE problem. At a high level, the algorithm combines linear-algebraic techniques with S-polynomial-based methods from Groebner basis computation. We provide a direct complexity analysis of our algorithm, avoiding semi-regularity assumptions and complexity bounds derived from the degree of regularity. Our algorithm achieves a polynomial improvement in complexity over prior results that use Groebner basis methods to solve Search-LWE.

</details>


### [207] [Breaking Ambient Trust: In-Network Per-Process Access Control Against Lateral Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29979)
*Osama Bajaber,Bo Ji,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出NetZone，一种面向企业网络的网络内访问控制机制。其核心是将可随网络移动而持续绑定于用户进程的AccessScope访问能力嵌入流量，使进程即使迁移到其他主机也只能访问预授权主机集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业网络防护通常以主机为信任边界，难以跨主机、按进程追踪用户的访问权限。主机失陷后，恶意进程可能继承该主机或受害用户的环境权限，导致权限范围扩大。论文旨在消除这种“主机内环境信任”，让权限约束随进程而非随主机变化。

Method: NetZone定义AccessScope抽象，表示与用户进程绑定的轻量级访问能力，其中编码该身份允许访问的主机集合。发送流量时，AccessScope被附加到网络报文；网络在报文到达目的地前验证其权限。进程跨主机迁移时，该能力随流量传播并在接收端重新绑定到对应进程，从而维持权限持久性。实现方面，系统协同使用可编程交换机与eBPF，并通过网络内优化及轻量级状态持久化支持在线检查。

Result: 摘要宣称，该设计能够在高流量条件下对嵌入式AccessScope进行线速处理，并带来可忽略的时延开销。实验评估表明，NetZone可限制复杂横向移动场景中的访问扩张，且未观察到明显性能负担。

Conclusion: NetZone将访问控制从主机级环境信任转变为随进程传播的、固定范围的能力约束。该方案的主要贡献在于AccessScope抽象、跨主机权限持久化机制，以及基于可编程数据平面和eBPF的高性能实现；其目标是降低主机失陷后权限继承与横向扩张的风险。

Abstract: Enterprise networks remain vulnerable to Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs), where adversaries gain an initial foothold and move laterally across the network, accumulating access permissions hop by hop to reach critical targets. Existing network defenses cannot track user movement at the process level across the network; instead, they grant ambient trust to all processes within a host. As a result, once a host is compromised, malicious processes inherit the victim's permissions, thereby expanding the attacker's access scope and enabling further lateral movement. To address this gap, we present NetZone, an in-network access control that confines each user process to a fixed access scope that persists as the user moves across the network. NetZone introduces a new abstraction, called AccessScope, which represents a lightweight access capability bound to the user's processes. Each AccessScope encodes the set of hosts a user identity is authorized to access and is embedded in the process's outgoing network traffic for validation before reaching its destination. As users pivot across hosts, AccessScope propagates with their traffic, rebinds to the receiving process, and persists across hosts. This ensures that regardless of network location, the user's processes are consistently governed by their bound AccessScope and their access permissions remain unchanged. To handle the high volume of network traffic generated by processes, we develop a data-plane co-design that integrates programmable switches with eBPF. NetZone employs a set of in-network optimizations and lightweight AccessScope persistence techniques to inspect the embedded AccessScope on the fly, enabling line-rate processing of high traffic volumes with negligible latency overhead. Our extensive evaluations show that NetZone can effectively defend against sophisticated attack scenarios without introducing noticeable overhead.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Object Authentication: Context-Closed Post-Quantum Authentication for the WebPKI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30004)
*Anis Bkakria*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“上下文闭包”原则，并据此设计两平面后量子WebPKI方案LRp：更新平面认证可变CA上下文，常规认证仅携带经协商选择的局部依赖引用，从而显著降低认证传输开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子迁移会提高WebPKI认证成本。仅压缩证书对象不足以保证验证方所接受的认证对象仍绑定其依赖的可变授权语义，例如CA状态、信任库视图及生命周期事件。论文旨在同时满足授权语义完整性与低带宽认证需求。

Method: 作者形式化“上下文闭包”：验证器接受的认证投影必须能够在已声明的源合约和事件覆盖见证条件下，唯一确定其声称采用的授权语义。基于该原则，LRp采用更新平面维护并认证可变CA上下文；常规路径使用显式配置协商选择状态局部依赖引用。实验基于固定版本的CCADB重建Apple、Chrome、Microsoft和Mozilla的信任视图，统计路径/视图上下文、CA谱系和依赖数量，并比较LR+选择器与无状态单签名依赖包的字节成本及生命周期交叉点。

Result: 重建数据包含44,912个路径/视图上下文和16,858条物理CA谱系。核心编译器生成的类型化依赖数中位数为6、95分位数为16、最大值为18。LR+常规选择器的大小分别为296、776和872字节；携带相同依赖向量的无状态单签名包分别为3,842、5,932和6,350字节。保留全部视图闭包状态需16.15 MB。在给定检查点和更新模型下，各视图的生命周期成本交叉点为每天19.60至50.41次中位路径常规认证。

Conclusion: LRp表明，后量子WebPKI优化不应只关注证书压缩，还必须认证影响授权决策的可变上下文。通过将上下文状态移至可更新认证平面，并在常规认证中传递少量局部依赖引用，该设计在保持授权语义可验证性的同时，大幅降低了传输开销；其实际收益取决于认证频率是否超过各视图对应的生命周期交叉点。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration increases WebPKI authentication cost, but authenticating a compressed certificate object does not by itself preserve the mutable authorization context under which a relying party accepts it. We formalize \emph{context closure}: the authenticated projection accepted by a verifier must determine the selected authorization semantics it claims, relative to declared source contracts and event-coverage witnesses. We instantiate this idea with \LRp, a two-plane post-quantum construction that authenticates mutable CA-context state in an update plane while the warm path carries only state-local dependency references selected by explicit profile negotiation.
  In a pinned CCADB reconstruction, we obtain 44,912 path/view contexts and 16,858 physical CA lineages across Apple, Chrome, Microsoft, and Mozilla views. The core compiler yields $m_{50}=6$, $m_{95}=16$, and $m_{\max}=18$ typed dependencies. A warm LR+ selector therefore costs 296, 776, and 872 bytes at median, p95, and maximum, compared with 3,842, 5,932, and 6,350 bytes for a one-signature stateless bundle carrying the same dependency vector. The retained all-view closure state is 16.15 MB, and per-view lifecycle crossovers range from 19.60 to 50.41 median-path warm authentications/day under the stated checkpoint and update model. The implementation and evaluation artifact are available at https://github.com/nserser/LR-WebPKI

</details>


### [209] [ActReal: System-Level Mobile Agents Challenge Mobile Automation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30038)
*Mingshuo Wang,Hanqing Guo,Huining Li,Yuliang Fu,Jing Xu,Chenhan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ActReal，研究具备系统级权限的移动智能体如何联合伪造触控与六轴惯性传感器（IMU）数据，以规避依赖行为轨迹、操作时序及触控—IMU物理耦合关系的自动化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端自动化检测通常假设触控输入与设备传感器信号之间具有难以伪造的物理一致性。但系统级执行器可同时影响输入注入和应用可见的传感器数据，使这一假设失效，因此需要评估此类高权限自动化能力带来的检测鲁棒性风险。

Method: ActReal将高层语义动作转换为满足任务要求的触控事件与IMU事件：一方面基于真实轨迹进行自适应以保持触控行为的自然性；另一方面利用物理约束生成与触控时间对齐的六轴IMU信号，从而模拟二者之间的联合一致性。

Result: 摘要报告的平均事件级攻击成功率为77.5%；在检测器同时使用触控和IMU信息时，成功率仍为71.1%。这表明仅依赖多模态行为特征的检测机制在高权限对手模型下仍可能存在明显脆弱性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是揭示：当自动化执行器能够共同控制输入与传感器可见数据时，触控—IMU耦合不再是可靠的真实性依据。该结果提示移动端防护应考虑可信数据来源、权限隔离、传感器链路完整性及面向系统级威胁模型的验证，而不能仅依赖行为统计或跨模态一致性检测。

Abstract: System-level mobile agents are evolving from fixed scripts into adaptive systems that continuously observe interfaces, reason, and adjust their actions, allowing automated attacks to navigate dynamic UIs and complete complex tasks. Existing applications detect automation using touch trajectories, action timing, and the physical coupling between touch and inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals. However, a privileged system-level agent executor can control both touchscreen input and application-visible sensor delivery, enabling it to jointly generate time-aligned touch and six-axis IMU signals and evade these defenses. We present ActReal, a physical-action attack framework for system-level mobile agents. ActReal converts semantic agent actions into task-valid touch and IMU events using genuine-trajectory adaptation and physics-guided IMU generation. ActReal achieves a mean event-level attack success rate of 77.5\%; even when detectors jointly observe touch and IMU, its attack success rate remains 71.1\%.

</details>


### [210] [Reachability-Based Capability Confinement for LLM Agents under Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30041)
*Wujie Xiong,Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Weidong Shi,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillGuard是一种面向大语言模型智能体的运行时安全框架，将外部技能返回的不可信数据视为状态污染，并据此动态限制后续能力；在多项基准和组合攻击测试中显著降低攻击成功率，同时保持较高的正常功能可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要依赖不可信内容分类或对拟执行操作进行授权，未直接解决不可信数据进入智能体状态后应如何调整其未来权限的问题。SkillGuard旨在从执行状态层面切断污染状态与部署者定义的禁止状态之间的联系。

Method: 在假设技能摘要和安全策略可靠的前提下，SkillGuard构建技能影响图表示安全相关状态转移，利用可操控性签名描述技能参数的控制范围，并通过内联引用监控器拦截技能调用。检测到污染后，采用二值、分数或分数流策略计算能力限制，且无需额外语言模型推理。实验覆盖四个AgentDojo套件、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Llama3.3-70B两个后端，并与无防御基线及Spotlighting、CaMeL、AttriGuard进行比较；同时构造由多个单独不足以造成违规的观察组合而成的组合攻击基准。

Result: 在AgentDojo工具知识攻击中，SkillGuard在两个后端上均使四个套件中的三个达到零攻击成功率，并将Slack套件的成功率降至Gemini的4.8%和Llama的14.3%。在组合攻击中，SkillGuard在Llama上优于所有基线，在Gemini上达到最强基线水平且具有更高正常功能效用。相同攻击成功率下，分数流限制比二值限制保留更多能力；两类实验中均未增加模型调用或令牌开销。

Conclusion: SkillGuard证明了基于状态污染和运行时能力降级的防御范式能够有效保护智能体免受不可信技能输出的后续影响。分数流策略在安全性与可用性之间取得更优平衡，并以零额外模型推理成本实现了较强的泛化防护效果。

Abstract: Large language model agents place outputs from external skills into their execution context, allowing attacker-controlled data to influence later privileged actions. Existing defenses mainly classify untrusted content or authorize proposed operations. They do not directly address how an agent's future authority should change once untrusted data enters its state. We present SkillGuard, a harness-level enforcement layer that treats this event as contamination and restricts future capabilities to disconnect the resulting state from deployer-defined forbidden states. Given sound skill summaries and policies, SkillGuard represents security-relevant transitions with a Skill Impact Graph, specifies admissible control over skill parameters via steerability signatures, and mediates invocations with an inline reference monitor. Following contamination, it computes weighted capability restrictions using binary, fractional, or fractional-flow strategies without auxiliary language-model inference. We evaluate SkillGuard on four AgentDojo suites with two backend LLMs, Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama3.3-70B, against an LLM-only No Defense baseline and three defenses at different system layers: Spotlighting, CaMeL, and AttriGuard. We construct a compositional attack benchmark in which each attack combines observations individually insufficient to induce target violation and evaluate the same baselines on it. Under AgentDojo's Tool Knowledge attacks, SkillGuard eliminates attack success on three of four suites for both backends and reduces it to 4.8% and 14.3% on Slack. Against compositional attacks, it outperforms every baseline on Llama and matches the strongest baseline on Gemini at higher benign utility. Fractional-flow restriction preserves substantially more capabilities than binary restriction at the same attack success rate. Across both settings, SkillGuard adds no model calls or token overhead.

</details>


### [211] [Zero-Knowledge Predicate Proofs Between AI Agents: A Measured, Cross-Protocol Gateway and the Source-Integrity Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30083)
*Ashok Subbabhatta Gopalakrishna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出零知识证明网关（Zero-Knowledge Proof Gateway），使多智能体仅交换针对私有数据的合规性证明，而非原始数据或自然语言声明；系统扩展了MCP与Agent2Agent协议，并结合可信执行环境证明，增强了证明与数据来源之间的绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体间信任机制主要依赖原始数据共享或自然语言自我声明：前者造成过度暴露，后者不可验证且容易成为提示注入攻击入口。既有方案偏重身份、可观测性和事后检测，缺少可在真实运行环境中执行、能够从设计上实现数据最小化的机制。此外，普通零知识证明只能证明承诺值满足谓词，无法证明该值确实来自权威系统记录。

Method: 作者设计并实现零知识证明网关，允许治理方定义私有数据上的谓词，数据持有方生成零知识证明，接收方只验证证明而不接触原始值。系统以32位阈值谓词为示例，采用Bulletproofs生成紧凑证明；同时提出协议扩展槽位，并以单一端点支持MCP和Agent2Agent两种互操作协议。针对数据来源绑定问题，论文将可信执行环境中的测量二进制远程证明与零知识证明双向融合，使单个验证工件同时证明谓词成立及数据由特定受测量程序读取。系统部署于Kubernetes，并通过对抗实验、协议检查、网络隔离验证及模拟权威机构测试进行评估。

Result: 在单个通用vCPU上，32位阈值证明的生成时间为6.2毫秒，验证时间为1.0毫秒，Bulletproofs证明大小为608字节。十一项对抗性实验和十九项协议检查均通过；Kubernetes部署中的网络隔离得到经验性验证。零售案例表明，客户端订单可证明未超过限额，而无需披露订单金额。融合可信执行环境证明后，系统能够将谓词真实性与特定测量程序的数据读取行为联合认证，但该部分在模拟权威机构环境中测试。

Conclusion: 该工作将多智能体数据最小化从概念性提案推进到可运行的协议与系统实现：通过零知识证明替代原始数据传输和自然语言合规声明，可降低隐私泄露及不可验证输入带来的风险。其主要贡献包括跨协议集成、可量化的低延迟性能，以及利用可信执行环境缓解证明与系统记录脱节的问题。仍需进一步评估真实权威数据源、复杂谓词、密钥与证明生命周期管理，以及大规模多智能体环境下的性能和运维成本。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI platforms move quickly from staging to production, but the way agents establish trust remains rudimentary: an agent either transmits raw data to a peer or accepts that peer's natural-language self-report that a value complies with policy. The first over-shares; the second is unverifiable and is exactly the channel prompt injection attacks. Prevailing responses emphasise identity, visibility, and post-hoc detection, and recent proposals for cryptographically enforced agent policy have been evaluated in simulation rather than execution. We take provable data minimisation between agents from proposal to running system. In our Zero-Knowledge Proof Gateway, agents exchange proofs of governance-defined predicates over private data rather than the data itself, so exposure is prevented by design rather than detected afterwards; because no interoperability protocol can carry such a proof, we propose a slot and implement it on both MCP and Agent2Agent from one endpoint. A 32-bit threshold predicate proves in 6.2 ms and verifies in 1.0 ms with a 608-byte Bulletproofs proof on one commodity vCPU; eleven adversarial experiments and nineteen protocol checks pass; and the system is deployed to Kubernetes with empirically verified network isolation. Our case study proves a retail client order is within its limit without revealing the amount, instantiating the GDPR data-minimisation principle as an enforced technical measure of the kind EU law now names explicitly. We then address the limitation no comparable work resolves: a predicate proof binds a statement to a committed value, never to the system of record. We give a construction fusing an enclave attestation with the proof in both directions, so verifying one artifact certifies jointly that the predicate holds and that the value was read by a specific measured binary, and test it against a mock authority.

</details>


### [212] [A Simple Transformer Pipeline for Full-Key Side-Channel Attacks on Uncropped Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30105)
*Jimmy Gammell,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向未裁剪功耗轨迹的全密钥侧信道分析Transformer基线，实现同时攻击完整密钥，并公开代码、训练方案与预训练权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有深度学习侧信道分析多聚焦单字节目标和人工裁剪轨迹，可能丢失可利用泄漏；现有解决方案通常依赖专用架构或重采样，缺少结构简单、可复现的全密钥未裁剪轨迹Transformer基线。

Method: 采用标准Transformer编码器作为主干，仅针对侧信道任务调整输入层和输出层；面向ASCADv1f、ASCADv1r和CHES-CTF-2018等数据集进行未裁剪全密钥攻击，并公开实现、训练配方及预训练模型。

Result: 在上述数据集上取得与已有方法具有竞争力的性能；显存占用低于10GB，使用单张NVIDIA A6000时最长训练时间为3.34小时，体现出较好的资源效率与可复现性。

Conclusion: 标准Transformer无需复杂专用改造即可作为未裁剪全密钥侧信道分析的有效基线，为后续研究提供了开源、低资源且易复现的实现。

Abstract: Deep learning-based side-channel analysis has historically focused on single-byte targets and manually cropped traces, which risks discarding exploitable leakage. While recent work has proposed specialized architectures and resampling techniques to address this gap, the literature lacks a simple transformer baseline for simultaneous full-key attacks on uncropped traces. We present an open-source transformer implementation for uncropped full-key attacks which uses the standard transformer encoder backbone, adapting only the input and output layers to the side-channel setting. We release our implementation, training recipes, and pretrained weights for uncropped ASCADv1f, ASCADv1r, and CHES-CTF-2018 which achieve performance competitive with previously-reported results, while using less than 10GB of VRAM and requiring at most 3.34 hours of training on a single NVIDIA A6000.

</details>


### [213] [Lie to Me: Finding Bugs in ZK DSL Toolchains with Adversarial Witness Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30648)
*Sebastian Watzinger,Christoph Hochrainer,Valentin Wüstholz,Maria Christakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Liezz 是一种面向零知识领域特定语言（ZK DSL）工具链的测试框架，通过注入并拼接来自不同执行的见证值，构造必然无效的见证，从而检测约束系统未充分表达源程序语义的健全性漏洞。它支持 Circom、Corset、Gnark 和 Noir，共发现 13 个漏洞，其中 7 个具有健全性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有效执行测试只能验证正确程序和见证能否通过，难以发现“有效执行均正常、无效执行却可被接受”的约束缺失问题。ZK DSL 编译器、见证生成器及标准库中的此类漏洞可能导致系统接受无效证明，因此需要专门针对无效见证的测试方法。

Method: Liezz 自动生成确定性 ZK DSL 程序，并为同一程序执行两组具有不同输出的公开输入。它将一次执行的输入与另一次执行的输出及见证进行拼接，构造按程序语义必然无效的见证；正确工具链应拒绝该见证，若接受则表明存在健全性漏洞。框架通过受控输出差异、多种见证拼接策略及参数化标准库调用，提高对缺失约束和复杂功能的覆盖能力。

Result: Liezz 在 Circom、Corset、Gnark 和 Noir 中发现 13 个漏洞，其中 7 个影响约束系统的健全性；部分漏洞只能通过生成的标准库调用触发。在相同测试预算下，有效执行测试基线未发现任何由注入见证揭示的健全性漏洞，说明对抗性见证注入能够覆盖传统测试遗漏的问题。

Conclusion: 对抗性见证注入是测试 ZK DSL 工具链健全性的有效方法。通过系统构造无效但结构上具有一定一致性的见证，Liezz 能直接检验工具链是否拒绝语义不合法的执行，并显著补充有效执行测试，尤其适合发现编译器、约束生成器和标准库实现中的缺失约束漏洞。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge domain-specific language (ZK DSL) toolchains compile programs into constraint systems and generate witnesses for cryptographic proofs. Bugs in these toolchains can leave the enforced constraints weaker than the source-program semantics, admitting proofs for invalid executions. Such soundness bugs may remain invisible to valid-execution testing because all valid executions still behave correctly.
  We present Liezz, a testing framework that generates ZK DSL programs and exposes these bugs through adversarial witness injection. For each generated deterministic program, Liezz executes two public input assignments with different outputs and splices their witnesses, combining the input of one execution with the output of the other. The resulting witness is invalid by construction. A correct toolchain must reject it; acceptance exposes a soundness bug. Controlled divergence and multiple witness-splicing strategies preserve enough consistency to expose missing constraints. Liezz also generates parameterized standard-library calls to reach complex functionality.
  Liezz supports Circom, Corset, Gnark, and Noir. It finds 13 bugs, including seven with soundness impact. Several are reachable only through generated standard-library calls. Under the same testing budget, a valid-execution baseline does not expose any of the soundness failures revealed by accepted injected witnesses, showing that adversarial witness injection reaches failures missed by valid-execution testing.

</details>


### [214] [Drishti: AI-Led Human-Directed Vulnerability Auditing for 5G Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30112)
*Sriram Ramachandran,Levente Csikor,Dinil Mon Divakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Drishti，一种由AI主导、人类指导的漏洞审计框架，针对验证、可达性、影响评估和修复完整性四类验证成本，应用于Open5GS和free5GC后发现三项安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件漏洞候选的生成成本已显著降低，但漏洞真实性、可触达性、实际影响及修复完整性的验证仍然稀缺且昂贵，尤其是在5G核心网等关键基础设施软件中。

Method: Drishti包含四个与验证成本对应的组件：用于确认缺陷模式的反模式目录、用于判断攻击路径的关键路径分诊、用于分层评估影响的同心验证，以及用于检查补丁充分性的补丁审查。框架采用AI辅助分析与人工专家指导相结合的方式，审计Open5GS和free5GC。

Result: 共发现三项问题：Open5GS NRF多部分解析器存在预认证NULL解引用，已由上游修复并申请CVE；free5GC NGAP解码器存在ASN.1-PER内存放大问题，恶意2字节输入可在6.2秒内耗尽AMF内存；针对CVE-2025-69248的补丁包含位于认证前、实际不可执行的纵深防御检查。

Conclusion: Drishti表明，漏洞审计的关键瓶颈已从候选生成转向系统化验证与影响评估。其四阶段框架能够识别可利用缺陷、量化实际影响并发现不完整或无效的修复，适合用于高风险网络基础设施的软件安全审计。

Abstract: Candidate generation for open-source vulnerabilities is no longer scarce. AI-assisted code review now produces defect candidates cheaply, and industry programs pair them with expert human triage. The remaining scarcity is validation and impact assessment, and the gap is largest in critical-infrastructure software like 5G cores. Here, validation has four costs: verification, reachability, impact, and fix-completeness. We present Drishti, an AI-led human-directed vulnerability audit framework with four components, one per cost: (i) an anti-pattern catalog for verification, (ii) critical-path triage for reachability, (iii) concentric validation for impact, and (iv) patch-review for fix-completeness. Across audits of Open5GS and free5GC, Drishti produced three findings. The first is a pre-authentication NULL-dereference in the Open5GS NRF multipart parser, fixed upstream with a CVE requested. The second is an ASN.1-PER memory amplification in the free5GC NGAP decoder. A 2-byte input from a rogue gNodeB OOM-kills the AMF in 6.2 seconds. The third is a defective patch on CVE-2025-69248 whose defense-in-depth check is dead code before authentication.

</details>


### [215] [Balancing Privacy, Utility, and Safety in LLM Alignment through Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30141)
*Dishu Yang,Jingjing Liu,Jize Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究在直接偏好优化（DPO）中混入合成隐私偏好样本，是否能降低模型记忆训练数据的信号。结果显示，所提出的P3M数据配比协议在测试条件下通常降低了金丝雀样本记忆代理指标，并在2B模型的混合来源评测中显著降低总体成员推断可区分性；但效果并非对所有数据来源均一致，且不构成形式化隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化常用于使大语言模型符合人类偏好，但偏好数据的组成可能影响模型对训练内容的记忆及由此产生的隐私风险。现有方案往往需要改变训练目标或引入专门隐私机制；本文希望检验一种更轻量的途径：仅通过调整DPO训练中隐私偏好数据的比例，能否改善隐私相关指标，同时观察其对有用性与安全性偏好的影响。

Method: 作者提出Privacy-Pressure Preference Mixing（P3M）：固定有用性与无害性偏好数据，仅改变合成隐私偏好对的混入量。实验设置包括无隐私混入的基线，以及隐私混入比例为0.5、1.0和2.0的三种条件。作者在Gemma 3 270M-IT上以5个随机种子评测四种条件，并在4比特量化的Gemma 2 2B-IT上以3个随机种子复现实验。评估指标包括金丝雀后缀对数似然记忆代理、成员推断攻击的AUROC与AUPRC，以及有用性和无害性偏好准确率。

Result: 在两个模型设置中，加入隐私偏好数据总体上与更低的平均金丝雀后缀对数似然代理值相关。在2B模型的混合来源评测中，隐私混入配置的成员推断性能低于基线：隐私配置的平均AUROC为0.596至0.629、平均AUPRC为0.541至0.575，而基线分别为0.804和0.790，表明成员身份的可区分性明显下降。但这种下降在不同数据来源上并不稳定。效用方面，有用性偏好准确率整体较稳定；无害性偏好准确率与隐私混入比例的关系则随模型设置而变化。

Conclusion: P3M表明，偏好数据配比本身可能是影响记忆与成员推断风险的实证因素，并可作为研究隐私、效用和安全性权衡的低成本实验协议。不过，结论仅限于所测试的模型、数据和指标；P3M不改变DPO目标，也未提供可证明的隐私性质，因此不能被解释为正式隐私保证或针对数据提取风险的通用防御措施。

Abstract: Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models with human preferences, but preference-data composition may also influence privacy-relevant memorization. We examine whether adding synthetic privacy-preference pairs to Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is associated with lower canary-based memorization signals without modifying the objective or introducing a formal privacy mechanism. We propose Privacy-Pressure Preference Mixing (P3M), a data-composition protocol that varies the amount of privacy-preference data while keeping helpfulness and harmlessness preference data fixed. We evaluate a non-privacy Baseline and privacy-mixing ratios of 0.5, 1.0, and 2.0 using Gemma 3 270M-IT across five random seeds and validate the same four conditions using 4-bit-quantized Gemma 2 2B-IT across three seeds. Overall, under the tested conditions, privacy-preference mixing is associated with lower mean canary suffix log-likelihood proxy values across both model settings and lower aggregate membership-inference attack performance relative to the Baseline in the mixed-source 2B evaluation. Specifically, across the privacy-aware 2B configurations, the mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) ranges from 0.596 to 0.629, and the mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) ranges from 0.541 to 0.575, compared with 0.804 and 0.790, respectively, for the Baseline. However, the reduction in membership distinguishability does not hold uniformly across data sources. Moreover, the relationship between the privacy ratio and harmlessness preference accuracy varies by model setting, whereas helpfulness preference accuracy remains broadly stable. These findings suggest that P3M should be viewed as a lightweight empirical protocol for examining privacy-utility-safety trade-offs rather than as a formal privacy guarantee or a defense against extraction attacks.

</details>


### [216] [The Exclusion Ratchet: False-Positive Suppression Accumulates and Persists in Detection Rule Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31062)
*Sudaroli Dhananjeyan,Kumaran U*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究检测规则维护中的“排除条件”累积现象：维护者为减少误报持续添加排除项，但这些收窄规则覆盖范围的修改很少被撤销，且现有基于结构差异的方法会漏检相当一部分收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有纵向研究已表明规则维护并不收敛，但主要考察后来被回退的修改，无法衡量那些长期存在、从未撤销的覆盖范围收窄。论文旨在量化此类“不可见且持久”的规则收窄，并判断其是否按潜在影响进行优先治理。

Method: 作者分析SigmaHQ语料库九年间的8,234次修订，以语义方式识别收窄：若处于否定条件下的谓词集合增加，而规则覆盖范围没有相应扩大，则判为抑制性修改。该检测器为确定性规则，不依赖训练数据，定义和代码均公开。作者通过盲法人工标注验证检测效果，并使用Kaplan-Meier方法估计排除项的持续时间；同时比较排除项是否影响某一ATT&CK技术唯一覆盖规则，以检验维护是否考虑覆盖后果。

Result: 语义检测在人工标注验证中达到0.828精确率和0.911召回率。研究发现排除项被新增1,642次、撤回304次，新增与撤回之比为5.4:1；在单条规则层面该比率升至13:1。31%的收窄无法通过结构比较发现，说明现有结构性研究存在系统性低估。Kaplan-Meier估计显示，三年后仍有效的排除项比例为86.7%。排除项是否作用于某技术的唯一覆盖规则，与其持久性无显著关系（p=0.49）。在路径相关排除项中，64.1%可由仅能选择文件名的非特权进程满足。

Conclusion: 规则维护中的排除条件呈持续累积且极少回访的特征，并未依据覆盖损失或后果进行有效分级处理。论文的主要贡献是提出并验证了可复现的语义收窄检测方法，揭示结构比较遗漏的问题，并给出用于确定排除项审查优先级的判定准则。

Abstract: When a rule produces too many false alarms an analyst adds an exclusion, and the rule thereafter declines to alert in that circumstance. Each such decision is locally reasonable; what becomes of them collectively is not known. Recent longitudinal work established that curation does not converge, but measured restoration time only for revisions that were later reverted -- a measure silent about narrowing that is never undone. We measure that. Across nine years and 8,234 revisions of the SigmaHQ corpus we detect suppression semantically -- growth in the set of predicates held under negation without compensating growth in coverage -- and validate it against blinded hand labelling (precision 0.828, recall 0.911). The test is deterministic: nothing is learned from the data, and the definitions are released as code. Exclusions were added 1,642 times and withdrawn 304, a ratio of 5.4 to 1 that rises to 13 to 1 at the level of the individual rule. Thirty-one per cent of the narrowing is invisible to structural comparison, which existing structural accounts therefore undercount. Estimated by Kaplan-Meier, 86.7 per cent of exclusions remain in force three years on, and persistence is independent of whether the rule is the only coverage for its ATT&CK technique (p = 0.49). Of path-valued exclusions, 64.1 per cent can be satisfied by an unprivileged process that chooses a filename. Narrowing accumulates, is rarely revisited, and is not triaged by consequence. We give a criterion for deciding which exclusions to examine first.

</details>


### [217] [Reducio: Optimized Confidential Serverless Cloud Deployments for Enterprise Customers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30171)
*Vikram Ramaswamy,Chuqi Zhang,Adil Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Reducio 是一种基于机密虚拟机（CVM）的无服务器平台，通过内核降权实现函数隔离，并结合分层缓存降低基础设施改造需求和平台内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 CVM 的无服务器方案虽能兼顾隐私与低延迟，但通常需要较大的基础设施改造和平台内存开销，因此需要一种部署要求更低、资源效率更高的设计。

Method: 系统采用两个核心组件：一是在单个 CVM 内利用内核降权功能实现函数隔离，从而减少对底层基础设施的依赖；二是设计分层缓存方法与算法，在较小的内存函数缓存中提高缓存利用率并降低函数内存消耗。

Result: 评估结果表明，Reducio 能显著降低平台部署所需的基础设施要求，并减少函数运行过程中的内存消耗。摘要未提供具体实验数据或对比基准。

Conclusion: Reducio 证明了在无需改造底层基础设施的情况下，通过 CVM 内函数隔离与分层缓存，可以同时降低平台部署复杂度和内存开销，为隐私保护型低延迟无服务器计算提供了更实用的实现路径。

Abstract: Serverless platforms based on Confidential Virtual Machines (CVMs) have been recently proposed to address the privacy problems with serverless functions, while achieving low latency. Unfortunately, our study indicates that to achieve these properties, existing proposals impose non-trivial requirements in terms of infrastructure changes and platform memory. Reducio is an alternate serverless platform design that does not require infrastructure changes and significantly reduces platform memory requirements. The platform is designed using two key components: (1) a function isolation framework inside a CVM based on kernel deprivileging features that minimize infrastructure requirements, and (2) a layer-wise caching methodology and algorithm that effectively uses a small in-memory function cache. Our evaluation indicates that Reducio can significantly reduce both platform requirements for deployment and function memory consumption.

</details>


### [218] [Understanding Stage-Wise Utility-Risk Trade-offs in LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30177)
*Chuanchao Zang,Zijian Cao,Xiangtao Meng,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xinyu Gao,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MemGauge，一个可控评估框架，用于分阶段分析大语言模型智能体长期记忆的效用与记忆投毒风险之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估通常将记忆效用和攻击风险分开测量，且多采用固定系统配置，因而难以揭示写入、管理和检索等不同记忆操作如何分别改变二者的权衡关系。

Method: MemGauge在干净与投毒条件匹配的设置下，分别控制三类机制：写入准入策略、记忆管理策略和检索暴露程度。研究在11个大语言模型及两个长期记忆基准上进行受控实验，并将类似的阶段级测量应用于4个已有记忆系统。

Result: 实验发现三种阶段特征：写入阶段存在类似阈值的风险跃迁；管理阶段的效用与风险关系依赖具体策略，并可出现局部解耦；检索阶段则表现为效用和风险同步增长。对既有系统的测量也观察到与这些特征定性一致的诊断关联。

Conclusion: 长期记忆的投毒风险并非在所有操作阶段均匀产生，而是强烈依赖具体的记忆机制与设计选择。论文因此主张采用面向阶段的评估与控制方法，以更精确地管理长期记忆带来的效用和安全风险。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core capability of LLM agents, enabling personalization and long-horizon interaction. However, memory mechanisms that retain, transform, or expose more information can affect both benign utility and susceptibility to memory poisoning. Existing evaluations typically measure memory utility or attack risk in isolation under fixed configurations, providing limited insight into how stage-specific design choices reshape their trade-off. We present \textsc{MemGauge}, a controllable framework that separately varies writing admission, management policy, and retrieval exposure under matched clean and poisoned conditions. Across 11 LLMs and two long-term memory benchmarks, controlled evaluations reveal three distinct profiles: a threshold-like risk transition during writing, policy-dependent local decoupling during management, and coupled growth of utility and risk during retrieval. We further apply analogous stage-level measurements to four existing memory systems and observe diagnostic associations qualitatively consistent with these profiles. These results show that targeted poisoning risk varies across memory operations and motivate stage-aware evaluation and control of LLM-agent memory.

</details>


### [219] [SIR: Self-improving Red-teaming for Compute Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30207)
*Chen Xiong,Zhiyuan He,Pin-Yu Chen,Stjepan Picek,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SIR，一种面向计算机使用智能体（CUA）的黑盒间接提示注入攻击评测方法。其通过组合可复用的隐蔽注入原则，并基于失败轨迹进行迭代反馈，显著提高攻击成功率，同时保持正常任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUA能够读取不受信任的屏幕内容并操作真实操作系统，因此容易受到间接提示注入影响。现有安全基准主要依赖人工编写、固定不变的注入样本，难以刻画会根据受害智能体行为持续调整策略的攻击风险，可能低估实际威胁。

Method: SIR采用黑盒攻击设置：首先从小型、以自然语言描述的可复用原则库中组合隐蔽注入内容；随后利用迭代反馈分析智能体失败或未被绕过的执行轨迹，将有效绕过方式提炼为具名策略，并跨任务重复应用。评测对象为操作系统层面的CUA，攻击是否成功由确定性状态检查判定，包括文件系统、服务与权限状态，而非依赖大模型裁判。

Result: 在三种前沿CUA上的实验表明，结合原则组合与反馈迭代的方法优于人工编写的固定基线。例如，Claude Opus 4.8上的攻击成功率由4%提升至24%，Gemini 3.5 Flash上由0%提升至28%；同时，正常任务仍可完成。此外，针对一种模型发现的攻击原则能够迁移到不同架构的模型，无需额外反馈。

Conclusion: 论文说明，针对CUA的间接提示注入风险不仅取决于单个固定样本，更受攻击策略的自适应组合与跨任务积累影响。确定性系统状态判定提供了比语言模型裁判更可复现的评测基础。研究结果表明，CUA安全评估应纳入自适应、可迁移的注入策略，并强化对不可信界面内容、权限操作和系统状态变更的防护。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) are vision-language models that perceive a screen and act on a real operating system through mouse, keyboard, and terminal, and they are increasingly deployed to automate everyday digital tasks. Because they can be exposed to untrusted content while operating, they are vulnerable to indirect prompt injection (IPI), in which an adversary plants instructions in content the agent will read and redirects it toward actions that violate the user's intent. Existing CUA safety benchmarks evaluate fixed injections written by hand, which may underestimate the risk posed by an adaptive adversary. We present SIR, a black box IPI attack that (i) composes stealthy injections from a small library of reusable principles stated in plain language and (ii) wraps composition in an iterative feedback loop that diagnoses the victim's failed trajectories and distills the bypasses into new, named strategies that are reapplied across tasks. Unlike prior red teaming of web agents, we target CUAs at the operating system level and score attacks with a fully deterministic oracle, using checks on filesystem, service, and permission state rather than an LLM judge. On experiment, we evaluate three frontier CUAs. Composing principles with feedback raises the attack success rate over a baseline written by hand, for example from 4% to 24% on Claude Opus 4.8 and from 0% to 28% on Gemini 3.5 Flash, while the benign task still completes. Principles discovered against one model further transfer to a different architecture with no additional feedback.

</details>


### [220] [Extracting Knowledge from Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30288)
*Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Xinyu Gao,Yingkai Dong,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型代理调用知识工具时的“工具介导知识提取”风险：攻击者可仅通过查询和观察输出，逐步恢复目标工具背后的知识源。作者提出ToolSiphon，在多类工具、多个领域数据集和真实代理平台上表现出较高的记录恢复率，表明现有工具集成架构存在显著的数据泄露隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识库、文件系统、数据库和搜索索引经由工具调用接入代理后，代理能够提供更强的领域服务，但其输出也可能成为还原底层数据的渠道。该问题相较直接访问接口更复杂，主要因为代理可能错误选择其他工具，以及代理生成的工具参数会压缩或遗漏细粒度查询信息，降低针对目标知识源的提取效率。

Method: ToolSiphon是一种仅依赖查询与响应的知识提取方法，由两类互补信号构成：其一，工具对比分析通过构造目标工具与竞争工具之间的可区分线索，引导代理更可能调用目标工具，从而缓解工具选择不确定性；其二，证据链式反馈利用已获得响应中的事实信息迭代生成后续查询，减少参数压缩造成的信息损失，并逐步扩展已覆盖的知识记录。论文在三类知识型工具、六个领域数据集及三个真实代理平台上评估该方法，并考察其面对代表性防御机制时的有效性。

Result: 在已知非目标工具粗粒度信息的条件下，ToolSiphon平均恢复74.3%的源记录，文本恢复率为83.2%，语义相似度为90.2%。即使缺乏非目标工具信息，平均记录恢复率仍达到66.3%。实验还显示，该方法对若干代表性防御措施仍具有较强适应性，并可迁移至真实世界代理平台。

Conclusion: 论文指出，代理式工具调用并不只是访问控制问题，还会因工具路由、参数生成和自然语言响应形成可被迭代利用的数据外泄通道。ToolSiphon证明了攻击者能够在不直接访问底层知识源的情况下实现较高覆盖度恢复。该工作强调防护应同时关注工具选择隔离、查询与响应审计、输出最小化、敏感内容粒度控制及针对迭代式信息收集的检测，而非仅限制单次工具调用权限。

Abstract: LLM agents commonly use knowledge-based tools and access their underlying files, databases, and search indexes through tool invocation. This integration improves agents' ability to provide domain-specific services but also introduces the risk of tool-mediated knowledge extraction: source content exposed to an agent for legitimate responses may be progressively recovered from its outputs, enabling reconstruction of the knowledge source behind a target tool. This paper systematically investigates this risk and identifies two challenges introduced by tool invocation: tool-selection uncertainty, where an agent may invoke a competing tool instead of the target tool, and tool-argument compression, where fine-grained query information may be lost when the agent generates tool arguments. To tackle these challenges, we propose ToolSiphon, a query-only extraction attack that introduces two complementary signals: a target-discriminative signal, implemented through Tool Contrastive Analysis, to steer queries toward the target tool; and a response-grounded factual signal, implemented through Evidence Chained Feedback, to mitigate argument compression and progressively expand extraction coverage. Across three types of knowledge-based tools and six domain-specific datasets, ToolSiphon recovers 74.3% of source records on average when coarse-grained information about non-target tools is available, with 83.2% textual recovery and 90.2% semantic similarity. Even without such information, it recovers 66.3% of source records. ToolSiphon also remains effective against representative defenses and on three real-world agent platforms.

</details>


### [221] [A Roadmap to Available ICS Datasets and Testbeds for Cybersecurity Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30332)
*Ebtesam J. Alqahtani,Mohammad Hammoudeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理工业控制系统网络安全数据集、测试床和数字孪生，分析其架构、特征、能力及优缺点，并总结现存研究空白与发展建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统在信息技术与运营技术融合及工业4.0推动下，连接性和攻击面不断扩大。然而，数据集与实验环境不足，限制了入侵检测、异常检测、威胁情报、攻击仿真和韧性评估等研究及方案验证。

Method: 建立数据集、测试床和数字孪生的分类体系，系统分析其架构、数据特征、功能能力、适用场景、优势与局限，并据此评估现有资源对工业控制系统网络安全研究和人工智能方案验证的支持程度。

Result: 研究发现，现有资源普遍存在缺乏标准化基准数据集、现代化场景不足、基于真实运行流量的数据集数量有限，以及人工智能驱动的网络安全方案难以充分验证等问题。

Conclusion: 工业控制系统网络安全研究亟需建设更标准化、真实化、多样化且可复现的数据集与实验环境，并完善评测基准和验证机制，以支持新型安全方案的可靠比较与实际部署。

Abstract: Industrial Control Systems (ICS) are the backbone of many critical infrastructure sectors; however, their growing level of connectivity, long lifespan and integration with the Information Technology (IT) environment introduces numerous cybersecurity challenges. The merging of Operational Technology (OT) and IT along with the deployment of Industry 4.0 technologies increases the attack surface of ICS environments, which in turn makes them more vulnerable to advanced cyber threats. Therefore, many researchers have shown interest in the field of cybersecurity of ICS. The topics of intrusion detection, anomaly detection, threat intelligence, attack simulation and resilience assessment of ICS have received much attention. Nevertheless, the development and testing of cybersecurity solutions for ICS remains to be challenging due to the lack of appropriate datasets and experimental environment. The main objective of this paper is to provide the roadmap of existing ICS cybersecurity datasets, testbeds and digital twins. This paper presents various taxonomies along with systematic analysis of architecture, characteristics, capabilities, pros and cons of these tools. The results of the analysis demonstrate the presence of persistent problems such as lack of standardized benchmarking datasets, lack of modern attack scenarios, insufficient number of datasets based on real operational traffic and difficulty in validating artificial intelligence-driven cybersecurity solutions. In addition to summarizing current research on ICS cybersecurity datasets and testbeds, this roadmap provides the identification of research gaps and recommendations on creation of new tools.

</details>


### [222] [KORD: Breaking the Key-Generation Bottleneck in Dealerless FSS via Protocol--Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30379)
*Yijing Peng,Lin Liu,Yujie Xue,Shaojing Fu,Shaoqing Li,Yaohua Wang,Rongmao Chen,Yang Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出KORD，一种面向无可信密钥分发方函数秘密共享（FSS）密钥生成的协议—硬件协同设计。其通过专用芯片间的双向认证建立受保护执行边界，在该边界内重构FSS密钥，将既有无分发方方案中随GGM树深度增长的多轮交互压缩为单轮，并以跨密钥调度提升硬件吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FSS是隐私计算的重要基础组件，但传统方式依赖可信密钥分发方为每次调用生成新密钥，扩大了信任边界。现有去中心化生成协议虽移除了该依赖，却具有高通信量和线性增长的交互轮数，使密钥生成成为实际部署及端到端推理中的主要瓶颈。

Method: KORD使用一对专用芯片，通过双向认证建立共同信任根，并在该受保护边界内完成FSS密钥重构，从而避免外部可信分发方。协议层面利用该边界重组密钥生成流程，使交互轮数与GGM树深度解耦并降为单轮；硬件层面采用跨密钥调度，交错执行多个独立GGM树遍历以减少流水线空泡并提高AES计算单元利用率。

Result: 相较于当前分布式FSS协议，KORD在多种FSS构件上的单次密钥生成通信量降低7,633至70,274倍。后布局布线评估显示，在204 MHz、21.5K LUTs条件下，设计可达到每秒1,275万对32位DPF密钥的生成吞吐率，AES通道利用率为99.8%。在私有ResNet-18推理中，密钥生成占端到端时间的比例由超过96%降至11.9%。

Conclusion: KORD表明，通过将受认证专用硬件提供的受保护执行边界与协议重构、硬件调度联合设计，可以显著缓解无可信密钥分发方FSS的通信、交互和吞吐瓶颈。其核心贡献是以特定硬件信任假设换取单轮密钥生成和高吞吐实现，为FSS在隐私计算系统中的实用部署提供了更可行的路径。

Abstract: Function secret sharing (FSS) has become a core primitive in privacy-preserving computation. However, each FSS invocation requires a fresh pair of function keys generated by a trusted dealer , expands the system's trust boundary and hinders practical deployment. Existing dealerless protocols eliminate this dependency, but incur substantial communication and a number of interaction rounds that grows linearly with the input bit-width, making key generation a major bottleneck.
  This paper present KORD, a protocol--hardware co-design that dramatically reduces the cost of dealerless FSS key generation. At its core is a pair of special-purpose chips that establish a common root of trust through mutual attestation and, within it, reconstruct FSS keys---eliminating the need for a dealer. This root of trust further forms a security boundary within which KORD restructures the generation protocol, collapsing the interaction of prior dealerless protocols into a single round, independent of GGM depth. A cross-key scheduling scheme then interleaves independent GGM-tree traversals, sustaining high computational throughput. KORD reduces per-key-generation communication by 7,633--70,274$\times$ over the state-of-the-art distributed FSS protocol across a comprehensive suite of FSS building blocks. Post-route analysis projects 12.75 million 32-bit DPF keys per second at 204 MHz using 21.5K LUTs, with 99.8% AES lane utilization. On private ResNet-18 inference, KORD cuts the share of end-to-end time spent on key generation from over 96% to 11.9%.

</details>


### [223] [Using Hyper-V Sockets for Real-time Data Extraction from a Malware Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30383)
*István-Attila Császár,Radu-Marian Portase,Adrian Coleşa,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We present how Hyper-V sockets can be used as a real-time communication channel for a malware analysis sandbox. We show that, compared to WinSock TCP sockets, Hyper-V sockets are not subject to TCP/IP-layer blocking and are not enumerated by common TCP connection listing tools. We compare the throughput of the two communication channels as a function of buffer size.

</details>


### [224] [Attesting Outputs and Delegation Ancestry in Multi-Agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30387)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需共享权威、公共日志或预先定义工作流的双层证明机制，用于多智能体动态委托场景中的输出归属与跨部署者边授权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凭据能够证明主体身份和行动权限，但无法将部署者与其实际发布的输出字节绑定，也难以证明动态创建的跨部署者边获得双方授权。系统还需应对子密钥泄露及提示注入等风险。

Method: 可信部署者运行时为每个发布输出签名其哈希，以绑定发布者与输出内容；同时通过祖先证据记录委托边授权。研究在统一威胁模型下比较签名链表、Merkle链变体和共同签名DAG，并使用固定攻击者矩阵与回归测试验证组合式验证器。

Result: 子密钥泄露后，单签名设计允许攻击者伪造未授权的父级绑定，而共同签名DAG因要求父部署者授权边而能够拒绝该攻击。Apple M1 Pro上，祖先证据检查耗时为每跳24.3至499.2微秒。本地多服务实验中，30个签名DAG任务均完成验证，受控的仅持有子密钥的伪造声明被拒绝；引入证据后的平均端到端延迟为813.1毫秒，无证据时为770.8毫秒。AWS三可用区实验中，1000条有效共同签名DAG路径全部通过验证，签发平均耗时3.651毫秒，完整验证平均耗时5.015毫秒。

Conclusion: 共同签名DAG能够同时提供输出归属和动态边授权证明，并在子密钥泄露场景下增强安全性；其验证与签发开销较低，适合多部署者协作工作流。实验结果未包含TLS/mTLS、KMS和模型服务延迟，因此整体生产环境开销仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Multi-agent applications delegate work across independently operated deployers. After an incident, a verifier must answer two questions: which deployer released the reported bytes, and whether each cross-deployer edge was authorized. Credentials establish who may act, but need not bind them to later output bytes or prove both deployers authorized a dynamically created edge. We present a two-layer attestation design for dynamic delegation without a shared authority, public log, or precommitted workflow. A trusted deployer runtime signs a hash of each released output; this records released bytes but does not prevent prompt injection. Ancestry evidence records edge authorization. Under a unified threat model, we compare a signed linked list, a Merkle-chain variant, and a co-signed DAG. The primitives are standard; the contribution is deployer-side binding and the evidence needed for the two questions. After child-key compromise, the single-signer designs permit an unauthorized parent binding, whereas the co-signed DAG rejects it because the parent must authorize the edge. Fixed adversary matrices and regression tests validate the composed verifier. On an Apple M1 Pro, ancestry-only checks take 24.3-499.2us per hop. In a live local multi-service workflow, a parent discovers the child's A2A Agent Card; the child calls an MCP tool and releases local-LLM output: all 30 signed-DAG tasks passed complete verification, while a controlled child-key-only claim was rejected; its mean end-to-end latency was 813.1ms versus 770.8ms without evidence. In a complementary three-availability-zone AWS deployment, all 1,000 valid co-signed-DAG paths verified; issuance averaged 3.651ms and complete verification 5.015ms. The cloud result excludes TLS/mTLS, KMS, and model-serving latency.

</details>


### [225] [Why Are LLM Backdoor Defenses Fragmented? A Feature-Level Explanation with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30403)
*Yizhe Zeng,Chenxu Niu,Wei Zhang,Hao Huang,Yunpeng Li,Dongxu Han,Dan Du,Cheng Hong,Hequn Xian,Yuling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从特征层面对大语言模型后门进行系统机制分析，解释了脏标签与干净标签攻击为何需要不同防御，并提出基于特征抑制的统一缓解思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御方法较为分散，难以同时应对脏标签和干净标签攻击。研究旨在揭示两类攻击在模型内部表示和决策机制上的差异，从而为统一、可解释的防御提供依据。

Method: 构建干净模型与中毒模型在干净输入和触发输入下的2×2对比，通过SAE追踪导致输出logit变化的高贡献特征，并将其划分为交互特征、抑制特征、混合特征和权重修改特征。随后在推理阶段对相关特征进行钳制，以验证机制分析和防御效果。

Result: 脏标签后门主要由孤立的交互特征驱动；干净标签后门则更多依赖混合特征与权重修改特征的异质组合。特征钳制后，多数脏标签设置下的攻击成功率（ASR）最高降至10.8%，多数干净标签设置下最高降至15.4%，同时基本保持良性任务性能。

Conclusion: 两类后门在特征编码机制上的系统差异是现有防御碎片化的重要原因。SAE能够提供可解释的后门机制分析，并指导一种兼顾不同攻击范式的推理时特征级缓解方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious threat to large language models (LLMs), but existing defenses remain fragmented, failing to pro?vide unified defense against both dirty-label and clean-label attacks. To investigate why such fragmentation arises, we present the first systematic feature-level mechanistic analysis of LLM backdoors using sparse autoencoders (SAEs). Starting from a 2 x 2 comparison of clean and poisoned models on clean and triggered inputs, we trace backdoor-induced logit shifts to high-contributing SAE features and categorize them into four roles: interac?tion, suppressed, mixed, and weight-modified features. This taxonomy reveals system?atic encoding differences: dirty-label back?doors are dominated by isolated interaction features, whereas clean-label backdoors rely more on heterogeneous mixtures of mixed and weight-modified features. These differ?ences explain why existing defenses remain fragmented across attack paradigms. We val?idate this hypothesis through inference-time feature clamping, which reduces ASR to at most 10.8% in most dirty-label settings and at most 15.4% in the majority of clean-label settings, while preserving benign-task perfor?mance. These results show that SAE-based analysis can explain defense fragmentation and guide interpretable backdoor mitigation.

</details>


### [226] [ECLIPSE: Self-Evolving Stealthy Prompt Injection Attack against Long-Horizon Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30441)
*Shiqian Zhao,Yangfan Zhou,Xinfeng Li,Runyi Hu,Yechao Zhang,Yi Xie,Tianwei Zhang,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出ECLIPSE，一种面向长时程LLM智能体的隐蔽提示注入框架，并构建LASE-Bench基准。实验表明，该框架在无防护及常见安全过滤条件下均表现出较高的攻击成功率，指出现有智能体安全防护存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体通过多轮工具调用完成复杂任务，扩大了提示注入风险。现有方法通常存在两类局限：单条显式恶意指令容易被检测；将意图拆分到多个阶段虽更隐蔽，但执行链不稳定、成功率有限。论文旨在同时提升攻击链的隐蔽性、可执行性与跨环境适应性。

Method: ECLIPSE结合直接用户提示注入和间接工具侧注入。其一，隐蔽攻击轨迹合成模块在沙箱中生成、迭代验证候选工具调用链，并将已验证轨迹表述为自然语言的一次性提示。其二，工具链引导模块通过静态工作流编码，在工具描述中嵌入状态转换线索；并通过动态轨迹纠偏，在实际执行偏离预定路径时提供修正信号。评测方面，LASE-Bench包含120项恶意任务和198个独特工具，其中96.7%的任务至少需要5次工具调用。

Result: 在作者报告的设置中，ECLIPSE无防护时最高攻击成功率为96.7%，经常见安全过滤后仍达到69.2%；在有防护条件下，较最强基线高27.5个百分点。针对多种代表性防御的评估显示，现有防护机制无法稳定阻断该类由提示、工具描述和动态执行反馈共同驱动的攻击链。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于将可验证的工具轨迹规划与工具侧状态引导结合，使长时程智能体中的提示注入更具持续性和隐蔽性。其结果表明，仅依赖通用安全过滤或孤立的提示检测不足以应对此类风险；更有效的防护应覆盖用户输入、工具元数据、跨步骤状态转移、轨迹异常检测及执行期策略约束等多个层面。

Abstract: Recently, large language model (LLM) agents, such as Codex, Claude Code, and OpenClaw, have become capable of planning and executing long-horizon tasks through repeated tool calls. This capability also creates new opportunities for prompt injection. Existing attacks either place the malicious objective in one explicit instruction, making it easy to detect, or distribute the intent across multiple execution stages, making successful completion unreliable.
  In this work, we propose ECLIPSE, a self-evolving and stealthy prompt-injection framework for long-horizon agentic systems. ECLIPSE combines direct user-prompt injection with indirect tool-side injection through two components. On the one hand, Stealthy Attack Trajectory Synthesis uses a sandbox to generate and iteratively verify candidate tool chains, then renders a verified chain as a natural one-shot prompt to serve as the direct instruction. Then, Tool-Chain Steering transfers this plan to the target environment through Static Workflow Encoding (SWE), which embeds state-transition cues in target-tool descriptions, and Dynamic Trajectory Correction (DTC), which supplies corrective signals when execution deviates from the planned chain.
  To enable systematic evaluation, we further introduce LASE-Bench, a long-horizon agent-safety benchmark with 120 malicious tasks and 198 unique tools; 96.7% of its tasks make at least five tool calls. The experimental results show that ECLIPSE is highly effective: it achieves up to 96.7% attack success without defense and 69.2% under the common safety filter, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5% in the defended setting. Evaluations against representative defenses further show that existing safeguards do not reliably defend it, which raises the need for more effective defenses.

</details>


### [227] [Towards Operator-Empowered Vulnerability Hotfixing for 5G Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30615)
*Dong Hyeok Kim,Xin Zhe Khooi,Hocheol Nam,Seungjin Baek,Mun Choon Chan,CheolJun Park,Min Suk Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Buckler是一种面向移动网络运营商的RAN临时热修复框架，可在标准永久修复完成前，通过有限、可逆且本地化的规则缓解部分蜂窝协议漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝协议漏洞可能长期暴露，而标准组织、设备厂商和运营商需要较长时间协调修复。现有RAN缺乏一种无需大规模改造、能够快速部署并可撤销的临时防御机制。

Method: Buckler在标准化L2/L3信道边界设置可复用钩子，并提供封闭的有状态匹配—动作接口，支持DROP、MODIFY和RELEASE三种防御操作。研究者从23篇论文中归纳64类源于L2/L3标准协议行为的问题，分析其RAN干预点，针对其中20类构造热修复，并在srsRAN和OpenAirInterface中实现5个标准信道钩子。

Result: 64类问题中有43类具备RAN层预防干预点，20类成功构造热修复。全部修复均使用同一规则词汇和仅5个标准化钩子；实验验证了三种操作在代表性可用性与隐私问题中的有效性。两个开源RAN实现所需改动较小且结构相似。其余问题主要依赖终端侧状态或行为，无法由RAN单独解决。

Conclusion: Buckler证明了由运营商实施的RAN临时热修复具有实用性、可移植性和较低部署成本，能够在正式标准修复前缓解相当一部分协议问题。同时，研究明确了RAN-only防御的边界：对于依赖终端协同或端点状态的问题，仅靠网络侧规则无法完成预防。

Abstract: Cellular protocol vulnerabilities can remain exploitable for months or years while standards bodies, vendors, and mobile network operators (MNOs) coordinate permanent fixes. We present Buckler, a framework that enables an MNO to deploy temporary, local, and reversible hotfixes in its radio access network (RAN) during this exposure window. Buckler places reusable hooks at standardized L2/L3 channel boundaries and exposes a closed, stateful match-action interface with three preventive actions: DROP, MODIFY, and RELEASE. We evaluate whether this bounded design provides useful coverage without requiring extensive changes to existing RANs. From 23 papers, we identify 64 attacks rooted in standard L2/L3 protocol behavior, of which 43 provide a preventive intervention point at the RAN, and we construct Buckler hotfixes for 20 of them. All 20 hotfixes use the same rule vocabulary and only five standardized channel hooks, while the unsupported attacks expose endpoint dependencies that a RAN cannot satisfy alone. We implement the five hooks on srsRAN and OpenAirInterface with small, structurally similar changes, and demonstrate all three actions against representative availability and privacy attacks. These results establish operator-empowered hotfixing as a practical and portable interim defense and delineate the architectural limits of RAN-only prevention.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond the Payload: How User Invocation Shapes Coding Agent Vulnerability to Repository Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30686)
*Fukang Zhu,Binbin Zhao,Ruixiao Lin,Ping He,Tianyu Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CIPR基准，用于系统评估编码代理在受污染代码仓库中受到提示级配置影响的安全风险。结果表明，任务类型、提示表达及技能/规则设置会显著改变攻击成功率与代理告警率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码仓库投毒研究主要关注攻击者的注入与伪装策略，较少考察开发者在任务选择、提示措辞以及技能或规则配置上的日常决策如何塑造风险。论文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建CIPR基准，包含20个真实世界受污染仓库中的1920个实例，覆盖4类任务、3种基于社交媒体风格的提示方式和3种技能/规则条件。采用自动化运行时预言机与基于代理轨迹的预言机，测量攻击成功率（ASR）和代理告警率（AR）。

Result: 风险具有显著情境依赖性：不同任务类型可造成最高约4.5倍的ASR差异，其中测试执行任务表现为高攻击成功率与低告警率的隐蔽攻击面。提示表达也会间接影响风险：信息不足的提示因缩短执行深度而降低ASR；包含噪声的提示则呈现抑制告警的趋势，使恶意内容更不易被察觉。

Conclusion: 编码代理的脆弱性并非静态属性，而是用户日常配置与受污染仓库交互共同形成的动态结果。安全评估和防护机制应将任务类型、提示表达及技能/规则配置纳入系统考量。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, including bootstrapping projects from third-party repositories whose integrity cannot be assumed. Prior work on repository poisoning largely focuses on attacker-controlled injection and disguise, but developers also shape risk through everyday invocation choices: what task to delegate, how to phrase the request, and which skills or rules to supply. We term these user-side choices Prompt-Level Configurations (PLCs) and introduce CIPR (Coding In Poisoned Repos), the first benchmark that systematically varies PLCs in poisoned real-world repositories.
