CIPR comprises 1,920 instances across 20 repositories, four task types, three social-media-grounded prompt styles, and three skill/rule conditions, and measures attack success rate (ASR) and agent alert rate (AR) using automated runtime and trace-based oracles. Our evaluation reveals two key insights: (1) Vulnerability is highly context-dependent, with task type creating up to a 4.5-fold difference in ASR, with test-execution task forming a silent attack surface (high ASR, low AR). (2) Prompt expression shifts risk indirectly: underspecified prompts reduce ASR by truncating execution depth; noisy prompts exhibit a directional trend toward suppressing alerts by making malicious content less conspicuous. These findings highlight that coding agent vulnerability is not a static property, but a dynamic outcome shaped by everyday user configurations.

</details>


### [229] [SingProbe Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30703)
*Sing Team*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SingProbe，一种直接复用大语言模型推理过程隐藏状态的轻量级运行时护栏，可在自回归生成期间以极低额外开销持续评估用户意图、回复安全性与幻觉风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时护栏通常依赖独立外部模型，因而带来额外推理成本、风险反馈滞后，并可能因模型能力不匹配而限制效果。作者旨在利用基础模型内部表征，实现更低成本、更及时且与生成过程紧密耦合的监控机制。

Method: SingProbe在模型逐词生成时读取其隐藏状态，并以约200万参数的轻量探针进行词元级预测，统一覆盖查询意图、生成安全性和幻觉风险。论文同时提出SingStreamBench，用于检验流式护栏能否在正常文本前缀上保持不干预，并在风险内容逐步出现时及时识别。进一步地，作者利用探针分数预测后续生成风险，并将其用于受约束的安全解码；在医疗生成场景中，SingProbe-Med仅在出现临床相关风险时触发定向解码干预。

Result: 实验表明，SingProbe相较于显著更大的独立护栏模型及专用幻觉检测器具有相当或更优的性能，同时额外开销低于0.5%。结果还显示，探针输出不仅可用于被动检测，也可提前预警未来生成风险，并有效引导安全解码。

Conclusion: 模型隐藏状态可作为生成时监控与控制的有效接口。SingProbe证明，内在式、流式护栏能够在几乎不增加推理负担的前提下，实现及时的风险检测和针对性的生成干预，为高效部署大语言模型护栏提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Runtime guardrails are essential for reliable large language model (LLM) deployment, yet existing approaches typically rely on independent, external models that introduce additional inference cost, delayed safety signals, and a capacity mismatch with increasingly capable base models. To address these issues, we introduce SingProbe, a lightweight intrinsic runtime guard that directly reuses hidden states produced during LLM inference and operates alongside autoregressive decoding. Within a unified framework, SingProbe continuously predicts query intent, response safety, and hallucination risk at the token level with negligible additional guardrail inference overhead, offering a "free-lunch" solution. We further introduce SingStreamBench, a benchmark designed to assess whether streaming guardrails remain inactive on benign prefixes while promptly detecting emerging unsafe content. Extensive experiments show that SingProbe achieves competitive or superior performance compared with substantially larger standalone guardrails and specialized hallucination detectors, with only $\approx$2M parameters and $<0.5\%$ extra overhead. Beyond passive detection, we also show that SingProbe scores can anticipate future generation risk and guide constrained safe decoding. We further extend this paradigm to medical generation through SingProbe-Med, which selectively activates risk-directed decoding interventions only when clinically relevant risks emerge. Together, these results demonstrate that internal model representations provide an effective and efficient interface for generation-time monitoring and control.

</details>


### [230] [The Fragility of Jailbreak Robustness Across Operational States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30748)
*Yuna Park,Hwang Youn Kim,Yujin Kim,Won Woo Ro,Suhyun Kim,Jae-In Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM 的越狱鲁棒性高度依赖运行状态；仅改变不针对安全性的普通系统提示词，就可能显著改变攻击成功率，因此单一默认状态评估不足以全面反映模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱评估通常仅在默认运行状态下报告单一攻击成功率，忽略了用户交互可能诱导出的多种运行状态，因而可能低估或误判模型在实际使用条件下的安全鲁棒性。

Method: 在 7 个对齐模型和 3 类代表性越狱攻击上，固定攻击方式，仅改变普通系统提示词以构造非默认运行状态，并比较不同状态下的攻击成功率。同时分析隐藏表示中与拒答相关方向的变化，并检验其对越狱结果的预测能力。

Result: 不同运行状态之间出现显著攻击成功率差异；某案例中攻击成功率由 2% 升至 58%，增加 56 个百分点。即使攻击最初针对默认状态设计和优化，这种状态依赖性仍然存在。状态相关的鲁棒性变化与隐藏表示在拒答相关轴上的差异系统关联，而沿该轴的投影能够较强地预测越狱结果。

Conclusion: 越狱鲁棒性并非固定不变，而是显著依赖运行状态。安全评估不应只采用默认状态下的单一攻击成功率，还应系统考察多种非默认运行状态，并结合内部表示分析以提升评估的完整性和解释性。

Abstract: Existing jailbreak evaluations typically characterize robustness using a single attack success rate (ASR) measured in a default configuration (the vanilla state). However, user-LLM interactions can induce diverse operational states beyond the vanilla state. In this work, we find that jailbreak robustness is highly fragile to operational-state variation: even when the attack remains fixed, changing only an ordinary system prompt not designed to affect safety can dramatically alter attack success rates. We systematically investigate this phenomenon across seven aligned models and three representative jailbreak attacks, observing substantial differences in ASR between vanilla and non-vanilla operational states. In one case, ASR increases by up to 56 percentage points (2% to 58%) solely due to a change in operational state. Remarkably, these increases occur even for attacks originally designed and optimized under vanilla-state evaluation. We further show that state-dependent robustness variation is systematically associated with differences in hidden representations along a refusal-related axis, and that projections onto this axis strongly predict jailbreak outcomes. Our results show that a single vanilla-state evaluation may not fully characterize jailbreak robustness, motivating evaluations that also examine how robustness changes across non-vanilla operational states.

</details>


### [231] [DP-VOXLET: Provable Speaker Anonymization for Disentangled Speech Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30969)
*Ivoline Ngong,Jack D'Iorio,Hailey Schoppe,Christopher Liberatore,Nichole Schimanski,Taisa Kushner,Joseph P. Near*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“说话人差分隐私”形式化定义及相应的可证明匿名化机制，在保护语义内容和韵律的同时，为任意攻击者的说话人重识别性能提供理论保证，并较以往差分隐私方法取得更高效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话人匿名化方法主要依赖解耦表示，将语义内容与说话人属性分离后替换后者，但多为启发式方案，隐私效果通常只能通过经验指标评估，缺乏对任意攻击者的形式化保证。

Method: 基于差分隐私框架定义说话人差分隐私，并设计满足该定义的说话人匿名化机制。该机制与现有的语义—说话人解耦表示兼容，在保留原始语义内容和韵律的基础上扰动或替换说话人相关属性。

Result: 该方法能够为任意潜在攻击者的说话人重识别性能提供可证明的下界，例如等错误率（EER）下界。实验表明，相较于先前面向说话人匿名化的差分隐私方法，该方法在隐私保证下实现了显著更高的语用效用。

Conclusion: 说话人差分隐私将说话人匿名化从经验性设计推进到具有形式化保证的隐私机制，并兼顾了隐私与语音可用性，为基于解耦表示的匿名化系统提供了可验证且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Systems for speaker anonymization obfuscate the speaker of an utterance, while maintaining its original semantic contents and prosody. Recent solutions for speaker anonymization rely on learned representations that disentangle an utterance into semantic contents and speaker properties. To anonymize an utterance, these systems replace the speaker properties while leaving the semantic contents unchanged---an approach that can produce strong results on empirical measures of privacy.
  In this work, we introduce speaker differential privacy, a formal definition of speaker anonymization based on the framework of differential privacy, and a mechanism for speaker anonymization that provably satisfies the definition. In contrast to prior heuristic-based anonymization systems, our approach enables a provable lower bound on re-identification success rate (e.g. equal error rate) for any possible adversary. We implement our approach in a framework that is compatible with existing disentangled representations. Compared to the prior work on differential privacy for speaker anonymization, our approach achieves significantly higher utility.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Rust's Type Checker Implementation Is Unsound: An Empirical Study on Soundness Bugs in rustc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28713)
*Yusung Sim,Sukyoung Ryu,Jaemin Hong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文对2022年1月1日至2025年9月1日期间报告的30个rustc潜在类型健全性缺陷进行实证分析，指出部分编译器错误会绕过本应执行的类型检查并导致内存安全问题；现有动态检测工具可发现部分后果，而形式化工具与规范文档尚不足以充当完整的健全性测试预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust的安全承诺依赖编译器正确实施类型系统。尽管语言设计旨在避免内存错误，rustc仍可能错误接受本应被拒绝的程序。该研究希望系统梳理此类缺陷的触发条件、影响、发现与修复过程，并评估现有实现和文档能否支持类型健全性测试。

Method: 作者从GitHub问题跟踪器收集30个潜在rustc健全性问题，逐项分析受影响语言特性、错误症状、最终后果、触发特性、社区对其是否构成缺陷的共识，以及特性引入、缺陷发现和修复的生命周期。同时，评估AddressSanitizer、Miri、Chalk和a-mir-formality等实现，以及Rust Reference、FLS和RFC等文档，作为测试类型健全性的潜在预言机的能力。

Result: 研究发现：由隐含约束或特征对象触发的部分缺陷可破坏内存安全；关联类型边界情形以及生命周期与特征的交互使健全类型检查尤为困难；多数问题自相关特性首次引入时即已存在，且通常需要较长时间才被发现；AddressSanitizer与Miri能够检测会演化为内存错误的一部分健全性缺陷，但Chalk和a-mir-formality虽具潜力，目前成熟度不足；既有文档常无法精确定义相关语言语义。

Conclusion: rustc的类型健全性风险主要集中在复杂类型系统特性的组合与边界行为，且缺陷具有长期潜伏特征。提升可靠性需要结合面向隐含约束、特征对象、关联类型及生命周期交互的针对性测试，利用动态工具捕获可观测内存后果，并持续完善形式化实现与语言规范，使其能够提供更精确、可执行的语义依据。

Abstract: Rust is claimed to be a type-sound language capable of preventing various undesirable behaviors, including memory bugs. However, rustc, the official Rust compiler, is not immune to defects; it contains soundness bugs, where the compiler accepts programs that should be rejected during type checking. In this work, we present an empirical study of 30 issues that report potential soundness bugs in rustc, collected from the GitHub issue tracker between January 1, 2022 and September 1, 2025. We analyze each issue in depth, focusing on its affected feature, symptom (how the feature is mishandled), consequence (the resulting undesirable behavior), triggering features, community consensus regarding whether it is a bug, and lifecycle, including introduction, discovery, and fix. Furthermore, we investigate existing artifacts, including implementations such as AddressSanitizer, Miri, Chalk, and a-mir-formality, alongside documentation such as the Rust Reference, the FLS, and Rust RFCs to assess their potential as oracles for testing the type soundness of rustc. Our key findings indicate that: (1) Certain soundness bugs, typically triggered by implied bounds or trait objects, compromise memory safety. (2) Sound type checking is challenged by edge cases involving associated types and the interaction between lifetimes and traits. (3) Most bugs persist from the initial introduction of the relevant features and require significant time to be discovered. (4) While AddressSanitizer and Miri can detect soundness bugs that lead to memory bugs, a-mir-formality and Chalk are currently immature despite their potential to identify other bug categories. (5) Existing documentation frequently fails to provide precise explanations of the language semantics.

</details>


### [233] [FlowCheck: Helping End-Users Specify and Verify Intent in Vibe-Coded Web Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28880)
*Reya Vir,Lydia Chilton,Zhuo Zhang,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出FlowCheck，一种面向“氛围式编程”应用的信息流约束语言。它允许开发者从用户界面视角声明可见信息应如何流向状态或输出，并将约束确定性地编译为CodeQL分析，以检测界面看似正常但实际数据未正确传递的静默行为错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大模型生成的应用可能具有可操作的界面，却存在输入、状态与输出之间的信息流断裂，导致用户可见功能失效。这类错误对不直接阅读代码的开发者尤其难以发现；同时，直接要求大模型在代码中找错的准确性和稳定性有限。因此，作者希望提供一种既贴近界面意图、又可被静态分析工具可靠验证的表达与检查机制。

Method: FlowCheck使用约束语言描述界面元素、用户输入、中间状态及可见输出之间应满足的信息流关系。约束可视化展示和人工检查，并具有适合大模型生成的结构化形式。系统随后将这些约束转换为确定性的CodeQL静态分析规则，在代码层面验证指定信息流是否存在。实验覆盖4个由Claude Code生成的应用，人工注入30个违反约束的缺陷；同时将Claude Opus 4.7、DeepSeek V3和Gemini Pro作为基于提示词的代码找错基线进行比较。

Result: FlowCheck成功转换全部约束，并检测出30个注入的信息流违反问题，未出现误报。相比之下，三种前沿模型在相同代码上的缺陷发现准确率明显较低，没有任何一个模型达到完全准确。结果表明，将界面层意图转化为确定性静态分析，比仅依赖模型进行代码审查更可靠。

Conclusion: FlowCheck将开发者熟悉的界面行为意图与代码级静态验证连接起来，使非代码中心的应用构建者能够声明并检查用户可见信息流。其核心贡献在于以结构化约束驱动确定性CodeQL分析，从而有效识别界面正常但行为失效的静默错误；不过，摘要中的评估规模较小，未来仍需在更多应用类型、真实缺陷和复杂动态数据流场景中验证泛化性。

Abstract: Vibe-coded applications often contain silent behavioral failures in which the interface appears functional even though user-visible information does not flow to the expected state or output. We introduce FlowCheck, a constraint language to specify these user-visible information flows directly through the application interface, where constraints can also be displayed and inspected without reading code, and are structured enough for reliable LLM generation. FlowCheck translates the constraints into deterministic CodeQL analyses, and we evaluate it across four applications generated via Claude Code, and compare with three coding models as bug-finding baselines. We find that FlowCheck correctly translates and flags all 30 of our injected constraint violations with no false positives. In contrast, frontier models (Claude Opus 4.7, DeepSeek V3, and Gemini Pro) showed significantly lower accuracy when prompted to find bugs in the same code, with none achieving full accuracy. This approach lets vibe coders state intent in terms of the interface they understand, and checks it deterministically against the code they do not.

</details>


### [234] [STEP: A Modular Silent Trial Engine for Operational Evaluation of Digital Pathology AI in Routine Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28708)
*Gabriele Campanella,Matthew Croken,Olga Lukatskaya,Jane Houldsworth,Ricky Kwan,Peter Schüffler,Chad Vanderbilt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出STEP（病理静默试验引擎），一个用于计算病理人工智能模型前瞻性静默试验的可复用软件平台。该平台已在三家机构部署，用于在不影响临床决策的前提下，评估EAGLE模型从全切片图像预测EGFR突变状态的实际运行表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理模型从回顾性验证走向临床应用前，需要在真实、持续产生的临床数据上进行前瞻性静默评估，以验证模型性能、运行稳定性和工作流可靠性。现有静默试验通常依赖针对单个机构或应用定制的软件，难以适配不同实验室信息系统、数字病理平台、计算资源及推理流程，导致工程重复建设和跨机构复用困难。

Method: 作者开发STEP，将通用的试验编排逻辑与机构特异性的数据访问及计算基础设施解耦，并通过模块化适配器进行连接。系统功能包括：定时病例发现、逐张切片的推理任务提交、确定性幂等执行、故障恢复、模型结果与附加数据导入、试验及运行状态持久化，以及审计日志记录。计算适配器同时支持本地运行和基于LSF、Slurm的高性能计算环境。作者在三家机构部署该系统，并以EAGLE模型的EGFR突变预测任务开展前瞻性静默评估。

Result: STEP实现了在异构病理与计算环境中运行统一静默试验框架的能力。其模块化设计使核心试验工作流无需因机构差异而重写，同时保留可恢复、可追溯、可审计的长期试验状态。三家机构的部署表明，该平台能够支持跨站点的计算病理模型前瞻性运行评估。

Conclusion: STEP为计算病理人工智能的临床前真实世界验证提供了通用的软件基础设施。通过分离试验级工作流与站点级集成，该平台有望减少重复工程开发，提高静默试验的标准化、可靠性和可审计性，并促进模型在进入干预性临床部署前接受系统性的现实环境评估。

Abstract: Prospective silent trials provide an important bridge between retrospective validation of artificial intelligence (AI) models and their use in clinical care by evaluating model performance and operational reliability on live clinical data without influencing patient management. In computational pathology, conducting silent trials requires integration across laboratory information systems, digital pathology infrastructure, computational resources, and model inference pipelines, and these workflows are often implemented using application-specific software. We developed the Silent Trial Engine for Pathology (STEP), a reusable software platform for orchestrating prospective silent trials of computational pathology AI models across heterogeneous clinical and computational environments. STEP separates common trial orchestration from institution-specific data access and compute infrastructure through modular adapter interfaces. The platform supports scheduled case discovery, per-slide inference submission, deterministic idempotency, failure recovery, result and ancillary-data ingestion, persistent trial and run state, and audit logging, with compute adapters supporting local execution and high-performance computing environments using LSF and Slurm. STEP was deployed at three institutions to support prospective silent evaluation of EAGLE, an AI model for predicting EGFR mutation status from hematoxylin and eosin-stained whole-slide images. By separating trial-level workflow logic from site-specific integrations, STEP enables a common execution framework to operate across heterogeneous pathology environments while maintaining durable and auditable trial state. This approach may reduce duplicated engineering effort and facilitate systematic real-world evaluation of computational pathology AI before interventional clinical deployment.

</details>


### [235] [Beyond Vector Search: Comparing Classical RAG with Hybrid GraphRAG for Climate Science Q\&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28766)
*Daniel Naiff,Ronnie Alves,Gustavo Pinto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种融合向量检索、GraphRAG、Leiden社区检测与交叉编码器重排序的混合检索增强生成架构，用于整合分散在多篇科学文献中的层次化概念关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG通常将文档或文本片段孤立处理，难以捕捉跨文献、跨层级的概念依赖，导致复杂科学领域中的上下文理解和问答质量受限。

Method: 结合向量搜索与GraphRAG构建局部—全局检索框架，通过Leiden算法识别概念社区，并利用交叉编码器对候选结果进行重排序，以增强相关信息的整合能力。

Result: 相较于经典RAG，系统的上下文相关性提升160%，上下文召回率提升177%。摘要未说明数据集、基线配置、实验规模及统计显著性。

Conclusion: 统一局部检索与全局图结构检索能够明显优于基于孤立文本片段的传统RAG，为分散科学文献上的高质量问答提供了有效方案；但仍需更多实验细节验证结果的普适性和可复现性。

Abstract: Traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems treat documents in isolation, failing to capture hierarchical relationships between concepts in complex scientific corpora. This limitation compromises answer quality in specialized domains such as climatology, where conceptual dependencies frequently traverse multiple articles. We propose a hybrid architecture that integrates vector search with GraphRAG, Leiden community detection, and cross-encoder re-ranking, achieving gains of 160\% in contextual relevance and 177\% in contextual recall compared to classical RAG. These results demonstrate that unifying local and global retrieval significantly outperforms text-span isolation, paving the way for more effective question-answering systems over dispersed scientific literature.

</details>


### [236] [The reach of a verification tool decides its value: A controlled study of verification surface, artifact quality, and cost in AI coding agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28795)
*Achint Mehta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过控制编码智能体可用的验证工具数量与类型，检验“验证面”扩展是否提升软件质量。结论是：验证工具并非普遍有效，其收益取决于工具能否覆盖应用的实际失效模式；启动探针以较低成本带来最显著、最稳健的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编码智能体可借助代码检查、启动探针、命令行环境和截图等工具自检，但增加工具会提高推理与操作成本。研究旨在隔离“工具集”这一变量，量化不同验证能力对最终交付软件质量、成本及不同类型缺陷的影响。

Method: 作者构建最小化编码智能体，将工具列表设为唯一受控变量；在6个模型、8种工具配置下实现1,116个网页应用。应用由不知晓实验条件的人工评审者依据固定评分量表打分，并通过自动化探针测试可通过API观察到的行为。比较无工具、仅启动探针、完整命令行环境及附加截图能力等配置的质量与令牌成本。

Result: 无工具时，约七分之一应用无法启动。单一启动探针以约完整命令行环境35%的令牌成本，消除了几乎全部启动失败；完整命令行环境的成本约为无工具配置的2.35倍。截图对布局、元素位置和交互等可视错误更有帮助，但相对命令行环境的增益较小，且经多重比较校正后不再显著。对于10万行列表滚动流畅性等需测量而难以从画面判断的问题，截图没有额外价值。

Conclusion: 验证能力的扩展应按故障覆盖范围进行匹配，而非默认叠加所有工具。优先部署低成本、可直接检测关键失效的工具，尤其是启动验证；视觉工具仅适用于视觉可观测的缺陷，且其边际收益可能有限。

Abstract: Modern artificial-intelligence coding agents can be equipped with tools for checking their own work e.g. a linter, a boot probe, a shell, a screenshot tool. We call this set the agent's verification surface. This study asks whether increasing only that surface, with everything else held fixed, produces a matching growth in the quality of the software the agent ships. We built a minimal coding agent whose tool list is the single controlled variable and used it to implement 1,116 web applications across six models and eight tool configurations. A condition-blind human graded every application against a frozen rubric, and automatic probes stress-tested the API-observable behaviors. Verification's cheapest benefit arrives first, which is to make sure that the application comes up. Without any tools, about one build in seven fails to launch at all and a single boot probe removes nearly all of these failures at roughly 35 percent of a full shell's token cost, while the full shell multiplies the no-tools cost by 2.35. Screenshots help most where mistakes are visible (e.g. element placement, interaction), though even there the gain over a shell is modest and does not survive correction for multiple statistical comparisons. In cases where failures can only be measured rather than seen, such as keeping scrolling smooth over a 100,000-row list, screenshots add nothing. A verification tool improves the output artifact only where its reach covers the way the application actually fails.

</details>


### [237] [UML Class Diagram Evaluation and Repair Strategies based on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28800)
*Jie Liang,Peng Liang,Chong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估主流大语言模型生成UML类图的能力，并提出三类缺陷修复策略。结果表明，模型生成的类图在关键类、关系、继承及设计规范方面存在明显缺陷，但经针对性修复后可获得显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UML类图的构建高度依赖建模人员经验，且传统建模成本较高。尽管大语言模型为自动化软件建模提供了新可能，但其在UML类图建模中的实际质量、常见错误类型及可修复性尚缺乏系统验证，因此需要开展多维评估与修复研究。

Method: 研究选取多个典型软件系统案例，生成并分析不同主流大语言模型产出的UML类图。评估结合SDMetrics自动度量与专家人工审查，从类图规模与完整性、关系正确性、继承层次结构以及设计规则遵从性等维度进行比较。针对发现的典型缺陷，提出记忆强化、外部知识注入和检测驱动的自动定向修复三种策略，并验证修复效果。

Result: 与专家绘制的类图相比，大语言模型生成结果普遍存在关键类识别不完整、类间关系混淆或遗漏、继承关系不足或缺失、未使用类以及循环依赖等问题。实施修复后，各模型均有不同程度提升：关键类识别的平均修复率达到85%，耦合关系修复率为46%，继承关系修复率为69%，未使用类和循环依赖的修复率均达到100%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够辅助UML类图建模，但尚不能稳定替代专家建模，尤其在语义完整性、关系推断和继承设计方面仍需加强。将模型输出与度量检测、领域知识及定向修复机制结合，可显著提升类图质量；其中，结构性规则问题较易自动修复，而关系正确性仍是后续优化的重点。

Abstract: UML class diagrams are a crucial tool for defining the structure of software systems, but designing accurate and comprehensive class diagrams is a challenging task. Traditionally, creating UML models relies on the expertise and experience of professionals. However, with the development of AI technologies, particularly LLMs, new opportunities for software modeling have emerged. Despite this, there has been limited research on the application of LLMs in software modeling, especially in UML class diagram modeling. This study conducts experiments on several typical software system cases. Combining SDMetrics with expert manual review, this paper comprehensively evaluates the practical performance of mainstream LLMs in UML class diagram modeling from multiple dimensions, including size and completeness, relationship correctness, inheritance hierarchy, and design rule compliance. Focusing on typical defects in LLM-generated UML class diagrams, this study reveals that LLMs exhibit uncertainties analogous to human memory. Accordingly, three targeted repair strategies are proposed, including memory reinforcement, external knowledge injection, and detection-guided automated targeted repair. Experimental results obtained from the case studies indicate that (1) compared to expert-crafted class diagrams, LLM-generated UML class diagrams exhibit several issues, such as incomplete identification of key classes, confusion or omissions in relationships, insufficient or absent inheritance relationships, unused classes, and circular dependencies, and (2) after applying the repair methods, all the LLMs show varying degrees of improvement in addressing these issues. The average repair rate for key class identification reaches 85%, the coupling relationship repair rate is 46%, the inheritance relationship repair rate is 69%, while repair rates for unused classes and circular dependencies both reach 100%.

</details>


### [238] [Legacy System Modernization with Coding Agents: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28972)
*Iago da Silva Rodrigues Alves,Cristiano Politowski,João Eduardo Montandon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过工业案例评估AI编码代理在遗留系统现代化中的表现：使用Claude Code将企业ERP中12个Visual Basic 6功能迁移至C# .NET 10，平均功能等价性为70%，但结果和成本受功能复杂度显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留系统依赖已停产平台，持续支撑关键业务流程，但完全依靠人工进行现代化改造通常成本高、效率低且容易出错，因此有必要评估AI编码代理在迁移任务中的实际价值与适用边界。

Method: 在真实企业ERP环境中，选取12个不同复杂度的功能，采用单次生成策略，由一个版本的Claude Code代理完成从Visual Basic 6到C# .NET 10的迁移。迁移后，以原功能为基准测量功能等价性，并记录代理耗时和令牌消耗，进一步比较不同复杂度下的效果与成本。

Result: 迁移功能平均等价性为70%。低复杂度功能表现最佳，等价性达到92%，消耗约147万令牌、成本1.66美元；高复杂度功能等价性降至47%，消耗约909万令牌、成本10.28美元。结果显示，功能复杂度与迁移质量、资源消耗之间存在明显不对称性。

Conclusion: AI编码代理能够有效辅助部分遗留系统迁移，尤其适合低复杂度、边界清晰的功能；对于高复杂度功能，其等价性明显下降且成本显著增加。该策略应结合人工审查、分阶段验证和复杂度评估使用，论文同时指出了单次生成策略及AI代理在可靠性、成本控制和复杂功能处理方面的局限。

Abstract: Legacy systems built on discontinued platforms are a recurring technological liability in organizations that depend on these applications to sustain critical business processes. Although modernization is strategically necessary, it is costly and error-prone when performed exclusively through manual effort. In this paper, we report on a case study conducted in a real industrial setting, where we evaluate both the effectiveness and efficiency of AI coding agents in supporting the migration of legacy systems to modern platforms. By using one version of Claude Code agent, we migrated 12 features of distinct complexity levels from a corporate ERP system written in Visual Basic 6 to C# .NET 10 under a single-generation strategy. Once the migration sessions were completed, we measured the equivalence of the migrated features against the original ones, and collected data about the time spent and the number of tokens consumed by the agent during the process. In our experiment, the agent achieved an average equivalence of 70%, with a strong asymmetry across complexity levels; low-level features achieved 92%, and high-complexity ones scored 47%. Similar asymmetry was observed in the cost-based metrics; low-level features consumed 1.47M tokens ($1.66), whereas high-level ones used 9.09M ($10.28). We reveal the practical scenarios and circumstances where this migration strategy is more effective, as well as discuss the limitations and challenges of using AI agents for legacy system modernization.

</details>


### [239] [AgentLogs: A Dataset for Opening the Black Box of GitHub's Cloud Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29204)
*Jonan Richards,Kosei Horikawa,Youmei Fan,Yutaro Kashiwa,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentLogs：一个面向GitHub生成式AI软件工程代理的大规模过程数据集，不仅记录代理最终产出，还逐步记录其执行任务时的提示、推理、工具调用和令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集主要覆盖代理生成的拉取请求等结果，难以揭示代理如何探索仓库、修改代码、执行命令和与GitHub交互。缺乏过程级数据限制了对代理行为、效率、成本、失败模式及人机协作的系统研究。

Method: 作者扫描1,812,362个热门公共代码仓库，从中识别出35,810个包含代理活动的仓库，构建AgentLogs数据集。数据集汇集307,416个代理任务、549,239个代理会话，以及64,255,174条会话日志条目；日志覆盖提示词、中间推理、文件编辑与Git操作等工具调用、GitHub交互和令牌使用情况。

Result: AgentLogs在规模和粒度上提供了大范围的代理执行轨迹，使研究者能够同时分析任务结果与任务执行过程。其数据可用于量化代理的行为模式、执行效率与成本，比较任务表述方式，识别失败环节，并研究开发者与代理之间的协作机制。

Conclusion: 该工作将生成式AI软件工程代理研究从仅关注代码贡献结果，扩展到可审视完整执行过程的实证研究。AgentLogs为评估和改进代理式软件工程系统提供了重要的数据基础，但摘要未说明数据质量控制、日志中推理内容的可用性边界、隐私处理策略及采样偏差等潜在局限。

Abstract: Generative AI-based software engineering agents are becoming routine contributors to real-world software projects. On GitHub, developers can assign tasks to the Copilot cloud agent, which autonomously explores the repository, edits code, runs commands, and opens or reviews pull requests, producing a detailed log of every step along the way. While existing datasets capture outcomes of agent contributions, such as agent-authored pull requests, the process by which agents produce these contributions remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce AgentLogs, a large-scale dataset of agent activity on GitHub. AgentLogs comprises 307,416 agent tasks and 549,239 agent sessions in 35,810 of the 1,812,362 popular public repositories that we scanned, together with 64,255,174 session log entries that record each agent run step by step, including prompts, intermediate reasoning, tool calls (e.g., file edits, git operations, and GitHub interactions), and token usage. By exposing not only what agents contribute but also how they work, AgentLogs enables research on agent behavior, efficiency and cost, task formulation, failure modes, and human-agent collaboration in agentic software engineering.

</details>


### [240] [Super Library Agent: Joint Generation and Maintenance of Multiple Applications Beyond the Single Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29310)
*Daegyu Sung,Yukyeong Lee,Geon Park,Yumin Choi,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“超级库代理（Super Library Agent）”问题：让LLM编码代理在顺序生成多个相关应用时，持续维护一个可复用的共享组件库，以降低跨项目重复、代码冗余与长期维护导致的结构退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关应用通常共享领域逻辑、界面模式和运维约定。若LLM代理逐个独立生成和维护应用，会重复实现相同功能，并在长期迭代中积累冗长代码、死代码和结构侵蚀。直接从现有应用中抽取共享代码并迁移到公共库，常因抽取召回率低和依赖迁移脆弱而失败。

Method: 方法包含三项关键机制：（1）基于代码块摘要的候选引导抽取，用于提高可复用代码识别与抽取的覆盖率；（2）抽取前代码库整合，先统一或整理应用内相关实现，降低碎片化对抽取的影响；（3）利用抽取轨迹与调用图的上下文感知迁移，辅助应用安全地替换为对共享库组件的依赖。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench和PaperBench上的实验显示，该方法能够保持应用功能，同时相较零样本生成显著减少冗余和令牌占用，尤其降低代码冗长度与文本长度；相较朴素共享库构建方法，可避免其引入的结构侵蚀，并进一步减少代码行数（LOC）和最小描述长度（MDL）。

Conclusion: 论文表明，面向应用组合的LLM代码生成不应采用彼此隔离的逐应用流程。通过候选引导抽取、代码库整合及基于调用上下文的迁移，代理可以逐步构建和维护共享组件库，在不损害功能的前提下提升跨应用复用性、压缩代码规模，并改善长期结构质量。

Abstract: Organizations often develop and maintain portfolios of related applications: independently deployable codebases that share substantial domain logic, interface patterns, or operational conventions. As LLM coding agents are increasingly used to generate and maintain such software, a naive application-by-application workflow duplicates shared logic across codebases and allows prolonged agentic maintenance to accumulate verbosity, dead code, and structural erosion. We introduce the Super Library Agent problem, where an agent sequentially generates a portfolio of N related applications while maintaining a shared Super Library of reusable cross-application components. A minimal sequential scaffold can in principle extract shared code and migrate applications to the evolving library, but in practice suffers from low extraction recall and fragile dependency migration. We address these failures with candidate-guided extraction over code chunk summaries, pre-extraction codebase consolidation, and context-aware migration using extraction traces and call-graph information. Across WebGen-Bench and PaperBench, our method preserves application functionality while significantly reducing redundancy and token footprint (verbosity, token length) over zero-shot, and avoiding the structural erosion introduced by naive library construction, with additional reductions in LOC and MDL. Our code is available at https://github.com/sbigstar0310/super-library-agent.

</details>


### [241] [Drive the Thoughts: Runtime Monitoring of VLA Reasoning-Trajectory Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29583)
*Tian Yu,Lu Feng,Sebastian Elbaum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究能否利用视觉-语言-动作模型生成的思维链，对自动驾驶轨迹进行运行时一致性监测。作者构建DriveAlignBench并提出多种监测器，最佳方法取得0.75的F1分数，表明思维链可作为辅助安全验证信号，但其自身可靠性仍是关键限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知与规划模型具有黑箱特征，传统验证与确认难以直接解释其决策依据。视觉-语言-动作模型除输出轨迹外还能生成自然语言推理过程，为将高层驾驶意图与低层运动控制相互校验提供了机会；若两者不一致，可能提示模型出现错误或非预期行为。

Method: 作者从NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型中整理150组“思维链—轨迹”样本，构建DriveAlignBench，并人工标注思维链可靠性、思维链与轨迹的一致性及安全相关属性。针对自然语言中开放词汇、相对场景化的驾驶承诺与依赖道路几何和运动上下文的低层轨迹之间的语义鸿沟，作者设计了一组自动一致性监测器，包括基于原始航点的大语言模型基线、规则方法，以及利用车道相对表示的F-LLM方法。

Result: 人工分析显示，33.3%的思维链不可靠；在可靠思维链样本中，74%的生成轨迹与其表述一致。性能上，采用GPT-5.5的车道相对F-LLM监测器达到F1=0.75，较最强原始航点大语言模型基线提升0.13个绝对F1点，较规则监测器提升0.38个绝对F1点。作者同时公开了数据集、监测器实现与标注工具。

Conclusion: 思维链—轨迹一致性检查具备作为自动驾驶运行时监测机制的可行性，尤其是将轨迹转换为车道相对语义表示后，可显著提升自动判定效果。不过，思维链存在较高比例的不可靠样本，因此该信号更适合作为辅助监测与告警依据，而非单独作为安全保证；后续工作应提高推理文本的真实性、扩展数据规模，并在更多道路结构和驾驶场景中验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) operate in complex environments where failures are consequential. Sophisticated machine learning models for perception and planning are key to overcoming at least part of that complexity, but their black-box nature complicates validation and verification (V&V). The recent integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into AVs introduces a unique opportunity: besides generating trajectories, these models produce an explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) explaining their underlying rationale. This CoT provides a rich specification to cross-check model outputs and detect inconsistencies that may expose unsafe or unintended behavior. This paper assesses whether CoTs from a recent open driving VLA can support such monitoring. We curate DriveAlignBench, a specialized dataset from NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 VLA for AVs containing 150 CoT-trajectory pairs, which we manually annotate for reliability, trajectory consistency, and safety. Our analysis reveals that 33.3% of CoTs are unreliable. Among reliable CoTs, the generated trajectory is consistent with the CoT in 74% of cases. Leveraging this potential, we propose integrating a CoT-trajectory consistency check into a runtime monitor. The check is nontrivial: CoTs express open-vocabulary, scene-relative driving commitments, while trajectories are low-level ego-motion sequences whose semantics depend on road geometry and motion context. To bridge this gap, we develop a family of automated consistency monitors. Our best monitor, lane-relative F-LLM with GPT-5.5, achieves F1 = 0.75, improving over the strongest raw-waypoint LLM baseline by +0.13 absolute F1 and over a rule-based monitor by +0.38. We release DriveAlignBench, the monitor implementations, and annotation tools at https://github.com/776styjsu/drive-the-thoughts.

</details>


### [242] [InteractBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Competitive Programming under Unrevealed Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29632)
*Jiaze Li,Aocheng Shen,Bing Liu,Boyu Zhang,Xiaoxuan Fan,Qiankun Zhang,Xianjun Deng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出InteractBench，用于评估大语言模型在需要动态获取信息、遵守交互协议并受查询预算约束的交互式编程问题上的算法推理能力。该基准包含322道来自多个竞赛平台的高质量题目，并支持离线执行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程基准主要考察输入一次性完整给出的任务，难以衡量程序在信息不完整、需多轮查询和持续维护状态时的推理能力。交互式问题能够补充这一评测维度。

Method: 从Codeforces、AtCoder、IOI和ICPC整理322道交互式问题，为每题配备可执行的本地交互器，以实现离线评测。评测同时关注最终解题成功率，并建立细粒度失败分类体系，分析算法逻辑错误、协议违规和查询预算超限等问题。

Result: 实验显示，即使是当前较先进的推理模型，在交互式问题上的成功率仍然有限，存在明显的交互能力差距。算法逻辑错误仍是主要失败原因，协议违规和查询预算超限也较为常见。

Conclusion: InteractBench填补了交互式算法推理评测的空白，可用于系统衡量模型的信息获取、状态跟踪、协议遵循和查询规划能力，并为定位模型生成程序的具体缺陷提供诊断工具。

Abstract: Competitive programming is increasingly being used to evaluate the algorithmic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on full-information tasks where all problem inputs are provided upfront. This overlooks a critical dimension of algorithmic reasoning: the ability of generated programs to operate when key information is not revealed upfront. Interactive problems, a distinctive component of competitive programming, embody this challenge. These problems require programs to engage in multi-round interaction with an interactor (a judge program) under strict protocol constraints and limited query budgets, with new information revealed only in response to queries. To address this gap, we introduce InteractBench, a benchmark comprising 322 high-quality interactive problems curated from Codeforces, AtCoder, IOI, and ICPC. Each problem is packaged with executable local interactors, enabling fully offline evaluation. Unlike existing benchmarks, InteractBench assesses whether model-generated code can acquire information and track state dynamically. Our evaluation reveals a significant interaction gap: even the most advanced reasoning models achieve limited success on interactive problems. Beyond success rates, we propose a fine-grained failure taxonomy to diagnose the root causes of these deficiencies. Although algorithmic logic errors remain dominant, protocol violations and query-budget overruns are frequent. Code is available at https://github.com/kmsgk0/InteractBench.

</details>


### [243] [Cost-Effective Repository Exploration for Agentic Issue Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29675)
*Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究将代码智能体中的“仓库探索/文件定位”环节交由低成本模型执行是否可行。基于999个任务的IssueLoc-Bench实验表明，低成本探索模型虽在定位质量上通常不及最高质量模型，但能以显著更低的时间与令牌成本保留较大比例的候选文件发现能力；模型选择应取决于下游如何使用文件定位结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化软件修复中，生成补丁之前需要从大型代码仓库中定位相关文件。该过程成本高，且常被视为代码生成模型的一部分而缺少独立评估。论文旨在验证：能否把这一前置阶段模块化地交给更便宜的模型，从而降低整体代理成本，同时维持足以支持后续修复的定位质量。

Method: 作者使用IssueLoc-Bench，在统一的只读交互接口下评估5个探索模型。数据包括499个源自SWE-bench Verified的任务，以及来自153个额外仓库的500个任务。评估指标涵盖：早期候选文件发现、前3个候选中金标准文件的覆盖率（Hit@3）、严格文件集合恢复、文件集合F1、智能体耗时与令牌用量。统计上采用成对实例级不确定性估计，并以仓库聚类方式进行敏感性分析，以检验结论的稳健性。

Result: 最高质量的探索模型在各项定位指标上领先；但低成本模型呈现具有吸引力的成本—质量折中。相对参考模型，低成本方案在不同模型和评估子集上可保留约78%至94%的Hit@3，以及73%至92%的F1；同时平均智能体时间降低41%至88%，令牌用量降低84%至95%。结果还显示，若下游可接收排序后的多个候选文件，Hit@3与覆盖率更具解释力；若下游要求严格限制可操作文件集，则F1和精确匹配更关键。

Conclusion: 仓库探索应被视为模块化代码智能体中可独立测量、优化和预算控制的阶段。低成本模型适合承担探索任务的前提是下游流程能够容忍候选集合中的噪声或遗漏；具体选择不应仅依据单一定位分数，而应与下游交接契约相匹配：候选重排序式交接侧重覆盖和排名，严格文件门控式交接侧重集合精度与完整性。

Abstract: Repository exploration is a distinct and costly stage of coding-agent pipelines: before generating a patch, an agent must identify which repository files are likely to matter. We study whether this stage can be delegated to lower-cost models while retaining useful localization quality. Using our IssueLoc-Bench, we evaluate five explorer models under the same read-only interactive interface on 499 SWE-bench Verified-derived tasks and 500 tasks from 153 additional repositories. We measure early candidate discovery, top-three gold-file coverage, strict file-set recovery, agent time, and token usage, with paired instance-level uncertainty and repository-clustered sensitivity analysis. The highest-quality explorer leads across localization metrics, but substantially cheaper operating points emerge: depending on the model and evaluation arm, lower-cost explorers retain approximately 78-94% of the reference Hit@3 and 73-92% of its F1 while reducing mean agent time by 41-88% and token usage by 84-95%. The preferred operating point depends on how localization is consumed downstream: ranking and coverage metrics characterize recoverable candidate handoffs, whereas F1 and exact match characterize restrictive file gates. These results support treating repository exploration as an independently measurable and budgetable stage of modular coding agents, with explorer selection guided by the downstream handoff contract.

</details>


### [244] [Agent-Driven Verification of Memory Safety for liblzma Decoder Components with VST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29716)
*Prokhor Shlyakhtun,Alexander Gryzlov,Vladimir Kukharenko,Vasilii Nesterov,Nikolai Vasiliev,Kirill Ziborov,Eugene Zolotarev,Alex Pokras*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文报告了对 liblzma 解码器关键组件的机器检查内存安全验证。工作基于 VST 与 Rocq，对既有生产级 C 代码而非新生成代码进行验证，并结合智能体生成证明脚本、人工负责模型与规范审查的协作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩库解码路径包含状态机、位流解析和滑动窗口等易发生越界、空指针及未定义行为的问题。验证成熟的生产级 C 实现可提升关键基础软件的可靠性，同时检验形式化方法在大规模遗留代码上的工程可行性。

Method: 作者使用 Verified Software Toolchain 为 LZMA2 状态机、其控制的 LZMA1 解码器、外层解码路径及共享滑动窗口字典建立契约和模型，并完成 27 个函数体证明，以证明内存安全和部分功能正确性。智能体承担证明目标求解及细化建议；人工编写、评审模型与规格，并批准语义修改；最终证明项由 Rocq 内核检查。工程上重点解决生产 C 的翻译与建模、Rocq 驱动框架，以及向证明智能体提供有效反馈。

Result: 全部 27 个函数体证明已完成。其中最大的 lzma_decode 函数原始代码为 338 行，预处理后扩展为 1,934 行 C；相应证明脚本达 183,268 行，机械提取的目标陈述达 775,768 行。验证还发现 LZMA1 原始零输入处理存在未定义行为：范围解码宏会对空指针执行加零运算，并对两个空指针执行减法。作者称 VST 的断言逻辑足以表达本项目所需的全部契约。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助 VST、Rocq 和人机协作证明流程，可对现实生产环境中的复杂 C 解码器开展大规模机器检查验证。其核心贡献不仅是获得内存安全与部分功能正确性保证、定位未定义行为，也总结了遗留代码建模、证明自动化编排和智能体反馈机制等可复用的证明工程经验。

Abstract: We report on the verification of memory safety for decoder components of liblzma, the compression library underlying xz-utils: the LZMA2 state machine, the LZMA1 decoder it controls, the outer decoding path, and the shared sliding-window dictionary. Built with the Verified Software Toolchain (VST), machine-checked body theorems establish memory safety and partial functional correctness. Across 27 completed body proofs, the largest covers lzma decode, whose 338 source lines expand to 1,934 lines of C after preprocessing; its proof comprises 183,268 lines of proof script over 775,768 lines of mechanically extracted goal statements. The verification exposed undefined behavior in raw LZMA1 zero-input handling, where range-decoder macros add zero to a null pointer and subtract two null pointers. Unlike similar work that synthesizes verified code, we verify pre-existing, production-scale C. AI agents complete proof goals and propose refinements; humans write and review models and specifications, and approve semantic changes; the Rocq kernel checks the proof terms. With agents constructing the proof scripts, the main engineering problems lay in translating and modeling production C, building a robust harness for driving Rocq, and providing feedback for proving agents. VST's assertion logic expressed every contract required by the development. We describe the pipeline, coordination mechanisms, and proof-engineering techniques that resolved these frictions.

</details>


### [245] [A Comprehensive Study of Native Code Bugs in Python Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29851)
*Haoran Yang,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文对Python应用中由C等低级语言原生组件引入的缺陷开展系统性实证研究，基于216个真实项目缺陷归纳其症状、引入位置、表现特征、根因与修复方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python应用广泛采用原生代码以获得性能和运行环境互操作性，但这也带来隐蔽且难以定位的原生代码缺陷。现有研究尚未对这类缺陷形成全面、系统的认识，因此需要揭示其发生机制并总结有效修复策略。

Method: 研究者从GitHub上的真实Python项目收集216个原生代码缺陷，结合自动化分析与人工审查，对缺陷的外部症状、代码引入位置、触发与表现规律、根本原因及补丁修复方案进行分类和分析。

Result: 研究获得了关于Python应用原生代码缺陷的经验性发现，揭示了此类缺陷的常见现象、成因模式、出现与显现机制，以及相应的解决策略。摘要未给出具体分类比例、统计显著性或各类缺陷的详细数量。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了Python应用原生代码缺陷系统研究的知识空白，可为开发者定位和修复跨语言缺陷、为测试与分析工具设计针对性检测能力提供依据。

Abstract: The impact of Python applications has been evidenced by their widespread presence in some of the most impactful software domains, such as machine learning frameworks and scientific computing platforms. These applications often integrate native code components written in a lower-level programming language like C. This multilingual construction brings various benefits such as greater performance efficiency and easier interoperability with diverse runtime environments. However, bugs in the native code (i.e., native code bugs), which are usually stealthy, also constitute a major additional challenge to the quality of the Python applications as a whole. Yet despite existing relevant studies, there remains a lack of comprehensive understanding of native code bugs in Python applications. In this paper, we aim to mitigate this knowledge gap through the first in-depth study of such bugs, dissecting their common symptoms, introducing locations, manifestation characteristics, root causes, and fixes. Based on our extensive automated and manual analyses of 216 native code bugs in real-world Python projects on GitHub, we obtained novel findings about and new insights into the occurrence mechanisms and resolution strategies of those bugs.

</details>


### [246] [Open-Source Autonomous Driving System Analysis and Multi-Disciplinary Hardware-in-the-Loop Research Paradigm with Reinforcement-Learning Testing and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30179)
*Dianjing Cheng,Yike Li,Lan Yang,Shan Fang,Wenjia Niu,Xiangyu Shi,Xinyi Zhao,Yunzhe Tian,XingYu Wu,Xiaoshu Cui,Yuanwan Chen,Jialu Sun,Zhongli Wang,Biao Liu,Jiaqi Yang,Jinghui Feng,Feifei Su,Juan Du,Shuangde Fang,Yi Qian,Huiyun Li,Yuansheng Liu,Peng Sun,Mingming Wan,Nan Chen,Ruipeng Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向Apollo-on-Hongqi EV实车环境的实验框架，将多车协同实验、代码仓库复用及软硬件测试反馈纳入统一的可审查记录流程，并以大语言模型和基于强化学习的测试作为辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源自动驾驶系统虽具备可检查的软件基础，但在多车辆、多团队、多阶段的实车实验中，任务执行、代码变更、硬件反馈和运行条件通常分散，导致实验过程难以连续追溯、解释系统行为及复用实验结果。

Method: 构建实车实验记录与审查框架：以代码仓库关联代码版本和复用关系；以统一流程连接多车实验任务、运行工况、软硬件测试反馈及实验产物；使用大语言模型辅助整理记录和归纳异常；使用基于强化学习的测试在仿真环境中生成候选测试场景；并基于多车协作、代码与实验技能共享、软硬件协同测试进行初步分析。

Result: 初步证据表明，该框架能够将实验记录与对应运行条件共同审查，支持多车协作实验中的过程关联、代码和实验技能共享，以及软硬件协同测试反馈的追溯。摘要未提供量化指标、对照实验、具体数据规模或异常检测准确率等结果。

Conclusion: 该研究的主要贡献是提出可审查的实车实验组织机制，为Apollo-on-Hongqi EV相关研究提供实验可追溯性和结果复用基础。其有效性目前仍属于初步验证，后续需通过更大规模部署、明确评价指标及与现有实验管理方案的对比进一步证明。

Abstract: Open-source autonomous driving systems provide an inspectable software foundation for intelligent vehicle research. Under real-vehicle deployment conditions, the recording and review of experimental conditions are important for interpreting system behavior and reusing experimental results. However, in a shared real-vehicle environment involving multiple vehicles, task processes, code modifications, and hardware testing feedback are often distributed across different teams and experimental stages, making it challenging to maintain continuous and reviewable experimental records. To address this limitation, this paper examines an Apollo-on-Hongqi EV environment and proposes a real-vehicle experimental framework. The framework connects multi-vehicle experiments, repository-based code reuse and software-hardware testing feedback within a unified review process. Large language models and RL-based testing serve as auxiliary components for record organization, anomaly summarization, and simulation-based candidate scenario generation. Based on this setting, this paper analyzes preliminary evidence from multi-vehicle collaborative experimentation, code and experimental-skill sharing, and software-hardware collaborative testing. The analysis shows that experimental records can be examined together with their operating conditions, providing a reviewable basis for Apollo-on-Hongqi EV research.

</details>


### [247] [DSEffi-Bench: Demystifying Large Language Models' Capability in Efficient Data Science Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30248)
*Zhihao Gong,Junzhe Yu,Dong Huang,Zeyu Sun,Jie M. Zhang,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出DSEffi-Bench，用于评测大语言模型生成数据科学代码时的执行效率，而非仅检验功能正确性。基准含1,000个覆盖10余个数据科学库的任务，并配备压力测试与人工验证参考实现。结果表明，正确率不能充分代表效率；分类诊断与按库路由可提升效率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据科学代码生成基准通常将“能否通过测试”视为质量标准，但不同正确实现的运行时间可相差多个数量级。因此，模型评测需要显式衡量执行效率，并定位效率不足的具体原因，以支持更实用的代码生成与优化。

Method: 作者构建DSEffi-Bench，收集1,000个跨10余个数据科学库的实例，为每个实例提供压力测试框架和人工验证的参考答案；评测16个不同层级模型，联合报告通过率Pass与条件效率指标B|P。进一步建立人工标注的五类效率缺陷分类体系，并开展两项探索实验：基于分类结果的定向优化，以及依据目标库进行模型路由。

Result: GPT-5.4的正确率最高，Pass为66.9%，但其效率得分B|P为71.7%，与少完成47个任务的GPT-5.4-mini的71.6%几乎相同。Kimi-K2.5在前沿模型中正确率最低，为40.2%，但以73.6%的B|P取得全部16个模型中的最高效率。人工分析显示，79.1%的效率问题并非仅由算法复杂度导致，还涉及数据科学库的特定使用模式等领域因素。分类引导优化最高带来14.7%的效率提升；按库路由在效率上接近Claude-Opus-4.6的Best@3，同时成本降低至其约1/13。

Conclusion: DSEffi-Bench证明，数据科学代码生成的功能正确性与执行效率应被分开评估。模型的效率表现不必与正确率同步，且大量性能缺陷源于领域与库特定问题。通过细粒度诊断、针对性优化和库条件模型路由，可在较低成本下改善生成代码的实用效率。

Abstract: Current data science (DS) code generation benchmarks equate correctness with quality, overlooking execution time differences that span orders of magnitude between correct solutions. We introduce DSEffi-Bench, the first benchmark specifically targeting execution efficiency in LLM-generated DS code, comprising 1,000 instances across 10+ DS libraries with stress-testing harnesses and human-validated references. Evaluating 16 models across 3 tiers, we find that correctness alone fails to characterize efficiency: GPT-5.4 leads in correctness (Pass, 66.9\%) but its efficiency score (B$|$P, 71.7\%) nearly matches GPT-5.4-mini (71.6\%), which solves 47 fewer tasks; Kimi-K2.5 ranks lowest in correctness among frontier models (40.2\%) yet achieves the highest efficiency score (73.6\%) across all 16 models. A human-annotated five-category taxonomy reveals that 79.1\% of efficiency deficits extend beyond algorithmic complexity to domain-specific root causes, with distinct failure profiles across model tiers and libraries. Two exploratory experiments provide initial evidence that these diagnostics can guide improvement, yielding up to +14.7\% efficiency gains via taxonomy-guided optimization and approaching Claude-Opus-4.6 Best@3 in efficiency at 13.0$\times$ lower cost via library-conditioned routing.

</details>


### [248] [Update from Hell: Can Coding Agents Survive Hidden Breakage in Dependency Upgrades?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30300)
*Zijian Luo,Runzhi He,Pengfei Gao,Yu Kang,Zeqi Lin,Minghua Ma,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Yongqiang Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DEPBENCH基准，用于评测编程智能体处理真实第三方依赖升级后隐式源码适配问题的能力。该基准覆盖5个软件包生态、5种语言社区中的203个真实任务；最佳配置仅完成104个任务（51.2%），表明现有智能体距离可靠解决真实软件维护需求仍有明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方依赖升级常改变函数签名、类型约束、API或运行时语义，导致既有代码需要同步修改。此类源码级破坏性变化往往未被充分或显式地传达给维护者，因而升级过程成本高、易引入可靠性风险。随着编程智能体被用于自动化开发，亟需一个贴近真实维护场景的评测基准来衡量其依赖升级适配能力。

Method: 作者构建DEPBENCH，收集203个来自真实项目的依赖升级任务。每项任务均包含由依赖变更引发、且需要修改客户端源码才能解决的隐藏代码级变化。基准横跨5个包管理生态系统和5种语言社区，并使用该基准评估主流编程智能体，比较不同智能体运行框架、基础模型及生态系统下的任务完成表现。

Result: 最优评测配置完成104/203个任务，成功率为51.2%。不同智能体运行框架、模型以及包生态之间存在显著性能波动。这说明任务难度不仅来自代码修复本身，也受到工具编排方式、模型能力及生态系统特征的共同影响。

Conclusion: DEPBENCH揭示了当前编程智能体在依赖升级维护中的能力边界：即使最佳配置也只能解决约半数真实任务。依赖升级后的源码适配仍是智能体软件工程能力中的关键短板；未来工作应增强智能体对API演化、类型与语义变化、跨文件修改及生态系统工具链的理解与验证能力。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely heavily on third-party dependencies, but upgrading those dependencies remains a costly maintenance activity. Dependency upgrades do not always preserve the function signatures, type systems, APIs, or runtime semantics assumed by existing code. Consequently, developers often need to perform source code adaptations to accommodate dependency-induced changes. However, such code-level changes are often not explicitly communicated to project maintainers, posing a significant challenge to software reliability.
  Meanwhile, coding agents have emerged as a new form of software development tool and are increasingly adopted by developers due to their automation capabilities. In this paper, we introduce DEPBENCH, a benchmark consisting of 203 real-world dependency-upgrade tasks across five package ecosystems spanning five language communities, each involving hidden code-level changes that require source code adaptation. We evaluate mainstream coding agents on DEPBENCH. The best completed configuration solves only 104/203 tasks (51.2%), with substantial variation across agent harnesses, models, and ecosystems, highlighting an important gap between current agent capabilities and real-world software maintenance needs.

</details>


### [249] [Bridge: Automatically Mining Ecosystem-Scale API Update Mappings and Client Update Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30497)
*Kai Gao,Yu Sun,Chang-ai Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Bridge，一个面向生态系统规模的自动化框架，用于构建连接API更新映射、适用版本迁移及客户端实际更新实例的库演化数据集，并揭示长尾API替换对当前大语言模型推荐能力构成显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库版本升级会导致客户端需要修改API调用，因此自动化更新工具需要高质量数据支撑训练与评估。现有数据集通常只覆盖API映射、版本迁移或客户端修改中的部分信息，且涉及的第三方库数量有限，难以反映真实软件生态中的大规模演化模式。

Method: Bridge首先从客户端项目的依赖版本更新提交中大规模挖掘候选更新实例；随后利用库端证据验证候选实例；最后从经验证的客户端更新实例中归纳API更新映射。该流程确保每条保留的映射至少有一个真实客户端迁移实例作为依据，并同时关联映射、版本转换和具体调用修改。研究者还基于人工标注真值集评估挖掘质量，并使用所得数据集测试四个大语言模型的替换API推荐能力。

Result: 在人工标注数据上，Bridge对Java的精确率为91.6%、召回率为88.7%；对Python的精确率为90.1%、召回率为64.0%。在WoC V3上，Bridge挖掘出381661个Java及277259个Python客户端更新实例，对应Java的18900条和Python的4456条API更新映射，分别覆盖2557个和999个库。映射呈明显长尾分布：多数映射仅在少量客户端更新中出现。四个模型在替换API推荐任务上的最佳准确率仅为Java 37.1%、Python 44.4%，且对高频映射明显优于低频长尾映射。

Conclusion: Bridge能够以客户端真实更新为基础，构建跨大量库、同时包含版本迁移和调用级修改证据的API演化数据集。实验说明该框架具有较高的挖掘可靠性，同时也表明现有大语言模型尚难有效处理低频、长尾的API替换关系；未来自动化库升级研究应更重视长尾映射的数据覆盖、表示学习与推荐机制。

Abstract: Library updates often require adapting client code to API changes. API update mappings that identify relations between legacy and replacement APIs, version transitions that these mappings apply, and client update instances that capture concrete API call changes are essential for developing and evaluating automated library update techniques. Existing library evolution datasets capture only subsets of this information and typically cover few third-party libraries. In this paper, we present Bridge, a client-driven framework for automatically constructing ecosystem-scale library update datasets that connect API update mappings, version transitions, and client update instances. Bridge first mines candidate update instances from client dependency update commits at scale, validates them using library-side evidence, and then derives API update mappings from validated instances. This design grounds each retained mapping in at least one client update instance. On a manually annotated ground truth dataset, Bridge achieves 91.6% precision and 88.7% recall for Java and 90.1% precision and 64.0% recall for Python. Applied to WoC V3, Bridge mines 381,661 Java and 277,259 Python client update instances, representing 18,900 and 4,456 API update mappings across 2,557 and 999 libraries, respectively. The mined mappings exhibit a pronounced long-tail distribution, with most appearing in only a few client update instances. As one application of the dataset, we evaluate four large language models on replacement API recommendation, a key step in library updates. The best recommendation accuracy reaches only 37.1% for Java and 44.4% for Python, and all evaluated models perform substantially better on frequently observed mappings than on mappings observed in only a few client update instances, highlighting the difficulty current LLMs face in recommending replacements for mappings in the long tail.

</details>


### [250] [Developer Attitudes and Practices Towards Optimizing Software Energy Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30527)
*Max Weber,Alina Mailach,Florian Sattler,Sven Apel,Norbert Siegmund*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究调查了134名专业开发者对软件能耗的认知、决策与实践，发现能耗通常未被直接纳入开发重点，开发者更多通过优化执行时间和CPU利用率间接影响能耗，但这种代理思维存在系统性盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件会显著影响硬件资源利用效率，软件能耗在日常开发中很少被视为一等重要目标。因此，研究旨在理解开发者如何识别、评估并处理软件能耗问题，以及阻碍能耗感知型开发实践普及的因素。

Method: 通过在线问卷调查134名软件开发者，结合定量分析与开放式问题的定性开放卡片分类，分析开发者对能耗的认知、实际做法及其推理模式；同时使用情境题考察开发者对性能与能耗关系的判断。

Result: 只有少数项目会明确考虑能耗。多数开发者通过优化执行时间、CPU利用率等代理指标间接影响能耗，但情境题显示其对性能提升可能增加能耗或与能耗无关的情况存在系统性误判。主要障碍包括组织层面的激励不足、测量与分析工具有限，以及相关教育缺口。

Conclusion: 软件能耗需要被制度化地纳入开发流程。研究建议通过具有示范作用的应用展示实际价值，加强能耗—性能权衡方面的研究与教育，并开发面向开发者、易于使用的测量和反馈工具，以降低采用能耗感知型开发方法的门槛。

Abstract: Context: Software significantly influences the efficiency with which hardware resources are utilized, yet software energy consumption is seldom treated as a first-class concern in day-to-day development practice. Objective: This study investigates professional developers' attitudes, decision-making, and development practices related to software energy consumption, with particular emphasis on how energy considerations are recognized, assessed, and acted upon during software development. Method: To this end, we conduct an online survey with 134 software developers. Our study combines quantitative analyses with a qualitative open-card sorting of free-text responses to characterize perceptions, practices, and reasoning patterns around energy consumption. Findings: Energy consumption is explicitly considered in only a minority of projects. More commonly, developers influence energy use indirectly by optimizing proxy properties such as execution time and CPU utilization. Responses to scenario-based questions reveal systematic blind spots in this mental model, including cases in which performance improvements increase energy consumption or exhibit no correlation. We also identify organizational disincentives, limited tooling, and educational gaps as major barriers to adoption. Implications: (1) Institutionalize energy-aware approaches through visible flagship deployments that demonstrate value, (2) expand research and education on energy-performance trade-offs, and (3) develop practical, developer-oriented measurement and feedback tools that lower adoption barriers.

</details>


### [251] [Practical Implementation Report on Introducing Spec-Driven Development Using AI Agents in Software Development PBL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30572)
*Hidetake Tanaka,Hiroshi Igaki,Kazumasa Shimari,Kiyoshi Honda,Naoki Fukuyasu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在软件开发项目制学习课程中实践规范驱动开发（SDD），发现AI代理可提高实现吞吐量，但可能削弱学生对代码的理解，因此需要教师持续检查与反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI代理在软件开发中的普及，有必要评估其在本科软件开发项目制学习中的实际教学效果及潜在风险。

Method: 研究面向三年级本科生，构建包含调查、规划、实现和评审四个阶段的SDD工作流，并配置AI代理在各阶段生成文档和代码。研究从学生主观AI使用情况、实现吞吐量和代码理解程度三个维度进行分析。

Result: 不同开发阶段和团队的AI使用模式存在差异。AI代理的使用提高了实现吞吐量，但也容易使学生在未充分理解代码的情况下继续开发。

Conclusion: 在项目制学习中引入SDD能够提升开发效率，但教育者必须定期验证学生的代码理解程度，并提供针对性反馈，以维持教学有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, autonomous AI agents such as GitHub Copilot and Claude Code have been rapidly gaining popularity. This study reports on the practical implementation of Spec-Driven Development, a software development methodology premised on AI agents, within a Software Development Project-Based Learning (SDPBL) course for third-year undergraduate students. We defined a workflow consisting of four phases, namely investigation, planning, implementation, and review. We also established an environment tailored for the SDPBL course where AI agents generate documentation and code during each phase. We analyzed the results from three perspectives, namely students' subjective AI usage, implementation throughput, and code comprehension. The analysis reveals that AI usage patterns varied across development phases and teams. Moreover, while AI agent utilization increased implementation throughput, it also tended to encourage students to proceed with development without fully understanding the code. This study demonstrates that regular verification of code comprehension by instructors and appropriate feedback are essential for maintaining educational effectiveness when introducing SDD into SDPBL.

</details>


### [252] [A Phased Workflow for Operating LLM-Based Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30701)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Tomislav Duricic,Andro Mercep,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型编码代理的四阶段协作工作流，通过前置人工投入、逐步增加代理委托比例，以及持续的上下文管理，提高复杂软件开发任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂编码任务中，代理的可靠性不仅取决于基础模型和工具框架，也取决于人类如何组织研究、规划、实现与验证过程。研究发现，上游研究和规划错误会在后续阶段累积，而事后修正代理生成的代码可能导致代码膨胀与脆弱性，因此需要将人工审查前置。

Method: 基于Infobip人工智能研究团队的实践经验，将代理辅助开发划分为四个阶段，并在各阶段采用四类上下文管理策略，以缓解已知失效模式。工作流遵循人类前期投入较多、随着软件制品逐渐成熟而增加代理委托的原则。

Result: 实践观察表明，研究与规划阶段的错误会跨阶段传播并放大；对生成代码进行后期修补则可能引入额外复杂度、代码膨胀和系统脆弱性。上下文管理是维持代理行为稳定性和开发可靠性的核心。

Conclusion: 可靠使用编码代理需要以阶段化工作流和前置人工审查为基础，而非仅依赖模型能力。未来需要建立衡量工作流有效性的指标，并弥合形式化上下文管理组件与实践者所需工作流模式之间的差距。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents combine a foundation model with a harness that shapes agent behavior. For non-trivial tasks, how practitioners structure their work with the coding agents determines whether reliable results follow. We report on a phased workflow for operating coding agents developed by the AI research team at Infobip. The workflow structures agent-assisted development into four phases where human effort is front-loaded and delegation increases as artifacts mature. Context management is the central concern, addressed through four strategies applied at each phase to counter known failure modes. From practitioner experience, we observe that upstream errors in research and planning can compound across later phases, while correcting generated code can introduce bloat and fragility. This motivates front-loading human review. We identify two open problems: the absence of metrics for workflow effectiveness and the gap between formalized context management components and the workflow-level patterns that practitioners need.

</details>


### [253] [sbom-unifier: Integration Framework for Heterogeneous SBOMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30708)
*Yusuke Moriwaki,Tetsuya Kanda,Yuki Manabe,Shi Qiu,Shiyu Yang,Erina Makihara,Norihiro Yoshida,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出sbom-unifier，通过整合多个SBOM工具的输出并进行文件级补全，提升SPDX字段完整性与跨字段引用一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM生成工具输出异构，许多SPDX字段缺失或仅部分填充；简单拼接不同SBOM也无法识别同一软件组件，导致信息冗余和完整性不足。

Method: 以Package URL（PURL）识别和匹配同一组件，采用确定性的优先级策略补全缺失字段，重建跨章节引用，并进一步丰富文件级字段。框架在多个工具输出之间执行字段级集成与互补。

Result: 在涵盖9种编程语言的90个开源项目上，sbom-unifier保持了较高的必需字段完整性。在SPDX 2.3的39个必需及可选字段中，相比表现最佳的单一工具，字段完全覆盖率提高8个百分点，字段完全缺失率降低11个百分点。

Conclusion: sbom-unifier能够有效改善多来源SBOM的字段覆盖率、组件关联和引用一致性，优于单独使用工具或简单拼接SBOM的方法，具有实际的SBOM质量提升价值。

Abstract: A Software Bill of Materials (SBOM) is a machine-readable inventory of software components, increasingly required for vulnerability management and license compliance. However, existing SBOM generation tools often leave many SPDX-defined fields missing or only partially populated, because different tools produce heterogeneous outputs with uneven field-level coverage. We present sbom-unifier, a framework that improves SBOM completeness through field-level integration and complementation of multiple tool outputs and file-level enrichment. Unlike existing tools that simply concatenate SBOMs without identifying records referring to the same component, sbom-unifier identifies components via Package URL (PURL), complements missing field values by a deterministic priority-based strategy, reconstructs cross-section references, and further enriches file-level fields. Across 90 open-source projects in 9 programming languages, sbom-unifier preserves high completeness for required fields and, over the 39 SPDX 2.3 required and optional fields, raises the fully covered rate by 8 percentage points and reduces the totally missing rate by 11 percentage points over the respective best-performing individual tools. sbom-unifier is available at https://github.com/MoriwakiYusuke/sbom-unifier.

</details>


### [254] [On the Prospects of Dynamic LLM Conversations in Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30756)
*Annemarie Wittig,Alina Mailach,Janet Siegmund,Norbert Siegmund*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过为期四个月的纵向实验，比较三类开发者—聊天式大语言模型交互条件：上下文感知增强、主动建议与无干预对照。结果未发现干预对既有交互模式或测量结果造成明显负面影响；主动建议组的满意度呈提升趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天式大语言模型对开发任务的帮助高度依赖开发者如何组织提示词及提供何种上下文。现有研究对如何在开发过程中有效支持此类交互、以及交互干预是否会带来收益或损害，缺乏实证认识。

Method: 研究招募三年级计算机科学学生，在全栈Web开发项目中持续使用聊天式大语言模型，并随机或分组置于三种条件：上下文感知组获得基于用户意图的对话增强；主动组获得后续提示和个性化建议；对照组不接受干预。干预设计保持最小化，以降低混杂因素并便于识别处理效应。研究结合交互日志与用户问卷进行分析。

Result: 三组在交互模式上未表现出显著差异，说明在所测指标范围内，最小化交互干预没有产生可检测的负面影响。与此同时，主动干预组在用户满意度方面出现上升趋势，但摘要未报告其统计显著性、具体效应量或对开发产出质量的明确改善。

Conclusion: 最小程度的动态交互引导已能产生可观察的用户体验效应，尤其主动建议可能提升开发者满意度。由于效果主要体现为趋势且交互行为未发生显著变化，后续应通过更大样本、明确的统计检验及对代码质量、任务效率和长期依赖性的综合测量，验证更强干预是否能够带来实质性收益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become an essential tool for assisting developers, yet we still lack knowledge on ways to effectively support their interactions during development activities. That is, the quality of interactions with a chat-based LLM still strongly depends on how developers phrase prompts and which information they include.
  Our goal is to evaluate whether interventions into these interactions with LLMs have an effect on software developers---be it harmful or beneficial. To this end, we conducted a four-month longitudinal study with third-semester computer science students working on a full-stack Web development project using chat-based LLMs under three conditions: (1) a \emph{context}-aware group received intent-based conversation augmentation, (2) a \emph{proactive} group received follow-up suggestions and tailored advice, and (3) a \emph{control} group without intervention. Our augmentations are minimal: (i) to reduce confounding factors and (ii) to isolate treatment effects.
  Analyzing interaction logs and user surveys revealed no major differences in interaction patterns, indicating no detectable harmful effects in the measured outcomes when intervening in interactions. Moreover, we observed trends of increased satisfaction with the \emph{proactive} treatment. The results indicate that even with minimal interventions, dynamic guidance mechanisms for developer-LLM interactions show observable effects, such that more severe augmentations may have the potential to substantially improve developer satisfaction.

</details>


### [255] [ProofPulse: Interactive Proof Coverage Analysis for Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30818)
*Álvaro F. Silva,Ruben Martins,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 ProofPulse：面向 Dafny 的规格质量诊断工具。它通过三值证明覆盖模型和证明依赖分析，识别真正支撑规格意图的元素、仅用于辅助检查的元素，以及与证明义务无关的元素，从而发现已验证程序中的弱规格、冗余不变式、不必要前置条件和空洞证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演绎验证只能证明实现满足给定规格，不能证明规格本身充分、准确或非冗余。因此，即使程序验证成功，过弱的规格或无效的不变式仍可能造成对代码正确性的过度信任。论文旨在提供一种可操作的信号，用于评估证明与规格之间的耦合强度。

Method: ProofPulse 对 Dafny 程序的证明依赖进行分析，并将程序元素按三值证明覆盖进行分类：直接贡献于规格意图、仅支持辅助性检查、或不参与任何证明义务。工具进一步采用不可满足核心最小化，以消除冗余依赖并降低误报。该方法在 dafny-synthesis 基准的 252 个带标注程序上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，ProofPulse 能以高精度识别规格弱点，尤其擅长检测不必要的前置条件与空洞证明。引入不可满足核心最小化后，工具在前置条件分类上达到完全精确，并在所有评测类别中降低了误报率。

Conclusion: 证明覆盖分析可作为传统验证的有效补充：它不替代对语义意图的人工判断，也无法完整刻画规格是否符合真实需求，但能够揭示表面验证成功背后的弱证明耦合、冗余约束和规格不足问题。

Abstract: Deductive verification ensures that an implementation satisfies its specification, but successful verification does not guarantee the quality of the specification. As such, weak specifications and redundant invariants may create overconfidence in ``verified'' code.
  We present \proofpulse, a tool for Dafny that diagnoses specification quality using a three-valued proof coverage model. By analyzing proof dependencies, ProofPulse distinguishes between elements that contribute to specification intent, those used only for auxiliary checks, and those irrelevant to any proof obligation.
  Evaluated against an oracle of 252 programs from the dafny-synthesis benchmark, ProofPulse provides a high-precision signal for specification weaknesses, particularly unnecessary preconditions and vacuous proofs. With unsat-core minimization, ProofPulse achieves perfect precision for precondition classification and reduces false positives across all evaluated categories. These results show that proof coverage is a practical complement to verification. Although it cannot fully capture semantic intent, it can reveal weak proof coupling in programs that otherwise appear fully verified.
  Just as a pulse check distinguishes vitality from the mere absence of symptoms, ProofPulse exposes weaknesses in proofs that technically verify successfully but still suffer from inadequate or redundant code and specifications.

</details>


### [256] [LLM Post-Training as Brownfield Maintenance: An Industrial Perspective on Dataware Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31102)
*Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Amir M. Ebrahimi,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从工业代码生成模型的后训练维护出发，提出“数据件（dataware）”视角：在固定算力与数据配比预算下，通过受限的数据混合补丁实现定向能力提升，同时控制既有能力回归。案例显示，优化教师蒸馏数据的可用转化率可显著提升监督数据接受量，并带来代码基准上的稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业后训练通常面对已部署的既有检查点，无法进行完全重训；团队必须在有限计算、固定混合比例和多项质量约束下改善特定能力。因此，关键问题不只是找到有效训练配方，而是如何持续、可靠地维护和更新决定模型行为的数据混合物。

Method: 论文归纳三项核心工程挑战：零和的数据混合设计、以有效监督产出率为约束指标，以及不确定条件下的端到端集成。案例采用相同的解题教师模型，并对每个候选题目固定生成四次解答；通过提高教师蒸馏结果转化为可接受训练数据的比例，构造面向代码能力提升的数据混合补丁。效果通过固定初始检查点下的多次随机评测验证，并同步检查内部回归集是否处于容忍范围。

Result: 经产出率优化后，在教师模型和每题四次生成尝试均不变的条件下，可接受监督数据量提高2.84倍。主评测中，CodeForces的pass@1提升2.59个百分点、pass@3提升3.11个百分点；LiveCodeBench v6的pass@1提升6.11个百分点、pass@3提升8.05个百分点。每项基准在每个条件下进行了16次随机评测，报告提升均具有统计显著性；内部AIME和MATH回归套件保持在预设容忍范围内。

Conclusion: 论文认为，工业后训练的核心应从一次性的训练技巧转向“编程数据件”的工程纪律：系统地管理数据混合补丁、最大化有限预算下的有效监督产出，并通过严格评测与回归控制实现局部能力提升而不损害整体性能。该案例表明，提升蒸馏数据的有效产出率可以在不增加教师推理预算的情况下带来可验证的代码能力改进。

Abstract: Industrial post-training is a brownfield regime. Teams inherit a deployed checkpoint and must land targeted improvements under fixed compute and mixture budgets without regressing the rest. The maintained artifact is increasingly dataware: behavior governed by a curated post-training mixture, updated via bounded mixture patches rather than clean-slate retraining. From an industrial code-generation improvement effort, we offer a maintainer's perspective on why this work is hard in practice, distilling three recurring challenges, zero-sum mixture design, yield as the binding metric, and end-to-end integration under uncertainty, and arguing that progress depends less on one-off recipes than on an engineering discipline for programming dataware. In our case study, interventions that raised the conversion of teacher distillation into usable training data increased accepted supervision by 2.84 times while using the same solution teacher and four solution attempts per candidate problem. In our primary evaluation, the yield-engineered patch improved CodeForces pass@1 by +2.59 points (+3.11 pass@3) and held-out LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1 by +6.11 (+8.05 pass@3), all statistically significant across 16 stochastic evaluations of each benchmark from one fixed checkpoint per condition, with internal AIME and MATH regression suites within tolerance.

</details>


### [257] [Auditing Anonymous AI Models: A Four-Stage Protocol for Black-Box Identity Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31142)
*Yisen Xi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向匿名API模型的四阶段黑箱取证审计协议，通过平台快照、配置指纹、分词器差异测试和行为探针，评估模型身份声明的一致性与潜在身份线索。实验显示方法在已知身份发布中具有较高一致性，并在一个前瞻性案例中正确指向GLM-5.3版本线，但尚未完成大规模匿名模型端到端识别验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匿名或代号发布的前沿模型会带来数据处理条款、供应链风险和能力预期的不确定性。现有实践主要依赖缺乏准确性证据的检查清单，且模型自我声明不可靠，因此需要一种可复核、分阶段、适用于黑箱API的身份验证方法。

Method: 协议包含四个阶段：Stage 0利用Internet Archive等历史平台快照重建发布时配置，识别预览版与生产版漂移；Stage 1根据上下文长度、输出上限、推理能力和模态等特征与平台目录进行配置指纹匹配；Stage 2通过跨长度差分测试验证分词器身份，降低短提示碰撞；Stage 3使用行为探针对候选身份进行交叉验证。研究还提供了仅依赖标准库的实现，并采用分级假设或拒绝猜测机制。

Result: 在10个已知身份的发布版本上，声明一致性结果为7个完全一致、2个存在精度差异、1个部分一致，未出现反向矛盾案例。一个前瞻性旗舰案例的分析指向GLM-5.3版本线，后续官方披露确认了其系列和版本线判断，但部署变体并未事先断言；另有3个仅完成Stage 0的案例输出了分级假设或选择不下结论。

Conclusion: 该协议为匿名API模型审计提供了结构化、可复核且强调不确定性控制的初步框架。现有结果支持其进行声明一致性检查和有限的身份线索推断，但不能证明其已具备普适的匿名模型端到端识别能力；后续仍需扩大样本、开展盲测并量化各阶段的误报率、漏报率和识别置信度。

Abstract: The 2025--2026 AI market has seen a wave of stealth releases: frontier models launched anonymously on developer platforms under codenames. For their users, identity determines data-handling terms, supply-chain risk, and capability expectations. No validated methodology exists for black-box identity verification of anonymous models: practitioner checklists lack accuracy evidence, and self-identification is untrustworthy by design. We propose a four-stage forensic audit protocol for API-served models. Stage 0 reconstructs launch-time configuration from archived platform snapshots (Internet Archive), exposing preview--production drift. Stage 1 fingerprints configuration (context, output ceiling, reasoning, modality) against the platform catalog. Stage 2 tests tokenizer identity with a cross-length differential that rejects short-prompt collisions. Stage 3 corroborates with behavioral probes. We test declaration consistency on 10 known-identity releases (7 exact, 2 precision-differences, 1 partial, 0 counter-directional), not end-to-end identification under anonymity. Identification is validated prospectively on a flagship case whose 2026-08-23 analysis pointed to the GLM-5.3 version line and whose official reveal confirmed those family and version-line inferences (deployment variant was not pre-asserted; Flash was consistent post-reveal), and on three Stage-0-only cases where the protocol produced a graded hypothesis or declined rather than guessed. A standard-library-only implementation is provided as supplementary material.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [A quantum let within the lambda calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29380)
*Alejandro Díaz-Caro,Tomás Miguez*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文为量子λ演算 λ_ρ^∘ 引入张量消去机制，使嵌入项内的多量子比特密度矩阵能够被分解、逐比特访问及按语义正确地丢弃；并建立了相应的类型、重写与指称语义理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子λ演算通常将量子态置于程序外部，以避免直接处理张量积；而 λ_ρ^∘ 虽将密度矩阵嵌入项中，便于验证整个计算状态，却缺少张量消去构造，因而不能访问复合态的单个量子比特，也不能丢弃量子比特，表达能力受限。

Method: 作者利用泡利分解及泡利矩阵的谱分解，证明任意 n 量子比特密度矩阵均可表示为单量子比特密度矩阵张量积的实线性组合。在此基础上扩展演算，加入“let x^{⊗n}=ρ in t”构造，将分解所得的各单量子比特密度矩阵绑定给变量；随后定义重写系统、类型系统和指称语义，并对其元理论性质进行证明。

Result: 扩展后的系统满足主题归约、进展性、强规范化、语义健全性和充分性。新构造允许从复合量子态中获得可组合的单量子比特成分，并恢复丢弃量子比特的能力。对项中未使用变量的解释恰好对应对相应子系统施加偏迹，从而与不可删除原理一致。论文还通过量子态传送和三量子比特比特翻转纠错码展示了组合性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在将量子态直接置于程序项内的密度矩阵量子λ演算中，张量消去不仅可实现，而且可在物理上合理、语义上严格的框架下实现。由此补足 λ_ρ^∘ 的关键表达能力缺口，并为面向验证的、状态内嵌式量子程序语言提供了更完整的理论基础。

Abstract: Since the seminal work of Selinger and Valiron, the standard design for quantum lambda calculi has kept the quantum state outside the program: terms manipulate pointers to an external register. This is largely due to the difficulty of eliminating tensor products. For example, the calculus $λ_ρ^\circ$ embeds density matrices directly within terms, where terms carry the entire computation state, a feature particularly appealing for program verification. However, lacking a tensor elimination construct, it can neither access the individual qubits of a compound state nor discard them. Borgna showed that this inability to discard qubits makes the calculus strictly less expressive than the quantum lambda calculus of Selinger and Valiron.
  In this paper we show that tensor elimination is possible in this setting. The key observation is that the Pauli decomposition, combined with the spectral decomposition of the Pauli matrices, allows any $n$-qubit density matrix to be expressed as a real linear combination of tensor products of single-qubit density matrices. Exploiting this fact, we extend $λ_ρ^\circ$ with a construct $\mathrm{let}\ x^{\otimes n} = ρ \mathrm{in}\ t$, which binds each $x_i$ to a single-qubit density matrix arising from the decomposition of $ρ$.
  We equip the extended calculus with a rewrite system, a type system, and a denotational semantics, and prove Subject Reduction, Progress, Strong Normalisation, Soundness, and Adequacy. The new construct also recovers the missing ability to discard qubits, thereby restoring expressiveness. Moreover, we show that this is achieved in a physically principled way: a variable unused in $t$ is interpreted exactly as being partial-traced out, as dictated by the no-deleting theorem. We illustrate the resulting compositionality through quantum teleportation and the three-qubit bit-flip code.

</details>


### [259] [Unary Functions, Automorphisms, and Unlabeled First-Order Model Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30580)
*Ondřej Kuželka*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文刻画了带一元函数符号的一阶逻辑模型计数复杂度边界：单变量、单一元函数的带计数量词逻辑可多项式时间计数；增加第二变量或第二一元函数即出现#P_1完全性。同时，论文通过显式标记自同构，将无标号模型计数精确归约为同规模的有标号模型计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套函数项如f(x)、f²(x)会显著增强逻辑描述能力，而其对固定公式模型计数复杂度的影响尚需精确界定。论文旨在确定一元函数、变量数及计数量词之间的复杂度临界点，并建立有标号与无标号枚举之间的直接联系。

Method: 首先，对任意固定的C^1_=[f]句子，构造关于规模n的多项式时间计数算法。其次，通过固定公式编码证明两类临界扩展的#P_1困难性：FO^2_=[f]中的单一元函数双变量情形，以及FO^1_=[f,g]中的双一元函数单变量情形。最后，对任意关系词汇表上的句子φ，扩展为φ_aut，使新增一元函数编码模型的自同构；再在有界元数关系词汇表上消去该辅助函数，得到逻辑片段间的单次查询归约。

Result: 对每个固定的φ∈C^1_=[f]，FOMC(φ,n)可在n的多项式时间内计算。若允许第二变量，则存在固定的FO^2_=[f]句子，其模型计数函数为#P_1完全；若允许第二一元函数，则存在仅含一元谓词及f、g的无常元全称FO^1_=[f,g]句子，其计数函数同样为#P_1完全。对于任意关系句子φ，满足FOMC(φ_aut,n)=n!·UFOMC(φ,n)。当关系符号最大元数不超过k且k≥2时，无标号FO^k_=及C^k模型计数可分别单次查询归约到有标号FO^{k+1}_=及C^{k+1}模型计数。

Conclusion: 单变量加单一元函数构成固定公式模型计数的可处理区域，而额外增加一个变量或一个一元函数便足以导致计数复杂度跃升至#P_1完全。自同构标记提供了无标号计数到有标号计数的精确且规模保持的桥梁，并表明在有界关系元数条件下，仅增加一个变量即可模拟该转换。

Abstract: Every fixed first-order sentence $\varphi$ determines an enumerative sequence $n\mapsto\mathrm{FOMC}(\varphi,n)$, counting its models on the labeled domain $[n]$. We study the complexity of these sequences when logical specifications may use genuine unary function symbols and hence nested terms $x,f(x),f^2(x),\ldots$.
  We first prove that, for every fixed sentence $\varphi\in\mathrm{C}^1_{=}[f]$, with one unary function and an arbitrary finite relational vocabulary, $\mathrm{FOMC}(\varphi,n)$ is computable in time polynomial in $n$. By contrast, permitting either a second variable or a second unary function already yields hardness. Without counting quantifiers, there is a fixed sentence in $\mathrm{FO}^2_{=}[f]$ whose model-counting function is $\#\mathrm{P}_1$-complete. With one variable and two unary functions, there is a fixed constant-free universal sentence in $\mathrm{FO}^1_{=}[f,g]$, using only unary predicates besides $f$ and $g$, whose model-counting function is again $\#\mathrm{P}_1$-complete.
  We also relate labeled and unlabeled enumeration exactly. For every relational sentence $\varphi$, we construct an extension $\varphi_{\mathrm{aut}}$ in which a unary function records an automorphism and $\mathrm{FOMC}(\varphi_{\mathrm{aut}},n)=n!\cdot\mathrm{UFOMC}(\varphi,n)$, where $\mathrm{UFOMC}(\varphi,n)$ denotes the number of $n$-element models of $\varphi$ up to isomorphism. Thus automorphism marking gives a one-query exact reduction from unlabeled to labeled model counting at the same domain size. Over relational vocabularies of maximum arity at most $k$, where $k\geq2$, eliminating the auxiliary function yields single-query reductions from unlabeled $\mathrm{FO}^k_{=}$ and $\mathrm{C}^k$ model counting to labeled $\mathrm{FO}^{k+1}_{=}$ and $\mathrm{C}^{k+1}$ model counting, respectively.

</details>


### [260] [Computational free will as global selection: from sheaf-theoretic gluing to a conditional separation of P and NP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30797)
*Jerome Clech*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文将“计算自由意志”形式化为：局部约束决定可接受性，而一次全局延拓由选择机制确定。若该选择可在事后高效验证、却无法由事前输入在多项式时间内统一预测，则可构造一个属于FNP但不存在确定性多项式时间选择器的全搜索关系；因此，在其明确假设成立时，可推出P≠NP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是把关于选择、可行性与事后可验证性的概念转化为计算复杂性框架，并考察这种形式化的“自由选择”是否意味着搜索问题中验证与求解能力之间存在本质差异。

Method: 作者使用有限选择预层的全局截面定义可接受集合GLUE，并以SELECT指定在预先标识事件处实际实现的全局延拓。随后引入统一轨迹关系，用于在行为发生后高效认证该延拓，同时假定该延拓不能从行为发生前的输入被统一地在多项式时间内预测。在统一性、平衡性、历史完备性和唯一投影等条件下，证明该轨迹关系构成一个全FNP搜索关系，且不存在确定性多项式时间选择器。

Result: 在全部形式化前提满足的条件下，存在一个解可多项式时间验证、但不能由确定性多项式时间算法统一求解的全搜索关系。该结论意味着FNP中存在不具备多项式时间选择器的关系，并据此导出P与NP不相等。

Conclusion: 该工作给出的是条件性复杂度结论：只有当所定义的计算自由意志机制及其不可预见性、统一性等假设成立时，才可推出P≠NP。论文并未提供P≠NP的无条件证明；其主要贡献在于建立了从特定选择模型到复杂度类分离的逻辑归约。

Abstract: We formalise computational free will by separating locally constrained admissibility from the selection of one global continuation. Global sections of a finite choice presheaf form an admissible set, GLUE; SELECT singles out the continuation realised at a pre-identified occurrence. A uniform trace relation certifies that continuation efficiently after the act, although it is assumed not to be uniformly anticipable in polynomial time from the prior occurrence input. Under explicit uniformity, balance, historical-completeness, and unique-projection assumptions, this trace defines a total FNP search relation with no deterministic polynomial-time selector. Thus existence of computational free will in the stated sense implies a separation between polynomially verifiable and polynomially solvable search, and hence that P differs from NP. The result is conditional and gives no unconditional class separation.

</details>


### [261] [Schwarz: Solver-Aware Agentic Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30803)
*Jingyu Ke,Ling-I Wu,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出Schwarz，一种面向C与Rust/Verus的智能体式验证框架。其核心贡献是将SMT求解失败转化为可定位、可检查、可修复的局部证明任务，并在1,475个任务上报告了较高的自动验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由大语言模型驱动的程序验证流程在SMT证明失败时，通常只能得到笼统的验证器报错、超时或unknown结果，难以区分规格错误、辅助引理缺失、无关上下文干扰或理论编码不适配等原因。这种低诊断性反馈限制了智能体对验证失败进行有效修复的能力。

Method: Schwarz将失败验证义务细化为局部修复任务：首先通过程序点快照暴露特定边界处已知和待证事实；其次允许智能体构造局部引理以补足证明步骤；最后采用理论感知的求解器策略，为数值、量词、内存与浮点类义务引导更适合SMT求解的表达形式。系统实现覆盖C以及Rust/Verus，并通过消融实验和纯智能体基线比较评估各组件价值。

Result: 在475个近期智能体验证工具基准上，Schwarz解决了95.2%的任务；在1,000个SV-COMP 2026 ReachSafety任务上，平均程序规模为1,427行代码，解决率为91.5%，高于CPAchecker报告的60.1%。摘要还称消融实验和与纯智能体基线的比较表明，求解器感知的局部修复策略具有有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作将自动验证中的关键瓶颈从“生成看似合理的规格”推进到“生成可被SMT证明的义务”，通过提供义务级诊断、局部引理机制和理论感知重写策略，提升了智能体修复证明失败的能力。其结论的说服力依赖于基准划分、超时与资源预算、比较工具配置、成功判定标准及消融细节；这些信息需在全文中进一步核查。

Abstract: Agentic verification systems can often generate source-level specifications that look plausible, but plausibility is not enough: the verifier must still turn those specifications into SMT obligations that the solver can prove. When this step fails, current LLM-driven loops usually expose only a coarse verifier error, timeout, or unknown solver result. The model cannot tell whether the specification is wrong, a helper lemma is missing, the proof context contains irrelevant facts, or the obligation needs a different theory view. This paper presents Schwarz, an agentic verification harness that makes SMT-backed proof failure local, checkable, and repairable. Schwarz turns failed verification into obligation-local repair tasks: program-point snapshots expose checked facts at a boundary, local lemmas let the agent propose missing proof steps, and theory-aware solver policies guide the agent toward solver-friendly formulations for numeric, quantified, memory, and floating-point obligations. We implement Schwarz for C and Rust/Verus and evaluate it on 1,475 tasks. On 475 benchmarks from recent agentic verification tools, Schwarz solves 95.2% of the tasks. On 1,000 tasks from the SV-COMP 2026 ReachSafety track, averaging 1,427 LOC, Schwarz solves 91.5% of the tasks, compared with 60.1% for CPAchecker. Ablations and comparison with a pure-agent baseline show that solver-aware repair is effective and scalable.

</details>
